  let dropdownOpen = false;

  const catalogDetails = new Map();
  let canCreate = false, initialized = false, catalogRequest = null, lastRefresh = 0;
  let patientGeneration = 0, adding = false, migrating = false;
  function normalize(value) {
    return String(value || '').normalize('NFKC').replace(/\p{Cf}/gu, '').replace(/\s+/gu, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
  }
  function status(text) {
    const node = document.getElementById('cc_catalog_status');
    if (node) { node.textContent = text; node.hidden = !text; }
  }
  function editable() { const input=getTargetInput(); return Boolean(input && !input.disabled && !input.readOnly && window.EmrSharedEncounter?.chiefEditable() !== false); }
  function remember(item) {
    const name = item.nameKh || item.nameEn;
    const key = normalize(name);
    let names = catalogDetails.get(key);
    if (!names) { names=[]; catalogDetails.set(key,names); catalog.push(name); }
    if (!names.some(x=>x.id===item.id)) names.push(item);
    return catalog.find(x=>normalize(x)===key) || name;
  }
  function findLabel(text) {
    const key=normalize(text);
    return catalog.find(name=>normalize(name)===key || (catalogDetails.get(normalize(name))||[]).some(x=>[x.nameKh,x.nameEn,x.code].some(v=>v && normalize(v)===key))) || text.trim();
  }
  function selectUnique(text) {
    const key=normalize(text);
    if (key && !Array.from(selectedSymptoms).some(s=>normalize(findLabel(s))===normalize(findLabel(text)))) selectedSymptoms.add(text);
  }
  function isEnglish() {
    try {
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.getBorkeoLanguage === 'function') {
        return window.getBorkeoLanguage() === 'en';
      }
      if (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') {
        return currentLang === 'english';
      }
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.documentElement) {
        return document.documentElement.lang === 'en';
      }
    } catch {}
    return false;
  }
  function getLocalizedName(item) {
    const canonical = typeof findLabel === 'function' ? findLabel(item) : item;
    const details = (typeof catalogDetails !== 'undefined' && catalogDetails.get(normalize(canonical))) || [];
    if (isEnglish()) {
      const en = details.find(x => x.nameEn)?.nameEn;
      if (en) return en;
      return canonical;
    } else {
      const kh = details.find(x => x.nameKh)?.nameKh;
      if (kh) return kh;
      return canonical;
    }
  }
  async function securityToken() {
    if (window.emrSession?.csrfToken) return window.emrSession.csrfToken;
    const response=await fetch('/api/auth/me',{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store'});
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Please sign in again.');
    return (await response.json()).csrfToken;
  }
  async function persist(text) {
    const response=await fetch('/api/symptoms',{method:'POST',credentials:'same-origin',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-CSRF-Token':await securityToken()},body:JSON.stringify({text})});
    const result=await response.json();
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'Cannot save symptom.');
    remember(result.item); return result;
  }
  async function migrateLocalCatalog() {
    if (migrating || !canCreate) return;
    let old; try { old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)||'[]'); } catch { return; }
    if (!Array.isArray(old) || !old.length) return;
    migrating=true;
    try {
      // Catalogue labels only: do not import stored patient records or auto-add them to a visit.
      for (const text of [...new Set(old.filter(x=>typeof x==='string' && normalize(x)))]) await persist(text);
      localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    } catch { status('បញ្ជីចាស់មិនទាន់រក្សាទុកទាំងអស់ / Some old catalogue labels could not be saved. Retry after reconnecting.'); }
    finally { migrating=false; renderOptions(document.getElementById('cc_search_input')?.value || ''); }
  }
  async function loadCatalog(force=false) {
    if (catalogRequest) return catalogRequest;
    if (!force && Date.now()-lastRefresh<15000) return;
    catalogRequest=(async()=>{
      try {
        const response=await fetch('/api/symptoms',{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store'});
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Cannot load shared symptoms.');
        const result=await response.json();
        if (!Array.isArray(result.items)) throw new Error('Invalid symptom catalogue.');
        catalog=[]; catalogDetails.clear(); result.items.forEach(remember);
        canCreate=Boolean(result.canCreate); lastRefresh=Date.now(); status('');
        const button=document.getElementById('cc_add_btn');if(button)button.disabled=!canCreate||!editable();
        renderOptions(document.getElementById('cc_search_input')?.value || '');
        void migrateLocalCatalog();
      } catch { status('មិនអាចទាញយកបញ្ជីរោគសញ្ញា / Cannot load symptoms. Reopen to retry; saved patient symptoms remain intact.'); }
      finally { catalogRequest=null; }
    })();
    return catalogRequest;
  }
  async function saveCatalog(text) {
    if (!canCreate) return null;
    try { return await persist(text); }
    catch (error) { status('មិនទាន់រក្សាទុកក្នុងបញ្ជី / Catalogue save failed: '+error.message); return null; }
  }

  function getTargetInput() {
    return document.getElementById('pmrs_chief_complaint');
  }

  function syncToInput() {
    const input = getTargetInput();
    if (!input) return;
    const labels = Array.from(selectedSymptoms).map(s => typeof getLocalizedName === 'function' ? getLocalizedName(s) : s);
    const value = labels.join(', ');
    const items = document.getElementById('pmrs_chief_complaint_items');
    if (items) items.value = JSON.stringify(labels);
    if (input.value !== value) {
      input.value = value;
      input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
      input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
    }
    renderTags();
  }

  function syncFromInput() {
    const input = getTargetInput();
    if (!input) return;
    const val = (input.value || '').trim();
    if (!val) {
      selectedSymptoms.clear();
      renderTags();
      renderOptions();
      return;
    }
    let saved; try { saved=JSON.parse(document.getElementById('pmrs_chief_complaint_items')?.value || 'null'); } catch {}
    const parts = Array.isArray(saved) && saved.every(x=>typeof x==='string') && saved.join(', ')===val
      ? saved : val.split(/[,،、]+/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    selectedSymptoms.clear();
    parts.forEach(s => {
      selectUnique(s);
    });
    renderTags();
    renderOptions();
  }

  function renderTags() {
    const list = document.getElementById('chief_complaint_tags_list');
    const placeholder = document.getElementById('cc_placeholder_text');
    if (!list) return;
    list.innerHTML = '';
    selectedSymptoms.forEach(symptom => {
      const display = typeof getLocalizedName === 'function' ? getLocalizedName(symptom) : symptom;
      const tag = document.createElement('span');
      tag.className = 'cc-tag';
      tag.innerHTML = `<span>${escapeHtml(display)}</span><span class="cc-tag-remove" data-symptom="${escapeHtml(symptom)}" title="${isEnglish() ? 'Remove' : 'លុប'}">✕</span>`;
      list.appendChild(tag);
    });

    if (placeholder) {
      placeholder.style.display = selectedSymptoms.size > 0 ? 'none' : 'block';
      placeholder.textContent = isEnglish() ? 'Select or type symptoms...' : 'ជ្រើសរើស ឬវាយបញ្ចូលរោគសញ្ញា...';
    }
  }

  function renderOptions(filterText = '') {
    const list = document.getElementById('cc_options_list');
    if (!list) return;
    list.innerHTML = '';
    const query = normalize(filterText);

    const options=[...catalog];
    for(const text of selectedSymptoms)if(!options.some(x=>normalize(x)===normalize(text)))options.push(text);
    const filtered = options.filter(item => {
      if (!query) return true;
      const details=catalogDetails.get(normalize(item))||[];
      return normalize(item).includes(query) || details.some(x=>[x.nameKh,x.nameEn,x.code].some(v=>v && normalize(v).includes(query)));
    });

    if (isEnglish()) {
      filtered.sort((a, b) => getLocalizedName(a).localeCompare(getLocalizedName(b), 'en', { sensitivity: 'base' }));
    }

    if (filtered.length === 0) {
      const empty = document.createElement('div');
      empty.className = 'cc-no-results';
      empty.textContent = query
        ? (isEnglish() ? 'Not in list (press + or Enter to add)' : 'គ្មានក្នុងបញ្ជី (ចុច + ឬ Enter ដើម្បីបន្ថែម)')
        : (isEnglish() ? 'No data' : 'គ្មានទិន្នន័យ');
      list.appendChild(empty);
      return;
    }

    const selectedKeys = new Set();
    for (const text of selectedSymptoms) {
      selectedKeys.add(normalize(findLabel(text)));
    }

    const canEdit = editable();
    const fragment = typeof document.createDocumentFragment === 'function' ? document.createDocumentFragment() : null;
    const targetParent = fragment || list;

    filtered.forEach(item => {
      const canonical = findLabel(item);
      const key = normalize(canonical);
      const isSelected = selectedKeys.has(key);
      const row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = `cc-option-item${isSelected ? ' selected' : ''}`;
      row.dataset.symptom = item;
      row.dataset.key = key;

      const cb = document.createElement('input');
      cb.type = 'checkbox';
      cb.dataset.emrTransient = '1';
      cb.checked = isSelected;
      cb.disabled = !canEdit;

      const span = document.createElement('span');
      const details = catalogDetails.get(normalize(item)) || [];
      span.textContent = getLocalizedName(item);
      row.title = details.map(x => x.code || 'Custom symptom — no official code').join(', ');

      row.appendChild(cb);
      row.appendChild(span);
      targetParent.appendChild(row);
    });

    if (fragment) {
      list.appendChild(fragment);
    }

    if (!list._ccDelegated) {
      list._ccDelegated = true;
      list.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (!editable()) return;
        const row = e.target.closest('.cc-option-item');
        if (!row) return;
        const cb = row.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]');
        if (!cb || cb.disabled) return;
        const sym = row.dataset.symptom;
        if (!sym) return;
        if (e.target === cb) {
          toggleSymptom(sym, cb.checked, row);
        } else {
          cb.checked = !cb.checked;
          toggleSymptom(sym, cb.checked, row);
        }
      });
    }
  }

  function toggleSymptom(symptom, select, sourceRow) {
    if (!editable()) return;
    const name = findLabel(symptom);
    const key = normalize(name);
    if (select) {
      selectUnique(name);
    } else {
      for (const text of selectedSymptoms) {
        if (normalize(findLabel(text)) === key) selectedSymptoms.delete(text);
      }
    }
    syncToInput();

    // Fast in-place DOM update without re-rendering all options:
    const list = document.getElementById('cc_options_list');
    let targetRow = sourceRow;
    if (!targetRow && list && list.querySelectorAll) {
      const rows = list.querySelectorAll('.cc-option-item');
      for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
        if (rows[i].dataset && rows[i].dataset.key === key) {
          targetRow = rows[i];
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (targetRow) {
      if (targetRow.classList) {
        if (typeof targetRow.classList.toggle === 'function') {
          targetRow.classList.toggle('selected', select);
        } else if (select) {
          targetRow.classList.add('selected');
        } else {
          targetRow.classList.remove('selected');
        }
      }
      const cb = targetRow.querySelector ? targetRow.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]') : null;
      if (cb && cb.checked !== select) cb.checked = select;
    }
  }

  async function addCustomSymptom(text) {
    const clean = String(text || '').trim();
    if (!clean || adding || !editable()) return;
    if (!canCreate) { status('មិនអាចបន្ថែមរោគសញ្ញា / You cannot create symptoms in this role.'); return; }
    const generation=patientGeneration;
    adding=true; const button=document.getElementById('cc_add_btn');if(button)button.disabled=true;
    try {
      const result=await persist(clean);
      if (generation!==patientGeneration || !editable()) return;
      selectUnique(result.item.nameKh || result.item.nameEn);
      syncToInput();
      const input=document.getElementById('cc_search_input');if(input)input.value='';
      status(result.created?'បានបន្ថែមរោគសញ្ញាថ្មី / New symptom saved.':'រោគសញ្ញាមានរួចហើយ / Existing symptom selected; no duplicate added.');
      renderOptions('');
    } catch (error) { status('មិនអាចរក្សាទុករោគសញ្ញា / Cannot save symptom: '+error.message); }
    finally { adding=false;if(button)button.disabled=!canCreate||!editable(); }
  }

  function toggleDropdown(show) {
    const dropdown = document.getElementById('chief_complaint_dropdown');
    const tagBox = document.getElementById('chief_complaint_tag_box');
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('cc_search_input');
    if (!dropdown || !tagBox) return;
    dropdownOpen = show !== undefined ? show : !dropdownOpen;
    dropdown.style.display = dropdownOpen ? 'block' : 'none';
    tagBox.classList.toggle('is-open', dropdownOpen);
    if (dropdownOpen) {
      void loadCatalog(true);
      renderOptions(searchInput?.value || '');
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (searchInput) searchInput.focus();
      }, 50);
    }
  }

  function bindEmergencyCheckboxes() {
    const emergencyCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.s1-check, .s2-check');
    emergencyCheckboxes.forEach(cb => {
      if (cb._ccBound) return;
      cb._ccBound = true;
      cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const row = cb.closest('.symptom-row');
        const symptomText = row?.querySelector('.symptom-text')?.textContent?.trim();
        if (!symptomText) return;
        if (cb.checked) {
          saveCatalog(symptomText);
          selectUnique(findLabel(symptomText));
          syncToInput();
        } else {
          selectedSymptoms.delete(symptomText);
          syncToInput();
        }
        renderOptions(document.getElementById('cc_search_input')?.value || '');
      });
    });
  }

  function syncCheckedEmergencyBoxes() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.s1-check:checked, .s2-check:checked').forEach(cb => {
      const row = cb.closest('.symptom-row');
      const text = row?.querySelector('.symptom-text')?.textContent?.trim();
      if (text) {
        saveCatalog(text);
        selectUnique(findLabel(text));
      }
    });
    syncToInput();
  }

  function escapeHtml(str) {
    return String(str || '')
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
  }

  function init() {
    if (initialized) return;
    initialized=true;
    void loadCatalog();
    const tagBox = document.getElementById('chief_complaint_tag_box');
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('cc_search_input');
    const addBtn = document.getElementById('cc_add_btn');
    const targetInput = getTargetInput();

    if (!tagBox) return;

    tagBox.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const removeBtn = e.target.closest('.cc-tag-remove');
      if (removeBtn) {
        if (!editable()) return;
        e.stopPropagation();
        const sym = removeBtn.dataset.symptom;
        if (sym) {
          toggleSymptom(sym, false);
        }
        return;
      }
      toggleDropdown(true);
    });

    tagBox.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Tab' || e.key === 'Shift' || e.key === 'Alt' || e.key === 'Control') return;
      toggleDropdown(true);
      if (searchInput && e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (searchInput) searchInput.focus();
        }, 10);
      }
    });

    if (searchInput) {
      searchInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
        renderOptions(searchInput.value);
      });

      searchInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        if (e.isComposing) return;
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (searchInput.value.trim()) {
            addCustomSymptom(searchInput.value);
          }
        } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          toggleDropdown(false);
        }
      });
    }

    if (addBtn) {
      addBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        addCustomSymptom(searchInput?.value);
      });
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (!dropdownOpen) return;
      const wrapper = document.querySelector('.chief-complaint-wrapper');
      if (wrapper && !wrapper.contains(e.target)) {
        toggleDropdown(false);
      }
    });

    if (targetInput) {
      targetInput.addEventListener('input', syncFromInput);
      targetInput.addEventListener('change', syncFromInput);
    }

    bindEmergencyCheckboxes();
    syncFromInput();
    syncCheckedEmergencyBoxes();
    updateLanguage();

    window.addEventListener('borkeo-language-change', updateLanguage);
    if (typeof MutationObserver !== 'undefined' && document.documentElement) {
      const langObserver = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
        for (const m of mutations) {
          if (m.attributeName === 'lang') { updateLanguage(); break; }
        }
      });
      langObserver.observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['lang'] });
    }

    document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded', () => {
      patientGeneration++;
      status('');
      setTimeout(() => {
        syncFromInput();
        bindEmergencyCheckboxes();
        syncCheckedEmergencyBoxes();
        updateLanguage();
      }, 50);
    });
  }

  function updateLanguage() {
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('cc_search_input');
    if (searchInput) {
      searchInput.placeholder = isEnglish() ? 'Search Khmer / English / Code' : 'ស្វែងរកខ្មែរ / English / Code';
    }
    const placeholder = document.getElementById('cc_placeholder_text');
    if (placeholder) {
      placeholder.textContent = isEnglish() ? 'Select or type symptoms...' : 'ជ្រើសរើស ឬវាយបញ្ចូលរោគសញ្ញា...';
    }
    syncToInput();
    renderOptions(searchInput?.value || '');
  }

  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
  if (document.readyState === 'complete' || document.readyState === 'interactive') {
    setTimeout(init, 0);
  }

  window.BorkeoChiefComplaint = {
    addSymptom: addCustomSymptom,
    toggleSymptom,
    syncFromInput,
    syncToInput,
    getSelected: () => Array.from(selectedSymptoms),
    clear: () => { if (editable()) { selectedSymptoms.clear(); syncToInput(); } },
    updateLanguage,
    isEnglish,
    getLocalizedName
  };
})();
===== END FILE: public/chief-complaint-tags.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: public/clinical-summary.js =====
// v41.0 - Medical record editors for the long-term clinical summary:
// - structured allergy record in the IPD Subjective box (slot "structured-allergy");
// - active problem list in the OPD and IPD Assessment boxes (slot "problem-list").
// (Nursing form name / age / sex / code: setAutofillTarget in emr-inpatient-workflow.js.)
// Data and labels come from the nonvisual provider below.
// Every control added here carries data-emr-transient, so it is never saved
// with the record and never shifts the position keys of existing fields.
'use strict';
(function clinicalSummaryProvider() {
  const patient = () => window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.() || null;
  const key = () => patient()?.patientKey || '';
  const role = () => String(window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole || '').toUpperCase();
  const escape = value => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
  const khmer = {
    'clinical.remove': 'ដកចេញ', 'clinical.removeReason': 'មូលហេតុនៃការដកចេញ',
    'clinical.confirmRemove': 'បញ្ជាក់ការដកចេញ', 'clinical.removeReasonRequired': 'សូមបញ្ចូលមូលហេតុនៃការដកចេញ។',
    'clinical.unsaved': 'សូមរក្សាទុកកំណត់ត្រាអ្នកជំងឺជាមុនសិន។',
    'clinical.requestError': 'មិនអាចផ្ទៀងផ្ទាត់ព័ត៌មានបាន។ សូមព្យាយាមម្ដងទៀត។',
    'clinical.allergy.type': 'ប្រភេទ', 'clinical.allergy.none': 'មិនទាន់មានកំណត់ត្រាក្នុងបញ្ជីអាលែកហ្ស៊ីនេះទេ។',
    'clinical.allergy.add': 'បន្ថែមប្រតិកម្មអាលែកហ្ស៊ី', 'clinical.allergy.substanceRequired': 'សូមបញ្ចូលសារធាតុ។',
    'clinical.problem.name': 'បញ្ហាសុខភាព', 'clinical.problem.none': 'មិនមានបញ្ហាសុខភាពដែលបានកត់ត្រាថាកំពុងបន្តមានទេ។',
    'clinical.problem.resolved': 'បញ្ហាសុខភាពដែលបានដោះស្រាយ', 'clinical.problem.resolve': 'សម្គាល់ថាបានដោះស្រាយ',
    'clinical.problem.reactivate': 'កំណត់ជាសកម្មឡើងវិញ', 'clinical.problem.add': 'បន្ថែមបញ្ហាសុខភាព',
    'clinical.problem.required': 'សូមបញ្ចូលបញ្ហាសុខភាព។',
    'allergyType.drug': 'ថ្នាំ', 'allergyType.food': 'អាហារ', 'allergyType.other': 'ផ្សេងទៀត',
    'severity.mild': 'ស្រាល', 'severity.moderate': 'មធ្យម', 'severity.severe': 'ធ្ងន់ធ្ងរ',
    'severity.life-threatening': 'គំរាមកំហែងដល់ជីវិត', 'severity.unknown': 'មិនទាន់ដឹង',
  };
  const english = () => window.getBorkeoLanguage?.() === 'en' || window.currentLang === 'english';
  const plainLabel = (name, en, kh) => english() ? en : kh || khmer[name] || window.BORKEO_NEW_TEXT_KH?.[name] || 'មិនអាចបង្ហាញព័ត៌មានបាន';
  let revision = 0;
  let state = { patientKey: '', summary: null, status: 'idle', revision };
  let pending = null;
  const unavailable = () => ({ patientKey: key(), summary: null, status: 'idle', revision });
  const get = () => state.patientKey === key() ? state : unavailable();
  const notify = () => document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('emr:clinical-summary', { detail: get() }));
  const valid = data => Array.isArray(data?.allergies) && Array.isArray(data?.problems)
    && Array.isArray(data?.options?.allergyTypes) && Array.isArray(data?.options?.allergySeverities)
    && ['allergy', 'none', 'not-recorded'].includes(data?.allergyAlert?.state)
    && Array.isArray(data.allergyAlert.items)
    && !(data.allergies.length && data.allergyAlert.state !== 'allergy');
  const matches = (patientKey, token) => key() === patientKey && revision === token;
  const safeError = () => plainLabel('clinical.requestError', 'Information cannot be verified. Please try again.');

  function refresh() {
    const patientKey = key(), token = ++revision;
    state = { patientKey, summary: null, status: patientKey ? 'loading' : 'idle', revision: token };
    pending = null;
    notify();
    if (!patientKey) return Promise.resolve(get());
    const request = (async () => {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(`/api/clinical-summary?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}`, { credentials: 'same-origin', cache: 'no-store' });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok || !valid(data)) throw new Error('Unavailable summary');
        if (matches(patientKey, token)) state = { patientKey, summary: data, status: 'ready', revision: token };
      } catch {
        if (matches(patientKey, token)) state = { patientKey, summary: null, status: 'error', revision: token };
      }
      if (matches(patientKey, token)) { pending = null; notify(); }
      return get();
    })();
    pending = request;
    return request;
  }

  async function post(path, body, expected) {
    const current = get();
    if (role() !== 'DOCTOR' || current.status !== 'ready' || !key()
        || expected?.patientKey !== key() || expected?.revision !== revision
        || !/^(?:allergies(?:\/[0-9a-f-]{36}\/remove)?|problems(?:\/[0-9a-f-]{36}\/status)?)$/.test(path)) {
      return { ok: false, error: safeError() };
    }
    const patientKey = key(), token = revision;
    state = { ...current, status: 'saving' };
    notify();
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/clinical-summary/${path}`, {
        method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': window.emrSession?.csrfToken || '' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ...body, patientKey }),
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!matches(patientKey, token)) return { ok: false, stale: true, error: safeError() };
      if (!response.ok || !valid(data)) throw new Error('Unavailable summary');
      // A fresh revision also invalidates handlers retained from a previous render.
      state = { patientKey, summary: data, status: 'ready', revision: ++revision };
      pending = null;
      notify();
      return { ok: true };
    } catch {
      if (matches(patientKey, token)) {
        state = { patientKey, summary: null, status: 'error', revision: ++revision };
        notify();
      }
      return { ok: false, error: safeError() };
    }
  }

  window.EmrClinicalSummary = {
    patient, get, refresh, ready: () => pending || Promise.resolve(get()), post,
    canWrite: () => role() === 'DOCTOR' && get().status === 'ready',
    allergyState: () => get().status === 'ready' ? get().summary.allergyAlert : { state: 'unknown', items: [], freeText: '', ticked: false },
    plainLabel, label: (name, en, kh) => escape(plainLabel(name, en, kh)),
    plainOptionLabel: (group, code) => plainLabel(`${group}.${code}`, code),
    optionLabel: (group, code) => escape(plainLabel(`${group}.${code}`, code)),
  };
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded', refresh);
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-saved', refresh);
  document.addEventListener('emr:new-registration-ready', refresh);
  window.addEventListener('emr-session-ready', refresh);
  // Editors below must be installed before a synchronous loading notification.
  window.setTimeout(refresh, 0);
})();
(function () {
  const api = () => window.EmrClinicalSummary;
  const escapeHtml = (value) => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
  const label = (key, en, kh) => api()?.label(key, en, kh) ?? escapeHtml(en);
  const plain = (key, en, kh) => api()?.plainLabel(key, en, kh) ?? en;
  const optionLabel = (group, code) => api()?.optionLabel(group, code) ?? escapeHtml(code);
  const plainOption = (group, code) => api()?.plainOptionLabel(group, code) ?? code;
  const unavailableMessage = () => api()?.patient()?.patientKey
    ? label('clinical.requestError', 'Information cannot be verified. Please try again.')
    : label('clinical.unsaved', 'Please save the patient record first.');
  const today = () => {
    const now = new Date();
    return `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };
  // The add / remove forms follow the record: shown to doctors while the record can be edited.
  const canEdit = (slot) => Boolean(api()?.canWrite()) && !window.EmrEncounterEditor?.isReadOnly?.(slot);

  // Read-only encounter status from the existing authorized summary API.
  // No new orders, risk calculations or resuscitation editing are introduced.
  function recordedStatusHtml(summary) {
    const recorded = summary?.resuscitation?.status;
    const value = recorded === 'dnr' ? 'DNR'
      : recorded === 'full' ? label('', 'Full resuscitation', 'សង្គ្រោះជីវិតពេញលេញ')
        : !summary ? unavailableMessage() : label('', 'Not recorded', 'មិនបានបញ្ជាក់');
    const severe = (summary?.allergies || []).filter(entry => ['severe', 'life-threatening'].includes(entry.severity));
    return `<section data-clinical-recorded-status class="clinical-summary-card" aria-live="polite">
      <h4>${label('', 'Resuscitation status', 'ស្ថានភាពការសង្គ្រោះជីវិត')}</h4>
      <p data-clinical-resuscitation${recorded === 'dnr' ? ' class="clinical-recorded-warning" role="alert"' : ''}>${value}</p>
      ${recorded === 'dnr' || severe.length ? `<h4>${label('', 'Risk flags', 'សញ្ញាហានិភ័យ')}</h4>
        <ul data-clinical-recorded-risks class="clinical-recorded-warning">${recorded === 'dnr' ? '<li>DNR</li>' : ''}${severe.map(entry => `<li>${escapeHtml(entry.substance)} (${optionLabel('severity', entry.severity)})</li>`).join('')}</ul>` : ''}
    </section>`;
  }

  function current() {
    const state = api()?.get();
    const patient = api()?.patient();
    if (!state || !patient || state.patientKey !== patient.patientKey) return null;
    return state.summary;
  }

  function message(slot, text) {
    const box = slot.querySelector('[data-clinical-message]');
    if (box) box.textContent = text || '';
  }

  async function submit(slot, button, path, body) {
    if (!canEdit(slot) || !slot.contains(button)) return;
    const expected = { patientKey: slot.dataset.summaryPatientKey, revision: Number(slot.dataset.summaryRevision) };
    button.disabled = true;
    message(slot, '');
    const result = await api().post(path, body, expected);
    if (!result.ok && slot.dataset.summaryPatientKey === expected.patientKey && Number(slot.dataset.summaryRevision) === expected.revision) {
      button.disabled = false;
      message(slot, result.error);
    }
    // On success the summary event re-renders every slot.
  }

  // ---- Structured allergy record (IPD S box)
  function renderAllergies(slot, summary) {
    const editable = canEdit(slot);
    const allergies = summary?.allergies || [];
    const options = summary?.options || { allergyTypes: [], allergySeverities: [] };
    const rows = allergies.map((a) => `<tr>
        <td>${optionLabel('allergyType', a.allergyType)}</td>
        <td><strong>${escapeHtml(a.substance)}</strong></td>
        <td>${escapeHtml(a.reaction || '—')}</td>
        <td>${a.severity ? optionLabel('severity', a.severity) : '—'}</td>
        <td>${escapeHtml(a.notedOn || '—')}</td>
        <td>${escapeHtml(a.recordedBy || '—')}</td>
        ${editable ? `<td class="clinical-actions">
          <button type="button" class="clinical-button secondary" data-allergy-remove="${escapeHtml(a.id)}">${label('clinical.remove', 'Remove')}</button>
          <span class="clinical-remove-form" data-required-island data-allergy-remove-form="${escapeHtml(a.id)}" hidden>
            <input type="text" data-emr-transient maxlength="300" data-allergy-remove-reason aria-label="${escapeHtml(plain('clinical.removeReason', 'Reason for removing'))}" placeholder="${escapeHtml(plain('clinical.removeReason', 'Reason for removing'))}">
            <button type="button" class="clinical-button danger" data-allergy-remove-confirm="${escapeHtml(a.id)}">${label('clinical.confirmRemove', 'Confirm remove')}</button>
          </span>
        </td>` : ''}
      </tr>`).join('');
    slot.innerHTML = recordedStatusHtml(summary) + `
      <div class="clinical-summary-card clinical-allergy-card">
        <h4 class="clinical-summary-title">${label('', 'Structured allergy record', 'កំណត់ត្រាប្រតិកម្មអាលែកហ្ស៊ី')}</h4>
        ${!summary ? `<p class="clinical-empty">${unavailableMessage()}</p>` : rows ? `<div class="clinical-table-wrap"><table class="clinical-table">
          <thead><tr>
            <th>${label('clinical.allergy.type', 'Type')}</th><th>${label('', 'Substance', 'សារធាតុ')}</th><th>${label('', 'Reaction', 'ប្រតិកម្ម')}</th>
            <th>${label('', 'Severity', 'កម្រិតធ្ងន់ធ្ងរ')}</th><th>${label('', 'Date', 'កាលបរិច្ឆេទ')}</th><th>${label('', 'Recorded by', 'អ្នកកត់ត្រា')}</th>${editable ? '<th></th>' : ''}
        </tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div>` : `<p class="clinical-empty">${label('clinical.allergy.none', 'No entries have been recorded in this allergy list yet.')}</p>`}
        ${summary && editable ? `<div class="clinical-add-form" data-required-island data-allergy-add-form>
          <label><span>${label('clinical.allergy.type', 'Type')}</span>
            <select data-emr-transient data-allergy-field="allergyType">${options.allergyTypes.map((code) => `<option value="${escapeHtml(code)}">${escapeHtml(plainOption('allergyType', code))}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
          <label class="wide"><span>${label('', 'Substance', 'សារធាតុ')}</span>
            <input type="text" data-emr-transient data-allergy-field="substance" maxlength="200" required></label>
          <label class="wide"><span>${label('', 'Reaction', 'ប្រតិកម្ម')}</span>
            <input type="text" data-emr-transient data-allergy-field="reaction" maxlength="500"></label>
          <label><span>${label('', 'Severity', 'កម្រិតធ្ងន់ធ្ងរ')}</span>
            <select data-emr-transient data-allergy-field="severity"><option value=""></option>${options.allergySeverities.map((code) => `<option value="${escapeHtml(code)}">${escapeHtml(plainOption('severity', code))}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
          <label><span>${label('', 'Date', 'កាលបរិច្ឆេទ')}</span>
            <input type="date" data-emr-transient data-allergy-field="notedOn" value="${today()}" max="${today()}"></label>
          <button type="button" class="clinical-button" data-allergy-add>${label('clinical.allergy.add', 'Add allergy')}</button>
        </div>` : ''}
        <p class="clinical-message" data-clinical-message role="status"></p>
      </div>`;
    slot.querySelector('[data-allergy-add]')?.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
      const value = (name) => slot.querySelector(`[data-allergy-field="${name}"]`)?.value.trim() || '';
      if (!value('substance')) { message(slot, plain('clinical.allergy.substanceRequired', 'Enter the substance.')); slot.querySelector('[data-allergy-field="substance"]')?.focus(); return; }
      submit(slot, event.currentTarget, 'allergies', {
        allergyType: value('allergyType'), substance: value('substance'), reaction: value('reaction'), severity: value('severity'), notedOn: value('notedOn'),
      });
    });
    slot.querySelectorAll('[data-allergy-remove]').forEach((button) => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const form = slot.querySelector(`[data-allergy-remove-form="${CSS.escape(button.dataset.allergyRemove)}"]`);
      if (!form) return;
      form.hidden = false;
      button.hidden = true;
      form.querySelector('input')?.focus();
    }));
    slot.querySelectorAll('[data-allergy-remove-confirm]').forEach((button) => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const reason = button.parentElement.querySelector('[data-allergy-remove-reason]')?.value.trim() || '';
      if (!reason) { message(slot, plain('clinical.removeReasonRequired', 'Enter the reason for removing.')); return; }
      submit(slot, button, `allergies/${button.dataset.allergyRemoveConfirm}/remove`, { reason });
    }));
  }

  // ---- Active problem list (OPD and IPD A boxes)
  function renderProblems(slot, summary) {
    const editable = canEdit(slot);
    const problems = summary?.problems || [];
    const active = problems.filter((p) => p.status === 'active');
    const resolved = problems.filter((p) => p.status === 'resolved');
    const item = (p, nextStatus, buttonKey, buttonText) => `<li>
        <span class="clinical-problem-text"><strong>${escapeHtml(p.problem)}</strong>${p.icd10 ? ` <span class="clinical-code">${escapeHtml(p.icd10)}</span>` : ''}${p.notedOn ? ` · ${escapeHtml(p.notedOn)}` : ''} · ${escapeHtml(p.recordedBy || '')}</span>
        ${editable ? `<button type="button" class="clinical-button secondary" data-problem-status="${escapeHtml(p.id)}" data-next-status="${nextStatus}">${label(buttonKey, buttonText)}</button>` : ''}
      </li>`;
    slot.innerHTML = `
      <div class="clinical-summary-card clinical-problem-card">
        <h4 class="clinical-summary-title">${label('', 'Active problem list', 'បញ្ជីបញ្ហាសុខភាពកំពុងជួបប្រទះ')}</h4>
        ${!summary ? `<p class="clinical-empty">${unavailableMessage()}</p>`
          : active.length ? `<ul class="clinical-problem-list">${active.map((p) => item(p, 'resolved', 'clinical.problem.resolve', 'Mark resolved')).join('')}</ul>`
            : `<p class="clinical-empty">${label('clinical.problem.none', 'No health problems are recorded as ongoing.')}</p>`}
        ${resolved.length ? `<details class="clinical-resolved"><summary>${label('clinical.problem.resolved', 'Resolved problems')} (${resolved.length})</summary>
          <ul class="clinical-problem-list">${resolved.map((p) => item(p, 'active', 'clinical.problem.reactivate', 'Make active again')).join('')}</ul></details>` : ''}
        ${summary && editable ? `<div class="clinical-add-form" data-required-island data-problem-add-form>
          <label class="wide"><span>${label('clinical.problem.name', 'Problem')}</span>
            <input type="text" data-emr-transient data-problem-field="problem" maxlength="300" required></label>
          <label><span>ICD-10</span>
            <input type="text" data-emr-transient data-problem-field="icd10" maxlength="20" pattern="[A-Za-z][0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9A-Za-z]{1,4})?" placeholder="E11"></label>
          <label><span>${label('', 'Date', 'កាលបរិច្ឆេទ')}</span>
            <input type="date" data-emr-transient data-problem-field="notedOn" max="${today()}"></label>
          <button type="button" class="clinical-button" data-problem-add>${label('clinical.problem.add', 'Add problem')}</button>
        </div>` : ''}
        <p class="clinical-message" data-clinical-message role="status"></p>
      </div>`;
    slot.querySelector('[data-problem-add]')?.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
      const value = (name) => slot.querySelector(`[data-problem-field="${name}"]`)?.value.trim() || '';
      if (!value('problem')) { message(slot, plain('clinical.problem.required', 'Enter the problem.')); slot.querySelector('[data-problem-field="problem"]')?.focus(); return; }
      submit(slot, event.currentTarget, 'problems', { problem: value('problem'), icd10: value('icd10'), notedOn: value('notedOn') });
    });
    slot.querySelectorAll('[data-problem-status]').forEach((button) => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
      submit(slot, button, `problems/${button.dataset.problemStatus}/status`, { status: button.dataset.nextStatus });
    }));
  }

  function renderSlots() {
    const summary = current();
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-soap-slot="structured-allergy"], [data-soap-slot="problem-list"]').forEach(slot => {
      slot.dataset.summaryPatientKey = api()?.get().patientKey || '';
      slot.dataset.summaryRevision = String(api()?.get().revision || 0);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-soap-slot="structured-allergy"]').forEach((slot) => {
      slot.hidden = false;
      slot.classList.add('clinical-summary-slot');
      renderAllergies(slot, summary);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-soap-slot="problem-list"]').forEach((slot) => {
      slot.hidden = false;
      slot.classList.add('clinical-summary-slot');
      renderProblems(slot, summary);
    });
    if (api()?.patient()?.patientKey && !['ready', 'saving'].includes(api()?.get().status)) {
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-soap-slot="structured-allergy"], [data-soap-slot="problem-list"]').forEach(slot => {
        const retry = document.createElement('button');
        retry.type = 'button';
        retry.textContent = plain('', 'Reload', 'ផ្ទុកឡើងវិញ');
        retry.addEventListener('click', () => api()?.refresh());
        slot.append(retry);
      });
    }
  }

  function refresh() {
    renderSlots();
  }

  document.addEventListener('emr:clinical-summary', refresh);
  // Runs after the record values are restored on load and after a save.
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded', () => window.setTimeout(refresh, 0));
  window.addEventListener('emr-session-ready', renderSlots);
  window.addEventListener('borkeo-language-change', refresh);
  // The record edit / view switch changes whether the add forms are shown.
  document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
    if (event.target.closest?.('[data-editor-toggle], [data-editor-cancel]')) window.setTimeout(renderSlots, 50);
  });
  window.EmrClinicalSummaryEditors = { refresh };
})();
===== END FILE: public/clinical-summary.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: public/emr-consultation-workspace.js =====
/**
 * Borkeo EMR v43.3.0 - Integrated Clinical Consultation Workspace Controller
 * Aligned with WCAG 2.2 AA and IEC 62366-1.
 * Screen presentation layer providing two-way sync with frozen form fields.
 */

'use strict';

(function initConsultationWorkspace() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;

  const VIEW_MODE_KEY = 'borkeo_consultation_view_mode';
  let currentViewMode = 'integrated';

  function isConsultationActive() {
    // The canonical SOAP form now owns consultation controls and shared fields.
    // Retain legacy helper definitions for compatibility, without creating a duplicate UI.
    return false;
  }

  function getViewMode() {
    try {
      const mode = localStorage.getItem(VIEW_MODE_KEY);
      if (mode === 'compact' || mode === 'classic' || mode === 'integrated') return mode;
      return 'integrated';
    } catch {
      return 'integrated';
    }
  }

  function setViewMode(mode) {
    currentViewMode = mode;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(VIEW_MODE_KEY, mode);
    } catch {}

    const body = document.body;
    body.classList.remove('consultation-mode-integrated', 'consultation-mode-compact', 'consultation-mode-classic');
    body.classList.add(`consultation-mode-${mode}`);

    document.querySelectorAll('.cw-view-mode-btn').forEach(btn => {
      const active = btn.dataset.viewMode === mode;
      btn.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
      btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(active));
    });

    ensureClassicReturnBar(mode === 'classic');
  }

  function ensureClassicReturnBar(show) {
    let bar = document.getElementById('cw_classic_return_bar');
    if (show) {
      if (!bar) {
        bar = document.createElement('div');
        bar.id = 'cw_classic_return_bar';
        bar.className = 'cw-classic-return-bar no-print';
        bar.innerHTML = `
          <button type="button" class="cw-btn-return-integrated" id="cw_btn_return_integrated">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left" aria-hidden="true"></i>
            <span>ត្រឡប់ទៅទម្រង់រួមបញ្ចូលគ្នា</span>
          </button>
        `;
        const host = document.getElementById('sub_opd');
        if (host) host.prepend(bar);
        bar.querySelector('#cw_btn_return_integrated')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
          setViewMode('integrated');
        });
      }
      bar.hidden = false;
    } else if (bar) {
      bar.hidden = true;
    }
  }

  // 1. Build the Modern Consultation Workspace HTML
  function renderConsultationWorkspace(host) {
    if (document.getElementById('emr-consultation-workspace')) return;

    const workspace = document.createElement('section');
    workspace.id = 'emr-consultation-workspace';
    workspace.className = 'emr-consultation-workspace no-print';
    workspace.setAttribute('aria-label', 'ទម្រង់ពិនិត្យព្យាបាល');

    workspace.innerHTML = `
      <div class="emr-consultation-grid">
        <!-- Left: Clinical Consultation Form Card -->
        <article class="emr-consultation-card">
          <div class="consultation-card-top-bar">
            <h2 class="consultation-heading">ការពិនិត្យព្យាបាល</h2>
            <div class="cw-view-mode-bar" role="group" aria-label="ទម្រង់បង្ហាញ">
              <button type="button" class="cw-view-mode-btn is-active" id="cw_mode_integrated" data-view-mode="integrated" aria-pressed="true" title="ទម្រង់រួមបញ្ចូលគ្នា">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-layer-group" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                <span>ទម្រង់រួមបញ្ចូលគ្នា</span>
              </button>
              <button type="button" class="cw-view-mode-btn" id="cw_mode_compact" data-view-mode="compact" aria-pressed="false" title="ទម្រង់សង្ខេប">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-table-columns" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                <span>ទម្រង់សង្ខេប</span>
              </button>
              <button type="button" class="cw-view-mode-btn" id="cw_mode_classic" data-view-mode="classic" aria-pressed="false" title="ទម្រង់ពិស្ដារពេញលេញ">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-file-lines" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                <span>ទម្រង់ពិស្ដារពេញលេញ</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Chief Complaint -->
          <div class="consultation-field-wrap">
            <label class="consultation-label" for="consultation_chief_complaint">
              ហេតុផលមកពេទ្យ <span class="req-star" aria-hidden="true">*</span>
            </label>
            <textarea
              id="consultation_chief_complaint"
              class="consultation-chief-complaint"
              data-emr-transient=""
              data-emr-no-save=""
              rows="2"
              placeholder="សូមបញ្ចូលហេតុផលមកពិនិត្យ ឬរោគសញ្ញាសំខាន់..."></textarea>
          </div>

          <!-- Vital Signs Section (6 Metric Cards) -->
          <h3 class="vitals-heading">សញ្ញាសំខាន់</h3>
          <div class="vitals-metric-grid">
            <div class="vital-metric-card">
              <span class="vital-metric-label">សម្ពាធឈាម (mmHg)</span>
              <input
                type="text"
                id="consultation_bp"
                class="vital-metric-input"
                data-emr-transient=""
                data-emr-no-save=""
                placeholder="120/80"
                aria-label="សម្ពាធឈាម">
            </div>
            <div class="vital-metric-card">
              <span class="vital-metric-label">ចង្វាក់បេះដូង (ដង/នាទី)</span>
              <input
                type="text"
                id="consultation_pulse"
                class="vital-metric-input"
                data-emr-transient=""
                data-emr-no-save=""
                placeholder="78"
                aria-label="ចង្វាក់បេះដូង">
            </div>
            <div class="vital-metric-card">
              <span class="vital-metric-label">សីតុណ្ហភាព (°C)</span>
              <input
                type="text"
                id="consultation_temp"
                class="vital-metric-input"
                data-emr-transient=""
                data-emr-no-save=""
                placeholder="36.8"
                aria-label="សីតុណ្ហភាព">
            </div>
            <div class="vital-metric-card">
              <span class="vital-metric-label">អុកស៊ីសែនក្នុងឈាម (SpO2, %)</span>
              <input
                type="text"
                id="consultation_spo2"
                class="vital-metric-input"
                data-emr-transient=""
                data-emr-no-save=""
                placeholder="98"
                aria-label="អុកស៊ីសែនក្នុងឈាម">
            </div>
            <div class="vital-metric-card">
              <span class="vital-metric-label">ដង្ហើម (ដង/នាទី)</span>
              <input
                type="text"
                id="consultation_resp"
                class="vital-metric-input"
                data-emr-transient=""
                data-emr-no-save=""
                placeholder="18"
                aria-label="ដង្ហើម">
            </div>
            <div class="vital-metric-card">
              <span class="vital-metric-label">ទម្ងន់ (kg)</span>
              <input
                type="text"
                id="consultation_weight"
                class="vital-metric-input"
                data-emr-transient=""
                data-emr-no-save=""
                placeholder="62"
                aria-label="ទម្ងន់">
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Row 1: HPI & Physical Exam -->
          <div class="consultation-fields-row">
            <div class="consultation-field-wrap">
              <label class="consultation-label" for="consultation_hpi">ប្រវត្តិឫសគល់រោគ</label>
              <textarea
                id="consultation_hpi"
                class="consultation-textarea"
                data-emr-transient=""
                data-emr-no-save=""
                placeholder="ប្រវត្តិជំងឺ និងការវិវត្តនៃរោគសញ្ញា..."></textarea>
            </div>
            <div class="consultation-field-wrap">
              <label class="consultation-label" for="consultation_exam">ការពិនិត្យរាងកាយ</label>
              <textarea
                id="consultation_exam"
                class="consultation-textarea"
                data-emr-transient=""
                data-emr-no-save=""
                placeholder="លទ្ធផលនៃការពិនិត្យរាងកាយតាមប្រព័ន្ធសរីរាង្គ..."></textarea>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Row 2: Diagnosis & Treatment Plan -->
          <div class="consultation-fields-row">
            <div class="consultation-field-wrap">
              <label class="consultation-label" for="consultation_diagnosis">
                រោគវិនិច្ឆ័យ <span class="req-star" aria-hidden="true">*</span>
              </label>
              <textarea
                id="consultation_diagnosis"
                class="consultation-textarea"
                data-emr-transient=""
                data-emr-no-save=""
                placeholder="1. រោគវិនិច្ឆ័យចម្បង&#10;2. រោគវិនិច្ឆ័យបន្ទាប់បន្សំ..."></textarea>
            </div>
            <div class="consultation-field-wrap">
              <label class="consultation-label" for="consultation_plan">
                ផែនការព្យាបាល <span class="req-star" aria-hidden="true">*</span>
              </label>
              <textarea
                id="consultation_plan"
                class="consultation-textarea"
                data-emr-transient=""
                data-emr-no-save=""
                placeholder="1. បញ្ជាវិភាគបន្ថែម&#10;2. ឱសថព្យាបាល&#10;3. ការណែនាំ និងការតាមដាន..."></textarea>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Bottom Action Bar -->
          <div class="consultation-actions-bar">
            <!-- NPCA Toggle -->
            <label class="npca-toggle-group">
              <span class="npca-toggle-label">NPCA (ថ្នាក់លើខំអនុវត្តអន្តរជាតិ)</span>
              <div class="cw-switch">
                <input type="checkbox" id="consultation_npca_toggle" data-emr-transient="" data-emr-no-save="">
                <span class="cw-slider" aria-hidden="true"></span>
              </div>
              <span class="cw-info-bubble" title="ការបញ្ជាក់សេវាបង់ប្រាក់គ្លីនិក NPCA">ⓘ</span>
            </label>

            <!-- Buttons Group -->
            <div class="consultation-buttons-group">
              <button type="button" class="btn-consultation-save" id="consultation_btn_save">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                <span>រក្សាទុក</span>
              </button>
              <button type="button" class="btn-consultation-print" id="consultation_btn_print">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-print" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                <span>បោះពុម្ព</span>
              </button>
              <div class="consultation-actions-dropdown-wrap">
                <button type="button" class="btn-consultation-other" id="consultation_btn_other" aria-expanded="false" aria-haspopup="true">
                  <span>សកម្មភាពផ្សេងៗ</span>
                  <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-down" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                </button>
                <div class="consultation-menu" id="consultation_menu_actions" hidden>
                  <button type="button" class="consultation-menu-item" data-action="insert-rx-to-plan">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-file-prescription" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                    <span>បញ្ចូលវេជ្ជបញ្ជាទៅក្នុងផែនការ</span>
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" class="consultation-menu-item" data-action="print-rx">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-prescription" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                    <span>បោះពុម្ពវេជ្ជបញ្ជា</span>
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" class="consultation-menu-item" data-action="print-admission">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-file-medical" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                    <span>បោះពុម្ភលិខិតចូល</span>
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" class="consultation-menu-item" data-action="print-triage">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-truck-medical" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                    <span>បោះពុម្ពទម្រង់ទ្រីយ៉ា</span>
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" class="consultation-menu-item" data-action="transfer-ward">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-bed-pulse" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                    <span>បញ្ជូនទៅសម្រាកពេទ្យ</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </article>

        <!-- Right: Tasks to Complete Checklist Card -->
        <aside class="emr-tasks-card" aria-label="ការងារត្រូវបំពេញ">
          <div class="tasks-card-header">
            <h3 class="tasks-card-title">ការងារត្រូវបំពេញ</h3>
            <button type="button" class="tasks-collapse-btn" id="tasks_collapse_toggle" aria-label="បង្រួម">
              <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-up" aria-hidden="true"></i>
            </button>
          </div>
          <div id="tasks_content_body">
            <div class="tasks-progress-summary">
              បានបំពេញ <span id="tasks_count_display">2/4</span>
            </div>
            <div class="tasks-progress-track">
              <div class="tasks-progress-fill" id="tasks_progress_bar" style="width: 50%;"></div>
            </div>
            <div class="tasks-list">
              <!-- Item 1: Allergy History -->
              <div class="task-item is-warning" id="task_item_allergy" role="button" tabindex="0" data-target="allergy">
                <div class="task-item-icon warning" id="task_icon_allergy">
                  <i class="fa-solid fa-exclamation" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                </div>
                <div class="task-item-content">
                  <div class="task-item-title">ប្រវត្តិអាឡែកស៊ី</div>
                  <div class="task-item-status" id="task_status_allergy">មិនទាន់កត់ត្រា</div>
                </div>
                <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right task-item-arrow" aria-hidden="true"></i>
              </div>

              <!-- Item 2: Physical Exam -->
              <div class="task-item" id="task_item_exam" role="button" tabindex="0" data-target="exam">
                <div class="task-item-icon pending" id="task_icon_exam"></div>
                <div class="task-item-content">
                  <div class="task-item-title">ការពិនិត្យរាងកាយ</div>
                  <div class="task-item-status" id="task_status_exam">មិនទាន់បំពេញ</div>
                </div>
                <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right task-item-arrow" aria-hidden="true"></i>
              </div>

              <!-- Item 3: Diagnosis -->
              <div class="task-item" id="task_item_diagnosis" role="button" tabindex="0" data-target="diagnosis">
                <div class="task-item-icon pending" id="task_icon_diagnosis"></div>
                <div class="task-item-content">
                  <div class="task-item-title">រោគវិនិច្ឆ័យ</div>
                  <div class="task-item-status" id="task_status_diagnosis">មិនទាន់បំពេញ</div>
                </div>
                <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right task-item-arrow" aria-hidden="true"></i>
              </div>

              <!-- Item 4: Treatment Plan -->
              <div class="task-item" id="task_item_plan" role="button" tabindex="0" data-target="plan">
                <div class="task-item-icon pending" id="task_icon_plan"></div>
                <div class="task-item-content">
                  <div class="task-item-title">ផែនការព្យាបាល</div>
                  <div class="task-item-status" id="task_status_plan">មិនទាន់បំពេញ</div>
                </div>
                <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right task-item-arrow" aria-hidden="true"></i>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </aside>
      </div>
    `;

    host.prepend(workspace);
    document.body.classList.add('modern-consultation-active');

    attachEventListeners(workspace);
    syncFromLegacy();
    updateChecklist();
    setViewMode(getViewMode());
  }

  // 2. Attach Interactive Listeners & Live Two-Way Sync
  function attachEventListeners(workspace) {
    // View mode switcher buttons
    workspace.querySelectorAll('.cw-view-mode-btn').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        setViewMode(btn.dataset.viewMode);
      });
    });

    // Chief Complaint sync
    const modernCc = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_chief_complaint');
    if (modernCc) {
      modernCc.addEventListener('input', () => {
        const legacy = document.getElementById('pmrs_chief_complaint');
        if (legacy && legacy.value !== modernCc.value) {
          legacy.value = modernCc.value;
          legacy.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
          legacy.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
        }
        updateChecklist();
      });
    }

    // Vitals sync
    const bpInput = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_bp');
    if (bpInput) {
      bpInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
        const parts = bpInput.value.split('/');
        const sys = parts[0]?.trim() || '';
        const dia = parts[1]?.trim() || '';
        const legacySys = document.getElementById('opd_bp_sys') || document.getElementById('web_triage_bp_sys');
        const legacyDia = document.getElementById('opd_bp_dia') || document.getElementById('web_triage_bp_dia');
        if (legacySys) { legacySys.value = sys; legacySys.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); }
        if (legacyDia) { legacyDia.value = dia; legacyDia.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); }
      });
    }

    const metricMap = [
      ['#consultation_pulse', ['opd_pulse', 'web_triage_pulse']],
      ['#consultation_temp', ['opd_temp', 'web_triage_temp']],
      ['#consultation_spo2', ['web_triage_spo2', 'opd_spo2']],
      ['#consultation_resp', ['opd_resp', 'web_triage_resp']],
      ['#consultation_weight', ['opd_weight', 'web_triage_weight']]
    ];

    metricMap.forEach(([sel, legacyIds]) => {
      const el = workspace.querySelector(sel);
      if (!el) return;
      el.addEventListener('input', () => {
        legacyIds.forEach(id => {
          const target = document.getElementById(id);
          if (target && target.value !== el.value) {
            target.value = el.value;
            target.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
            target.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
          }
        });
      });
    });

    // History textarea sync
    const modernHpi = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_hpi');
    if (modernHpi) {
      modernHpi.addEventListener('input', () => {
        const legacyHpi = document.querySelector('#sub_opd textarea[data-emr-key-index="13"]') || document.getElementById('ipd_history_present_illness');
        if (legacyHpi && legacyHpi.value !== modernHpi.value) {
          legacyHpi.value = modernHpi.value;
          legacyHpi.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
          legacyHpi.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
        }
      });
    }

    // Physical exam textarea sync
    const modernExam = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_exam');
    if (modernExam) {
      modernExam.addEventListener('input', () => {
        const legacyExam = document.querySelector('#sub_opd textarea[data-emr-key-index="22"]');
        if (legacyExam && legacyExam.value !== modernExam.value) {
          legacyExam.value = modernExam.value;
          legacyExam.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
          legacyExam.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
        }
        updateChecklist();
      });
    }

    // Diagnosis textarea sync
    const modernDiag = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_diagnosis');
    if (modernDiag) {
      modernDiag.addEventListener('input', () => {
        const legacyDiag = document.getElementById('emr_pmrs_opd_diagnosis');
        if (legacyDiag && legacyDiag.value !== modernDiag.value) {
          legacyDiag.value = modernDiag.value;
          legacyDiag.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
          legacyDiag.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
        }
        updateChecklist();
      });
    }

    // Treatment plan textarea sync
    const modernPlan = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_plan');
    if (modernPlan) {
      modernPlan.addEventListener('input', () => {
        const legacyPlan = document.getElementById('opd_plan_notes') || document.getElementById('web_rx_counsel_note');
        if (legacyPlan && legacyPlan.value !== modernPlan.value) {
          legacyPlan.value = modernPlan.value;
          legacyPlan.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
          legacyPlan.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
        }
        updateChecklist();
      });
    }

    // Save button
    const saveBtn = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_btn_save');
    if (saveBtn) {
      saveBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        saveBtn.disabled = true;
        const originalContent = saveBtn.innerHTML;
        saveBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin" aria-hidden="true"></i> <span>កំពុងរក្សាទុក…</span>';
        try {
          if (typeof window.savePatientRecord === 'function') {
            await window.savePatientRecord();
          }
        } finally {
          saveBtn.disabled = false;
          saveBtn.innerHTML = originalContent;
        }
      });
    }

    // Print button
    const printBtn = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_btn_print');
    if (printBtn) {
      printBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (typeof window.printSignedOpdForm === 'function') {
          window.printSignedOpdForm();
        } else {
          window.print();
        }
      });
    }

    // Other actions dropdown
    const otherBtn = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_btn_other');
    const menuEl = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_menu_actions');
    if (otherBtn && menuEl) {
      otherBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const isHidden = menuEl.hasAttribute('hidden');
        if (isHidden) {
          menuEl.removeAttribute('hidden');
          otherBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
        } else {
          menuEl.setAttribute('hidden', '');
          otherBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        }
      });

      document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (!otherBtn.contains(e.target) && !menuEl.contains(e.target)) {
          menuEl.setAttribute('hidden', '');
          otherBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        }
      });

      menuEl.querySelectorAll('.consultation-menu-item').forEach(item => {
        item.addEventListener('click', () => {
          menuEl.setAttribute('hidden', '');
          otherBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
          const action = item.dataset.action;
          if (action === 'insert-rx-to-plan') {
            insertPrescriptionsToPlan();
          } else if (action === 'print-rx' && typeof window.printSignedOpdPrescription === 'function') {
            window.printSignedOpdPrescription();
          } else if (action === 'print-admission' && typeof window.printAdmissionOnly === 'function') {
            window.printAdmissionOnly();
          } else if (action === 'print-triage' && typeof window.printTriageOnly === 'function') {
            window.printTriageOnly();
          } else if (action === 'transfer-ward') {
            const wardSelect = document.getElementById('web_opd_transfer_ward');
            if (wardSelect) {
              wardSelect.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
              wardSelect.focus();
            }
          }
        });
      });
    }

    // Helper: Insert prescribed medicines into plan textarea
    function insertPrescriptionsToPlan() {
      const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#opd_rx_table tbody tr');
      const medLines = [];
      rows.forEach(tr => {
        const nameInput = tr.querySelector('input[type="text"][list]') || tr.querySelector('input[type="text"]');
        const medName = nameInput?.value?.trim();
        if (medName) {
          const dose = tr.querySelector('[data-col="dose"] input, .opd-rx-dose')?.value?.trim();
          const route = tr.querySelector('[data-col="route"] select, .opd-rx-route')?.value?.trim();
          const sched = tr.querySelector('[data-col="schedule"] select, .opd-rx-schedule')?.value?.trim();
          const dur = tr.querySelector('[data-col="dur"] input, .opd-rx-dur')?.value?.trim();
          const qty = tr.querySelector('[data-col="qty"] input, .opd-rx-qty')?.value?.trim();
          let line = `- ${medName}`;
          const details = [dose, route, sched, dur ? `${dur}ថ្ងៃ` : '', qty ? `សរុប ${qty}` : ''].filter(Boolean).join(', ');
          if (details) line += ` (${details})`;
          medLines.push(line);
        }
      });

      if (medLines.length > 0) {
        const planEl = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_plan');
        if (planEl) {
          const current = planEl.value.trim();
          const header = 'ឱសថព្យាបាល៖';
          const newBlock = `${header}\n${medLines.join('\n')}`;
          if (current.includes(header)) {
            planEl.value = current + '\n\n' + newBlock;
          } else {
            planEl.value = current ? `${current}\n\n${newBlock}` : newBlock;
          }
          planEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
          planEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
          updateChecklist();
        }
      }
    }

    // Tasks checklist collapse toggle
    const collapseBtn = workspace.querySelector('#tasks_collapse_toggle');
    const bodyEl = workspace.querySelector('#tasks_content_body');
    if (collapseBtn && bodyEl) {
      collapseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isCollapsed = bodyEl.hidden;
        bodyEl.hidden = !isCollapsed;
        collapseBtn.innerHTML = isCollapsed
          ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-up" aria-hidden="true"></i>'
          : '<i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-down" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
      });
    }

    // Task items click-to-focus navigation
    workspace.querySelectorAll('.task-item').forEach(item => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const target = item.dataset.target;
        if (target === 'allergy') {
          window.switchOpdTab?.('ipd');
          const allergySlot = document.querySelector('[data-soap-slot="structured-allergy"]');
          if (allergySlot) {
            allergySlot.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
            allergySlot.querySelector('[data-allergy-field="substance"], button')?.focus();
          }
        } else if (target === 'exam') {
          const el = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_exam');
          if (el) { el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }); el.focus(); }
        } else if (target === 'diagnosis') {
          const el = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_diagnosis');
          if (el) { el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }); el.focus(); }
        } else if (target === 'plan') {
          const el = workspace.querySelector('#consultation_plan');
          if (el) { el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }); el.focus(); }
        }
      });
    });

    // Listen to legacy fields changes to update modern inputs dynamically
    const legacyDiag = document.getElementById('emr_pmrs_opd_diagnosis');
    if (legacyDiag) {
      legacyDiag.addEventListener('input', () => {
        if (modernDiag && modernDiag.value !== legacyDiag.value) {
          modernDiag.value = legacyDiag.value;
          updateChecklist();
        }
      });
    }

    const legacyPlan = document.getElementById('opd_plan_notes');
    if (legacyPlan) {
      legacyPlan.addEventListener('input', () => {
        if (modernPlan && modernPlan.value !== legacyPlan.value) {
          modernPlan.value = legacyPlan.value;
          updateChecklist();
        }
      });
    }
  }

  // 3. Sync initial/loaded data from legacy inputs into modern inputs
  function syncFromLegacy() {
    const modernCc = document.getElementById('consultation_chief_complaint');
    const legacyCc = document.getElementById('pmrs_chief_complaint');
    if (modernCc && legacyCc && legacyCc.value) modernCc.value = legacyCc.value;

    const bpInput = document.getElementById('consultation_bp');
    const sys = document.getElementById('opd_bp_sys')?.value || document.getElementById('web_triage_bp_sys')?.value || '';
    const dia = document.getElementById('opd_bp_dia')?.value || document.getElementById('web_triage_bp_dia')?.value || '';
    if (bpInput && (sys || dia)) bpInput.value = `${sys}/${dia}`;

    const pulseInput = document.getElementById('consultation_pulse');
    const pulse = document.getElementById('opd_pulse')?.value || document.getElementById('web_triage_pulse')?.value || '';
    if (pulseInput && pulse) pulseInput.value = pulse;

    const tempInput = document.getElementById('consultation_temp');
    const temp = document.getElementById('opd_temp')?.value || document.getElementById('web_triage_temp')?.value || '';
    if (tempInput && temp) tempInput.value = temp;

    const spo2Input = document.getElementById('consultation_spo2');
    const spo2 = document.getElementById('web_triage_spo2')?.value || document.getElementById('opd_spo2')?.value || '';
    if (spo2Input && spo2) spo2Input.value = spo2;

    const respInput = document.getElementById('consultation_resp');
    const resp = document.getElementById('opd_resp')?.value || document.getElementById('web_triage_resp')?.value || '';
    if (respInput && resp) respInput.value = resp;

    const weightInput = document.getElementById('consultation_weight');
    const weight = document.getElementById('opd_weight')?.value || document.getElementById('web_triage_weight')?.value || '';
    if (weightInput && weight) weightInput.value = weight;

    const modernHpi = document.getElementById('consultation_hpi');
    const legacyHpi = document.querySelector('#sub_opd textarea[data-emr-key-index="13"]') || document.getElementById('ipd_history_present_illness');
    if (modernHpi && legacyHpi && legacyHpi.value) modernHpi.value = legacyHpi.value;

    const modernExam = document.getElementById('consultation_exam');
    const legacyExam = document.querySelector('#sub_opd textarea[data-emr-key-index="22"]');
    if (modernExam && legacyExam && legacyExam.value) modernExam.value = legacyExam.value;

    const modernDiag = document.getElementById('consultation_diagnosis');
    const legacyDiag = document.getElementById('emr_pmrs_opd_diagnosis');
    if (modernDiag && legacyDiag && legacyDiag.value) modernDiag.value = legacyDiag.value;

    const modernPlan = document.getElementById('consultation_plan');
    const legacyPlan = document.getElementById('opd_plan_notes') || document.getElementById('web_rx_counsel_note');
    if (modernPlan && legacyPlan && legacyPlan.value) modernPlan.value = legacyPlan.value;
  }

  // 4. Update Tasks Checklist State & Progress Bar
  function updateChecklist() {
    let completedCount = 0;

    // 1. Allergy History
    const allergyState = window.EmrClinicalSummary?.allergyState?.()?.state;
    const allergyRecorded = allergyState === 'allergy' || allergyState === 'none';
    const allergyItem = document.getElementById('task_item_allergy');
    const allergyIcon = document.getElementById('task_icon_allergy');
    const allergyStatus = document.getElementById('task_status_allergy');

    if (allergyItem && allergyIcon && allergyStatus) {
      if (allergyRecorded) {
        completedCount++;
        allergyItem.className = 'task-item is-success';
        allergyIcon.className = 'task-item-icon success';
        allergyIcon.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-check" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
        allergyStatus.textContent = 'បានកត់ត្រា';
      } else {
        allergyItem.className = 'task-item is-warning';
        allergyIcon.className = 'task-item-icon warning';
        allergyIcon.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-exclamation" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
        allergyStatus.textContent = 'មិនទាន់កត់ត្រា';
      }
    }

    // 2. Physical Examination
    const examVal = (document.getElementById('consultation_exam')?.value || '').trim();
    const examCompleted = examVal.length > 0;
    const examItem = document.getElementById('task_item_exam');
    const examIcon = document.getElementById('task_icon_exam');
    const examStatus = document.getElementById('task_status_exam');

    if (examItem && examIcon && examStatus) {
      if (examCompleted) {
        completedCount++;
        examItem.className = 'task-item is-success';
        examIcon.className = 'task-item-icon success';
        examIcon.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-check" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
        examStatus.textContent = 'បានបំពេញ';
      } else {
        examItem.className = 'task-item';
        examIcon.className = 'task-item-icon pending';
        examIcon.innerHTML = '';
        examStatus.textContent = 'មិនទាន់បំពេញ';
      }
    }

    // 3. Diagnosis
    const diagVal = (document.getElementById('consultation_diagnosis')?.value || '').trim();
    const diagCompleted = diagVal.length > 0;
    const diagItem = document.getElementById('task_item_diagnosis');
    const diagIcon = document.getElementById('task_icon_diagnosis');
    const diagStatus = document.getElementById('task_status_diagnosis');

    if (diagItem && diagIcon && diagStatus) {
      if (diagCompleted) {
        completedCount++;
        diagItem.className = 'task-item is-success';
        diagIcon.className = 'task-item-icon success';
        diagIcon.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-check" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
        diagStatus.textContent = 'បានបំពេញ';
      } else {
        diagItem.className = 'task-item';
        diagIcon.className = 'task-item-icon pending';
        diagIcon.innerHTML = '';
        diagStatus.textContent = 'មិនទាន់បំពេញ';
      }
    }

    // 4. Treatment Plan
    const planVal = (document.getElementById('consultation_plan')?.value || '').trim();
    const planCompleted = planVal.length > 0;
    const planItem = document.getElementById('task_item_plan');
    const planIcon = document.getElementById('task_icon_plan');
    const planStatus = document.getElementById('task_status_plan');

    if (planItem && planIcon && planStatus) {
      if (planCompleted) {
        completedCount++;
        planItem.className = 'task-item is-success';
        planIcon.className = 'task-item-icon success';
        planIcon.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-check" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
        planStatus.textContent = 'បានបំពេញ';
      } else {
        planItem.className = 'task-item';
        planIcon.className = 'task-item-icon pending';
        planIcon.innerHTML = '';
        planStatus.textContent = 'មិនទាន់បំពេញ';
      }
    }

    // Update Counter & Progress Bar
    const countDisplay = document.getElementById('tasks_count_display');
    const progressBar = document.getElementById('tasks_progress_bar');
    if (countDisplay) countDisplay.textContent = `${completedCount}/4`;
    if (progressBar) progressBar.style.width = `${(completedCount / 4) * 100}%`;
  }

  // 5. Enhance Patient Tabs and Breadcrumbs
  function enhanceRecordNavigation() {
    const tabsWrapper = document.getElementById('emr-chart-tabs-wrapper');
    if (!tabsWrapper) return;
  }

  // 6. Bootstrap Initializer
  function init() {
    if (!isConsultationActive()) return;

    const opdHost = document.getElementById('sub_opd');
    if (opdHost) {
      renderConsultationWorkspace(opdHost);
      enhanceRecordNavigation();
    }
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded', () => {
    init();
    syncFromLegacy();
    updateChecklist();
  });
  document.addEventListener('emr:clinical-summary', updateChecklist);

  // Periodic safety check to catch delayed session / patient state
  [300, 1000, 2500].forEach(delay => {
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (isConsultationActive()) {
        init();
        syncFromLegacy();
        updateChecklist();
      }
    }, delay);
  });

  window.EmrConsultationWorkspace = {
    init,
    syncFromLegacy,
    updateChecklist,
    setViewMode,
    getViewMode: () => currentViewMode
  };
})();
===== END FILE: public/emr-consultation-workspace.js =====

      loadError: 'មិនអាចបើកទិន្នន័យអ្នកជំងឺបានទេ។', saveError: 'មិនអាចរក្សាទុកទិន្នន័យបានទេ៖ ',
      idRequired: 'សូមបញ្ចូលលេខករណី ឬលេខប័ណ្ណសុខភាព។', nameRequired: 'សូមបញ្ចូលឈ្មោះអ្នកជំងឺ។',
      detailsRequired: 'សូមបំពេញភេទ អាយុ និងកាលបរិច្ឆេទចូល។', departmentRequired: 'សូមជ្រើសរើសផ្នែកដែលត្រូវបញ្ជូនអ្នកជំងឺ។',
      opdOnly: 'អ្នកជំងឺត្រូវបានបញ្ជូនទៅសេវាពិគ្រោះក្រៅ។ បង្ហាញតែទម្រង់ OPD។',
      ipdOnly: 'អ្នកជំងឺត្រូវបានបញ្ជូនទៅផ្នែកសម្រាកពេទ្យ។ បង្ហាញតែទម្រង់ IPD និងដំណើរការថែទាំ។',
      care: 'ដំណើរការថែទាំ', rights: 'សិទ្ធិអតិថិជន', agreement: 'កិច្ចសន្យា',

  let csrfToken = '';
  let storagePrepared = false;

  function language() {
    return window.currentLang === 'english' ? 'english' : 'khmer';
  }

  function t(key) {
    return labels[language()][key] || labels.khmer[key] || key;
  }

  function value(id) {
    const element = document.getElementById(id);
    return element && 'value' in element ? String(element.value || '').trim() : '';
  }

  function selectedSex() {
    return document.querySelector('input[name="sex_radio"]:checked')?.value || (document.getElementById('web_sex_male')?.checked ? 'ប្រុស' : (document.getElementById('web_sex_female')?.checked ? 'ស្រី' : ''));
  }

  function restorePatientSex(patient) {
    const stored = String(patient?.sex || patient?.data?.['scope:registration:id:web_sex'] || patient?.data?.['system:sex'] || '').trim();
    const latin = stored.toUpperCase();
    const normalized = stored === 'ប្រុស' || ['M','MALE','BOY'].includes(latin) ? 'ប្រុស'
      : stored === 'ស្រី' || ['F','FEMALE','GIRL'].includes(latin) ? 'ស្រី' : '';
    if (!normalized) return;
    document.querySelectorAll('input[name="sex_radio"]').forEach(control => {
      const selected = control.value === normalized;
      control.checked = selected;
      if (selected) control.setAttribute('checked','checked');
      else control.removeAttribute('checked');
    });
    const maleRadio = document.getElementById('web_sex_male');
    const femaleRadio = document.getElementById('web_sex_female');
    if (maleRadio && femaleRadio) {
      if (normalized === 'ប្រុស') {
        maleRadio.checked = true;
        femaleRadio.checked = false;
        maleRadio.setAttribute('checked', 'checked');
        femaleRadio.removeAttribute('checked');
      } else if (normalized === 'ស្រី') {
        femaleRadio.checked = true;
        maleRadio.checked = false;
        femaleRadio.setAttribute('checked', 'checked');
        maleRadio.removeAttribute('checked');
      }
    }
    window.syncFields?.();
  }

  function getEmrCarePath() {
    if (loadedPatient?.serviceMode === 'ipd' || (loadedPatient?.departmentCode && loadedPatient.departmentCode !== 'opd')) {
      const code = loadedPatient.departmentCode;
      const def = Object.values(departmentDefinitions).find(d => d.code === code) ||
                  Object.values(otherDefinitions).find(d => d.code === code);
      if (def) return { ...def, selected: [def] };
      return {
        code: loadedPatient.departmentCode || '',
        kh: loadedPatient.departmentNameKh || 'សម្រាកពេទ្យ',
        en: loadedPatient.departmentNameEn || 'Inpatient',
        mode: 'ipd',
        module: '',
        selected: [],
