        if (!cell) return;
        const dateText = row.querySelector('[data-nursing-date]')?.value || '';
        const local = /^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/.exec(dateText);
        const day = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.exec(dateText)?.[0] ||
          (local ? `${local[3]}-${local[2].padStart(2,'0')}-${local[1].padStart(2,'0')}` : '');
        const entry = signaturesByDay.get(day);
        const signer = entry?.editorName || '';
        const textarea = cell.querySelector('textarea');
        if (textarea) {
          if (entry && (!textarea.value.trim() || textarea.dataset.autoNurseName === 'true')) {
            textarea.value = signer;
            textarea.readOnly = true;
            textarea.dataset.autoNurseName = 'true';
          }
        }
        let stamp = cell.querySelector('[data-inline-nursing-esign]');
        if (!stamp) {
          stamp = document.createElement('div');
          stamp.dataset.inlineNursingEsign = '1';
          stamp.className = 'inline-nursing-esign';
          cell.append(stamp);
        }
        const sameSignatureDay = parsedSignature('nursing')?.signedAt &&
          editorHistory.filter(item => item.scope === 'nursing').at(-1)?.day === day;
        const signature = sameSignatureDay ? parsedSignature('nursing') : null;
        stamp.innerHTML = entry ? nursingSignatureStamp(signature || { signatureDataUrl:'',username:'' }, escapeHistory(signer)) : '';
        const dailyHistory = editorHistory.filter(item => item.scope === 'nursing' && item.day === day);
        if (dailyHistory.length) {
          let details = cell.querySelector('[data-nursing-history]');
          if (!details) { details = document.createElement('details'); details.dataset.nursingHistory = '1'; details.className='no-print nursing-editor-history'; cell.append(details); }
          details.innerHTML = `<summary>${escapeHistory(signer)} · ${escapeHistory(new Date(entry.savedAt).toLocaleString())} · កំណត់ត្រាមុន</summary>${historyNotes(dailyHistory)}`;
        }
      });
    });
    const signature = parsedSignature('nursing') || currentNursingIdentitySignature();
    const signer = signature?.signerName || signature?.displayName || signature?.username || '';
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-auto-nursing-signature]').forEach(slot => {
      if (signature) slot.innerHTML = nursingSignatureStamp(signature, escapeHistory(signer));
    });
  }

  function renderRecordSignatures() {
    ensureNursingSignatureSlot();
    const opdSig = parsedSignature('opdDoctor') || parsedSignature('medical') || currentDoctorIdentitySignature();
    const opdContent = document.querySelector('#opd-doctor-esign [data-emr-esign-content]');
    if (opdContent) opdContent.innerHTML = signatureHtml(opdSig, 'រក្សាទុកដោយវេជ្ជបណ្ឌិត ដើម្បីភ្ជាប់ហត្ថលេខា');
    editorHistoryPanel('opd-doctor-esign', 'medical');

    const ipdSig = parsedSignature('ipdDoctor') || parsedSignature('medical') || currentDoctorIdentitySignature();
    const ipdContent = document.querySelector('#ipd-doctor-esign [data-emr-esign-content]');
    if (ipdContent) ipdContent.innerHTML = signatureHtml(ipdSig, 'រក្សាទុកដោយវេជ្ជបណ្ឌិត ដើម្បីភ្ជាប់ហត្ថលេខា');
    editorHistoryPanel('ipd-doctor-esign', 'medical');

    const admContent = document.querySelector('#adm-doctor-esign [data-emr-esign-content]');
    if (admContent) {
      const admSig = getAdmissionDoctorSignature();
      admContent.innerHTML = signatureHtml(admSig, 'មិនទាន់មានហត្ថលេខាគ្រូពេទ្យ');
    }
    const triage = document.querySelector('#triage-nurse-esign [data-emr-esign-content]');
    const triageSignature = parsedSignature('triage');
    if (triage) {
      const preview = triageSignature || currentTriageIdentitySignature();
      triage.innerHTML = signatureHtml(preview, 'នឹងបំពេញស្វ័យប្រវត្តិពីគណនីគិលានុបដ្ឋាកទ្រីយ៉ា');
    }
    const nursing = document.querySelector('#nursing-staff-esign [data-emr-esign-content]');
    if (nursing) nursing.innerHTML = signatureHtml(parsedSignature('nursing') || currentNursingIdentitySignature(), 'រក្សាទុកដោយគិលានុបដ្ឋាក ឬឆ្មប ដើម្បីភ្ជាប់ហត្ថលេខា');
    const nursingSlot = document.getElementById('nursing-staff-esign');
    if (nursingSlot) {
      let history = nursingSlot.nextElementSibling;
      if (!history?.matches('[data-nursing-day-history]')) {
        history = document.createElement('div'); history.dataset.nursingDayHistory='1'; history.className='emr-editor-history no-print';
        nursingSlot.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', history);
      }
      const byDay = new Map();
      editorHistory.filter(entry => entry.scope === 'nursing').forEach(entry => {
        if (!byDay.has(entry.day)) byDay.set(entry.day, []);
        byDay.get(entry.day).push(entry);
      });
      history.innerHTML = [...byDay].map(([day, entries]) => `<details><summary>${escapeHistory(day)} · ${escapeHistory(entries.at(-1).editorName)} · ${escapeHistory(new Date(entries.at(-1).savedAt).toLocaleString())}</summary>${historyNotes(entries)}</details>`).join('');
      history.hidden = !byDay.size;
    }
    renderInlineNursingSignatures();
  }

  function createSaveButton(options = {}) {
    const button = document.createElement('button');
    button.type = 'button';
    button.className = 'btn-blue emr-save-button';
    button.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk" aria-hidden="true"></i><span></span>';
    button.querySelector('span').textContent = t('save');
    button.addEventListener('click', () => void savePatientRecord({
      ...options,
      markMedicalComplete: options.markMedicalComplete || document.body.dataset.clinicalView === 'medical',
    }));
    return button;
  }

  function addSaveBar(container, position = 'afterbegin', options = {}) {
    if (!container || container.querySelector(':scope > .emr-save-bar')) return;
    const bar = document.createElement('div');
    bar.className = 'emr-save-bar no-print';
    bar.append(createSaveButton(options));
    container.insertAdjacentElement(position, bar);
  }

  async function showPmrs2FollowUpPreview() {
    const patientKey = loadedPatient?.patientKey;
    if (!patientKey) { alert('សូមរក្សាទុក និងបើកកំណត់ត្រាអ្នកជំងឺជាមុនសិន។'); return; }
    const dialog = document.createElement('dialog');
    dialog.style.cssText = 'width:min(850px,94vw);max-height:88vh;overflow:auto;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:12px;padding:20px;font:14px/1.5 sans-serif';
    const heading = document.createElement('h2');
    heading.textContent = 'ពិនិត្យទិន្នន័យសម្រាប់ PMRS2';
    const intro = document.createElement('p');
    intro.textContent = 'ទិន្នន័យនេះមកពីកំណត់ត្រាដែលបានរក្សាទុក។ សូមពិនិត្យមុនបញ្ចូលក្នុង PMRS2។';
    const close = document.createElement('button');
    close.type = 'button';
    close.textContent = 'បិទ';
    close.onclick = () => dialog.close();
    dialog.append(heading, intro, close);
    dialog.addEventListener('close', () => dialog.remove());
    document.body.append(dialog);
    dialog.showModal();
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/pmrs2/preview?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}`, { credentials: 'same-origin', cache: 'no-store' });
      const payload = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(payload.error || 'មិនអាចទាញទិន្នន័យបានទេ។');
      const identity = document.createElement('p');
      identity.textContent = `អ្នកជំងឺ: ${payload.preview?.patientName || '—'} · លេខករណី: ${payload.preview?.caseNumber || '—'} · លេខប័ណ្ណសុខភាព: ${payload.preview?.healthId || '—'}`;
      dialog.append(identity);
      const explain = {
        FOLLOW_UP_DATE_TIME_REQUIRED: 'ត្រូវបញ្ជាក់ថ្ងៃ និងម៉ោង',
        FOLLOW_UP_VITALS_INCOMPLETE: 'សញ្ញាជីវិតមិនទាន់គ្រប់',
        FOLLOW_UP_EVOLUTION_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះការវិវត្តន៍',
        FOLLOW_UP_CONCLUSION_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះសេចក្តីសន្និដ្ឋាន',
        FOLLOW_UP_MEDICATION_REVIEW: 'ត្រូវពិនិត្យកម្រិត និងម៉ោងប្រើថ្នាំ',
        SAME_TIME_AS_ADMISSION_REVIEW: 'ម៉ោងតាមដានស្ទួននឹងម៉ោងចូលសម្រាក',
        MEDICATION_UNIT_REVIEW: 'ឯកតាថ្នាំត្រូវត្រួតពិនិត្យ',
        MEDICATION_TIMING_REVIEW: 'ពេលប្រើថ្នាំត្រូវបំពេញដោយដៃ',
        MEDICATION_DOSE_CONFLICT: 'កម្រិតថ្នាំមិនដូចគ្នា',
        MEDICATION_DOSE_REVIEW: 'កម្រិតថ្នាំត្រូវត្រួតពិនិត្យ',
        MEDICATION_SCHEDULE_REVIEW: 'កាលវិភាគថ្នាំត្រូវត្រួតពិនិត្យ',
        MEDICATION_ROUTE_REVIEW: 'ត្រូវជ្រើសផ្លូវប្រើថ្នាំក្នុង PMRS2',
        MEDICATION_NAME_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះឈ្មោះថ្នាំ',
        WARD_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះផ្នែក', SERVICE_MODE_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះប្រភេទអ្នកទទួលសេវា',
        CASE_TYPE_REQUIRED: 'សូមជ្រើសករណីថ្មី ឬករណីចាស់',
        CHIEF_COMPLAINT_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះរោគសញ្ញា',
        DOCTOR_DIAGNOSIS_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះរោគវិនិច្ឆ័យគ្រូពេទ្យ',
        ICD10_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះកូដ ICD10',
        PRESCRIPTION_EMPTY_REVIEW: 'វេជ្ជបញ្ជាគ្មានថ្នាំ',
        PRESCRIPTION_MEDICATION_REVIEW: 'ត្រូវពិនិត្យព័ត៌មានថ្នាំក្នុងវេជ្ជបញ្ជា',
        LAST_FOLLOW_UP_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះការតាមដានមានថ្ងៃម៉ោង',
        DISCHARGE_NOT_SAVED: 'ទម្រង់បញ្ចេញមិនទាន់បានរក្សាទុក',
        DISCHARGE_ADMISSION_TIME_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះថ្ងៃ និងម៉ោងចូល',
        DISCHARGE_ADMISSION_ICD10_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះ ICD10 ពេលចូល',
        DISCHARGE_DATE_TIME_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះថ្ងៃ និងម៉ោងចេញ',
        DISCHARGE_BEFORE_ADMISSION_REVIEW: 'ថ្ងៃម៉ោងចេញនៅមុនថ្ងៃម៉ោងចូល',
        DISCHARGE_DIAGNOSIS_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះរោគវិនិច្ឆ័យពេលចេញ',
        DISCHARGE_ICD10_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះ ICD10 ពេលចេញ',
        DISCHARGE_OUTCOME_REVIEW: 'ត្រូវពិនិត្យលទ្ធផលព្យាបាល',
        DISCHARGE_STATUS_REVIEW: 'ត្រូវពិនិត្យស្ថានភាពបញ្ចេញ',
        DISCHARGE_DECEASED_CONFLICT: 'លទ្ធផលស្លាប់ និងប្រអប់ស្លាប់មិនត្រូវគ្នា',
        TRIAGE_WEIGHT_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះទម្ងន់នៅ Triage',
        TRIAGE_HEIGHT_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះកម្ពស់នៅ Triage',
        TRIAGE_DATE_TIME_REQUIRED: 'ខ្វះថ្ងៃម៉ោងចូលនៅ Triage',
        TRIAGE_VITALS_INCOMPLETE: 'សញ្ញាជីវិត Triage មិនគ្រប់',
      };
      const section = (parent, title, value) => {
        const block = document.createElement('p');
        const label = document.createElement('strong');
        label.textContent = `${title}: `;
        const content = document.createElement('span');
        content.style.whiteSpace = 'pre-wrap';
        content.textContent = value || '—';
        block.append(label, content);
        parent.append(block);
      };
      const medicine = (parent, med) => section(parent, med.name || 'ថ្នាំ',
        `${med.doseAmount || '—'} ${med.unit || ''} · ${med.pmrsRoute || med.route || 'ផ្លូវប្រើមិនទាន់ច្បាស់'} · ${med.scheduleType || '—'} · ` +
        `ព្រឹក-ថ្ងៃ-ល្ងាច-យប់: ${med.morning || '—'}-${med.afternoon || '—'}-${med.evening || '—'}-${med.night || '—'}` +
        ` · ${med.days || '—'} ថ្ងៃ · សរុប ${med.quantity || '—'}` +
        (med.warnings?.length ? ` · ${med.warnings.map(code => explain[code] || code).join(', ')}` : ''));
      const review = payload.preview || {};
      section(dialog, 'ផ្នែកចូល / អ្នកទទួលសេវា', `${review.receivingWard || '—'} · ${review.serviceType || '—'}`);
      const triage = review.triage || {};
      section(dialog, 'ថ្ងៃម៉ោងចូល / ទម្ងន់ / កម្ពស់',
        `${triage.dateTime || '—'} · ${triage.weight || '—'} kg · ${triage.height || '—'} cm`);
      section(dialog, 'សញ្ញាជីវិតនៅ Triage',
        `T ${triage.temperature || '—'} °C · BP ${triage.systolic || '—'}/${triage.diastolic || '—'} · ` +
        `P ${triage.pulse || '—'} · RR ${triage.respiratoryRate || '—'} · SpO₂ ${triage.oxygenSaturation || '—'}%`);
      const triageIssues = (payload.reviewIssues || []).filter(issue => issue.path.startsWith('triage.'));
      if (triageIssues.length) section(dialog, 'ត្រូវពិនិត្យ Triage',
        triageIssues.map(issue => explain[issue.code] || issue.code).join(' · '));
      section(dialog, 'ទម្រង់ព្យាបាល', review.caseType);
      section(dialog, 'រោគសញ្ញា (បញ្ចូល ហើយចុច +)', review.chiefComplaint);
      section(dialog, 'រោគវិនិច្ឆ័យ / ICD10', `${review.diagnosisText || '—'} · ${review.diagnosisIcd10 || '—'}`);
      section(dialog, 'សេវាអមវេជ្ជសាស្ត្រ', review.ancillaryService);
      if (review.warnings?.length) section(dialog, 'ត្រូវពិនិត្យ', review.warnings.map(code => explain[code] || code).join(' · '));
      (payload.preview?.followUps || []).forEach((entry, index) => {
        const article = document.createElement('article');
        article.style.cssText = 'border-top:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:14px 0';
        const title = document.createElement('h3');
        title.textContent = `${index + 1}. ${entry.source === 'admission' ? 'ថ្ងៃចូលសម្រាក' : 'ពិនិត្យតាមដាន'} · ${entry.dateTime || entry.originalDateTime || 'ត្រូវបំពេញថ្ងៃ/ម៉ោង'}`;
        article.append(title);
        const vital = entry.vitals || {};
        section(article, 'សញ្ញាជីវិត', `T ${vital.temperature || '—'} °C · BP ${vital.systolic || '—'}/${vital.diastolic || '—'} · P ${vital.pulse || '—'} · RR ${vital.respiratoryRate || '—'} · SpO₂ ${vital.oxygenSaturation || '—'}%`);
        section(article, 'ការវិវត្តន៍', entry.evolution);
        section(article, 'សេចក្តីសន្និដ្ឋាន', entry.conclusion);
        (entry.medications || []).forEach(med => medicine(article, med));
        if (entry.warnings?.length) section(article, 'ត្រូវពិនិត្យ', entry.warnings.map(code => explain[code] || code).join(' · '));
        dialog.append(article);
      });
      if (review.prescription) {
        const rx = document.createElement('article');
        rx.style.cssText = 'border-top:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:14px 0';
        const title = document.createElement('h3');
        title.textContent = review.prescription.source === 'opd' ? 'វេជ្ជបញ្ជា OPD' : 'វេជ្ជបញ្ជាពេលចេញ IPD';
        rx.append(title);
        if (review.prescription.sourceDateTime) section(rx, 'ការតាមដានចុងក្រោយ', review.prescription.sourceDateTime);
        (review.prescription.medications || []).forEach(med => medicine(rx, med));
        if (review.prescription.followUpDate) section(rx, 'ថ្ងៃណាត់បន្ទាប់', review.prescription.followUpDate);
        if (review.prescription.counselling) section(rx, 'ការណែនាំ', review.prescription.counselling);
        if (review.prescription.warnings?.length) section(rx, 'ត្រូវពិនិត្យ', review.prescription.warnings.map(code => explain[code] || code).join(' · '));
        dialog.append(rx);
      }
      if (review.discharge) {
        const discharged = document.createElement('article');
        discharged.style.cssText = 'border-top:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:14px 0';
        const title = document.createElement('h3');
        title.textContent = 'បញ្ចប់ការព្យាបាល IPD';
        discharged.append(title);
        for (const [label, value] of [
          ['ផ្នែកចូល / ផ្នែកចេញ', `${review.discharge.admissionWard || '—'} / ${review.discharge.dischargeWard || '—'}`],
          ['ថ្ងៃម៉ោងចូល / ថ្ងៃម៉ោងចេញ', `${review.discharge.admissionAt || '—'} / ${review.discharge.dischargeAt || '—'}`],
          ['រោគវិនិច្ឆ័យពេលចូល / ICD10', `${review.discharge.admissionDiagnosis || '—'} / ${review.discharge.admissionIcd10 || '—'}`],
          ['រោគវិនិច្ឆ័យពេលចេញ / ICD10', `${review.discharge.dischargeDiagnosis || '—'} / ${review.discharge.dischargeIcd10 || '—'}`],
          ['លទ្ធផល / ស្ថានភាពចេញ', `${review.discharge.outcome || '—'} / ${review.discharge.status || '—'}`],
          ['ថ្ងៃណាត់បន្ទាប់', review.discharge.followUpDate],
          ['កន្លែងបញ្ជូនបន្ត', review.discharge.referralDestination],
        ]) if (value) section(discharged, label, value);
        if (review.discharge.warnings?.length) section(discharged, 'ត្រូវពិនិត្យ', review.discharge.warnings.map(code => explain[code] || code).join(' · '));
        dialog.append(discharged);
      }
      const role = String(window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole || '').toUpperCase();
      if (['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR'].includes(role)) {
        const exportArea = document.createElement('section');
        exportArea.style.cssText = 'border-top:2px solid #0b7895;padding:16px 0;margin-top:8px';
        const exportTitle = document.createElement('h3');
        exportTitle.textContent = 'រៀបចំទិន្នន័យទាំងអស់ក្រោយពិនិត្យ';
        const exportNote = document.createElement('p');
        exportNote.textContent = 'ទាញយកទិន្នន័យ Triage, ការព្យាបាល, ការតាមដានទាំងអស់, វេជ្ជបញ្ជា និងទម្រង់បញ្ចេញជាឯកសារ JSON។ វាមិនទាន់បញ្ចូលទិន្នន័យទៅគេហទំព័រ PMRS2 ដោយស្វ័យប្រវត្តិទេ។';
        const identifierLabel = document.createElement('label');
        identifierLabel.textContent = 'សូមវាយលេខបណ្ណសុខភាព ឬលេខករណីដែលបានផ្ទៀងផ្ទាត់៖ ';
        const identifier = document.createElement('input');
        identifier.type = 'text';
        identifier.autocomplete = 'off';
        identifier.style.cssText = 'min-width:220px;padding:7px;margin:6px';
        identifierLabel.append(identifier);
        const reviewLabel = document.createElement('label');
        reviewLabel.style.cssText = 'display:block;margin:10px 0';
        const reviewed = document.createElement('input');
        reviewed.type = 'checkbox';
        reviewed.style.marginRight = '7px';
        reviewLabel.append(reviewed, document.createTextNode('ខ្ញុំបានផ្ទៀងផ្ទាត់អ្នកជំងឺ និងពិនិត្យទិន្នន័យ/សញ្ញាព្រមានទាំងអស់ខាងលើ។'));
        const download = document.createElement('button');
        download.type = 'button';
        download.className = 'btn-blue';
        download.textContent = 'ទាញយកកញ្ចប់ទិន្នន័យ PMRS2';
        download.disabled = true;
        const exportStatus = document.createElement('p');
        exportStatus.setAttribute('role', 'status');
        const enable = () => { download.disabled = !reviewed.checked || !identifier.value.trim(); };
        identifier.addEventListener('input', enable);
        reviewed.addEventListener('change', enable);
        download.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          download.disabled = true;
          exportStatus.textContent = 'កំពុងរៀបចំទិន្នន័យ…';
          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/pmrs2/prepare-export', {
              method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin', cache: 'no-store',
              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': await getCsrfToken() },
              body: JSON.stringify({ patientKey: review.patientKey, reviewedVersion: review.patientVersion,
                confirmedIdentifier: identifier.value.trim(), reviewed: reviewed.checked }),
            });
            const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'មិនអាចរៀបចំទិន្នន័យបាន។');
            const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
            const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const link = document.createElement('a');
            link.href = objectUrl;
            link.download = 'Borkeo-PMRS2-reviewed-record.json';
            document.body.append(link);
            link.click();
            link.remove();
            window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl), 60_000);
            exportStatus.textContent = 'បានទាញយកកញ្ចប់ទិន្នន័យ។ សូមរក្សាទុកដោយសុវត្ថិភាព។ PMRS2 មិនទាន់បានទទួលទិន្នន័យនេះទេ។';
          } catch (error) {
            exportStatus.textContent = `មានបញ្ហា: ${error.message}`;
          } finally { enable(); }
        });
        exportArea.append(exportTitle, exportNote, identifierLabel, reviewLabel, download, exportStatus);
        dialog.append(exportArea);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      const message = document.createElement('p');
      message.textContent = `មានបញ្ហា: ${error.message}`;
      dialog.append(message);
    }
  }

  function switchNursingSection(sectionName) {
    const sections = {
      care: document.getElementById('nursing-care-section'),
      rights: document.getElementById('sub_rights'),
      agreement: document.getElementById('sub_agreement'),
    };
    Object.entries(sections).forEach(([name, panel]) => {
      if (panel) panel.hidden = name !== sectionName;
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-nursing-section]').forEach(button => {
      const active = button.dataset.nursingSection === sectionName;
      button.classList.toggle('active', active);
      button.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(active));
    });
    if (sectionName !== 'care') window.syncRightsForm?.();
  }

  function organizeNursingSections() {
    if (nursingOrganized) return;
    const nursing = document.getElementById('sub_nursing');
    const rights = document.getElementById('sub_rights');
    const agreement = document.getElementById('sub_agreement');
    if (!nursing || !rights || !agreement) return;
    nursingOrganized = true;

    const care = document.createElement('div');
    care.id = 'nursing-care-section';
    while (nursing.firstChild) care.append(nursing.firstChild);

    rights.classList.remove('sub-tab', 'active');
    agreement.classList.remove('sub-tab', 'active');
    rights.classList.add('nursing-auxiliary-panel');
    agreement.classList.add('nursing-auxiliary-panel');

    const tabs = document.createElement('div');
    tabs.className = 'nursing-section-tabs no-print';
    tabs.setAttribute('role', 'tablist');
    [
      ['care', 'fa-user-nurse', 'care'],
      ['rights', 'fa-scale-balanced', 'rights'],
      ['agreement', 'fa-file-contract', 'agreement'],
    ].forEach(([key, icon, labelKey]) => {
      const button = document.createElement('button');
      button.type = 'button';
      button.className = 'inner-tab';
      button.dataset.nursingSection = key;
      button.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid ${icon}" aria-hidden="true"></i><span></span>`;
      button.querySelector('span').textContent = t(labelKey);
      button.addEventListener('click', () => switchNursingSection(key));
      tabs.append(button);
    });
    nursing.append(tabs, care, rights, agreement);
    switchNursingSection('care');
  }

  function historyFieldLabel(fieldId) {
    const control = document.getElementById(fieldId);
    if (!control) return fieldId.replace(/^web_/, '').replace(/_/g, ' ');
    const container = control.closest('.input-group, .form-group, .modern-grid > div, td, .opd-field, .ipd-field') || control.parentElement;
    const label = container?.querySelector?.('label, .label-text, [data-key]');
    return String(label?.textContent || control.getAttribute('aria-label') || fieldId).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
  }

  function historyValue(value) {
    if (value === true) return '✓';
    if (value === false || value === null || value === undefined) return '';
    return String(value).trim();
  }

  function encounterSections(record) {
    const sections = { registration: [], triage: [], medical: [], nursing: [] };
    Object.entries(record.data || {}).forEach(([key, stored]) => {
      const match = /^scope:(registration|triage|medical|nursing):id:(.+)$/.exec(key);
      if (!match) return;
      const value = historyValue(stored);
      if (!value) return;
      sections[match[1]].push({ label: historyFieldLabel(match[2]), value });
    });
    try {
      const daily = JSON.parse(record.data?.['system:dailyMonitoringRecords'] || '[]');
      if (Array.isArray(daily) && daily.length) sections.medical.push({ label: 'កំណត់ត្រាតាមដានប្រចាំថ្ងៃ', value: `${daily.length} ថ្ងៃ` });
    } catch {}
    return sections;
  }

  function historySectionHtml(title, rows) {
    if (!rows.length) return '';
    return `<details class="patient-history-section"><summary>${escapeHtml(title)} <span>${rows.length}</span></summary><div class="patient-history-fields">${rows.map(row => `<div><strong>${escapeHtml(row.label)}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(row.value)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div></details>`;
  }

  async function openPatientHistory() {
    const patientKey = loadedPatient?.patientKey || new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('patient') || patientContext().key;
    if (!patientKey) { alert(language() === 'khmer' ? 'សូមបើកអ្នកជំងឺជាមុនសិន។' : 'សូមបើកអ្នកជំងឺជាមុនសិន។'); return; }
    const overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className = 'patient-history-overlay';
    overlay.innerHTML = '<section class="patient-history-dialog"><header><div><h2>ប្រវត្តិព្យាបាលទាំងមូល</h2><p>តម្រៀបកំណត់ត្រាសម្រាកពេទ្យ និងពិគ្រោះក្រៅតាមកាលបរិច្ឆេទ</p></div><button type="button" data-close>×</button></header><div class="patient-history-content"><div class="patient-history-loading">កំពុងទាញប្រវត្តិ…</div></div></section>';
    document.body.append(overlay);
    const close = () => overlay.remove();
    overlay.querySelector('[data-close]').onclick = close;
    overlay.addEventListener('click', event => { if (event.target === overlay) close(); });
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/patient-history?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}`, { credentials:'same-origin', cache:'no-store' });
      const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'មិនអាចបង្ហាញប្រវត្តិបានទេ។');
      const rows = result.history || [];
      const content = overlay.querySelector('.patient-history-content');
      content.innerHTML = rows.map(record => {
        const date = escapeHtml(record.entryDate || new Date(record.createdAt).toLocaleDateString());
        const mode = escapeHtml(String(record.serviceMode || '').toUpperCase() || 'ENCOUNTER');
        const department = escapeHtml(record.departmentNameKh || record.departmentNameEn || '');
        const caseNumber = escapeHtml(record.caseNumber || '—');
        const dischargeDate = record.dischargeDate ? ` · ចេញពីមន្ទីរពេទ្យ: ${escapeHtml(record.dischargeDate)}` : '';
        const sections = encounterSections(record);
        return `<article class="patient-history-encounter"><header><div><span class="patient-history-date">${date}</span><strong>${mode} · ${department}</strong><small>លេខករណី: ${caseNumber}${dischargeDate}</small></div><a href="/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(record.patientKey)}${record.serviceMode==='ipd'?'&view=medical':''}" target="_blank">បើកកំណត់ត្រា</a></header>${historySectionHtml('ព័ត៌មានចុះឈ្មោះ', sections.registration)}${historySectionHtml('Triage', sections.triage)}${historySectionHtml('កំណត់ត្រាវេជ្ជសាស្ត្រ', sections.medical)}${historySectionHtml('ឯកសារថែទាំ', sections.nursing)}</article>`;
      }).join('') || '<div class="patient-history-loading">មិនមានប្រវត្តិពីមុនទេ។</div>';
    } catch (error) {
      overlay.querySelector('.patient-history-content').innerHTML = `<div class="patient-history-error">${escapeHtml(error.message)}</div>`;
    }
  }

  function refreshLanguage() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.emr-save-button span').forEach(node => { node.textContent = t('save'); });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-nursing-section]').forEach(button => {
      const label = button.querySelector('span');
      if (label) label.textContent = t(button.dataset.nursingSection);
    });
    const notice = document.getElementById('emr-care-route-notice');
    if (notice) notice.textContent = t(notice.dataset.mode === 'opd' ? 'opdOnly' : 'ipdOnly');
  }

  function organizeOpdHeader() {
    const host = document.getElementById('sub_opd');
    const header = document.getElementById('opd-header-patient-card');
    if (!host || !header || !host.contains(header)) return;
    // Keep this one identity header visible even when a specialty form is selected.
    host.insertBefore(header, host.querySelector(':scope > .opd-module-toolbar') || host.firstChild);
  }

  function initialize() {
    prepareStorageKeys();
    organizeNursingSections();
    ensureAdmissionApprovalPanel();
    applyTriageWorkspaceRestrictions();
    renderRecordSignatures();

    const pageOne = document.getElementById('page1');
    const pageTwo = document.getElementById('page2');
    const pageThreeTabs = document.querySelector('#page3 > .inner-tabs');
    const pageThree = document.getElementById('page3');
    // Page 2 Triage save button is located at the bottom action-bar next to btn-next; no separate top Save bar.
    document.querySelectorAll('#page2 > .emr-save-bar').forEach(el => el.remove());
    const pageTwoActionBar = document.querySelector('#page2 .action-bar');
    const pageTwoBtnNext = document.querySelector('#page2 .action-bar .btn-next');
    if (pageTwoActionBar && pageTwoBtnNext && !pageTwoActionBar.querySelector('.emr-save-button')) {
      pageTwoBtnNext.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', createSaveButton());
    }
    organizeOpdHeader();
    ['sub_opd', 'sub_ipd', 'sub_nursing'].forEach(id => addSaveBar(document.getElementById(id), id === 'sub_opd' ? 'beforeend' : 'afterbegin'));
    document.querySelectorAll('#sub_ipd > .emr-save-bar').forEach(el => el.remove());
    const ipdActionBar = document.querySelector('#sub_ipd > .action-bar');
    if (ipdActionBar) {
      ipdActionBar.querySelectorAll('.emr-save-button, .btn-pmrs2-preview').forEach(el => el.remove());
      const saveBtn = createSaveButton();
      const saveBtnText = saveBtn.querySelector('span');
      if (saveBtnText) saveBtnText.textContent = 'រក្សាទុក និងបញ្ចប់កំណត់ត្រាវេជ្ជសាស្ត្រ';
      ipdActionBar.append(saveBtn);
      const preview = document.createElement('button');
      preview.type = 'button';
      preview.className = 'btn-blue btn-pmrs2-preview no-print';
      preview.textContent = 'ពិនិត្យទិន្នន័យ PMRS2';
      preview.addEventListener('click', () => void showPmrs2FollowUpPreview());
      ipdActionBar.append(preview);
    }
    updateIpdObGynVisibility();
    document.querySelectorAll('input[name="sex_radio"]').forEach(radio => {
      radio.addEventListener('change', updateIpdObGynVisibility);
    });
    const opdBar = document.querySelector('#sub_opd > .emr-save-bar');
    if (opdBar) {
      const submit = document.createElement('button');
      submit.type='button'; submit.className='btn-blue'; submit.hidden=true;
      submit.textContent='រក្សាទុក និងផ្ញើវេជ្ជបញ្ជាទៅឱសថស្ថាន';
      submit.addEventListener('click',async()=>{submit.disabled=true;try{await savePatientRecord({submitOpdPrescription:true,markMedicalComplete:true});}finally{submit.disabled=false;}});
      const show = user => {submit.hidden=user?.activeRole !== 'DOCTOR';};
      show(window.emrSession?.user);
      window.addEventListener('emr-session-ready',event=>show(event.detail?.user));
      opdBar.append(submit);
    }
    ['sub_opd', 'sub_ipd'].forEach(id => {
      const saveBar = document.querySelector(`#${id} > .emr-save-bar`);
      if (!saveBar) return;
      const preview = document.createElement('button');
      preview.type = 'button';
      preview.className = 'btn-blue';
      preview.textContent = 'ពិនិត្យទិន្នន័យ PMRS2';
      preview.addEventListener('click', () => void showPmrs2FollowUpPreview());
      saveBar.append(preview);
    });

    if (pageThreeTabs && !document.getElementById('emr-care-route-notice')) {
      const notice = document.createElement('div');
      notice.id = 'emr-care-route-notice';
      notice.className = 'emr-care-route-notice no-print';
      notice.hidden = true;
      pageThreeTabs.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', notice);
    }
    if (pageThree && !document.getElementById('emr-record-status')) {
      const status = document.createElement('div');
      status.id = 'emr-record-status';
      status.className = 'emr-record-status no-print';
      status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      status.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      const statusAnchor = document.getElementById('patient-record-workspace-navigation') ||
        document.querySelector('.step-wrapper');
      statusAnchor?.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', status);
    }

    let languageRefreshPending = false;
    const scheduleLanguageRefresh = () => {
      if (languageRefreshPending) return;
      languageRefreshPending = true;
      setTimeout(() => { languageRefreshPending = false; refreshLanguage(); }, 0);
    };
    document.getElementById('langBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (typeof window.setBorkeoLanguage !== 'function') scheduleLanguageRefresh();
    });
    window.addEventListener('borkeo-language-change', scheduleLanguageRefresh);
    document.getElementById('btn_view_patient_history')?.addEventListener('click', () => void openPatientHistory());
    document.addEventListener('emr:patient-saved', event => {
      if (event.detail?.patient) { loadedPatient = event.detail.patient; renderRecordSignatures(); }
    });
    window.addEventListener('emr-session-ready', () => { renderRecordSignatures(); void loadCurrentSessionSignature(); applyTriageWorkspaceRestrictions(); void refreshAdmissionApprovalPanel(); });
    if (window.emrSession?.user) void loadCurrentSessionSignature();
    document.addEventListener('change', event => {
      if (event.target.matches('.dept-check, #p2_other_dept')) configureTreatmentWorkflow();
    });

    const parameters = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const requestedPatient = parameters.get('patient');
    if (requestedPatient) void loadPatientRecord(requestedPatient);
    else if (parameters.get('newVisitFrom')) void loadNewRegistrationVisit(parameters.get('newVisitFrom'));
    else {
      const names = parameters.get('name');
      if (names) {
        const parts = String(names).trim().split(/\s+/);
        const first = document.getElementById('web_first_name');
        const last = document.getElementById('web_last_name');
        if (first) first.value = parts.pop() || '';
        if (last) last.value = parts.join(' ');
      }
      const phone = parameters.get('phone');
      if (phone) {
        const phoneEl = document.getElementById('web_phone');
        if (phoneEl) phoneEl.value = phone;
      }
      document.getElementById('web_adm_no')?.focus();
      if (typeof window.applyAutoCaseNumber === 'function') window.applyAutoCaseNumber();
      if (typeof window.applyAutoHealthId === 'function') window.applyAutoHealthId();
    }
    setupPhoneValidation();
    configureTreatmentWorkflow();
  }

  function setupPhoneValidation() {
    function bindPhoneInput(inputEl, errorEl, isRequired) {
      if (!inputEl) return;
      const getLang = () => document.documentElement.lang === 'en' ? 'en' : 'km';

      inputEl.addEventListener('input', () => {
        const val = inputEl.value.trim();
        if (!val) {
          if (!isRequired) {
            inputEl.classList.remove('field-missing');
            if (errorEl) {
              errorEl.textContent = '';
              errorEl.style.display = 'none';
            }
          }
          return;
        }
        if (typeof window.validateCambodianPhone === 'function') {
          const res = window.validateCambodianPhone(val, getLang());
          if (res.valid) {
            inputEl.classList.remove('field-missing');
            if (errorEl) {
              errorEl.textContent = '';
              errorEl.style.display = 'none';
            }
          }
        }
      });

      inputEl.addEventListener('blur', () => {
        const val = inputEl.value.trim();
        if (!val) {
          if (isRequired) {
            inputEl.classList.add('field-missing');
            if (errorEl) {
              errorEl.textContent = getLang() === 'en' ? 'Caregiver phone number is required.' : 'សូមបញ្ចូលលេខទូរសព្ទអ្នកកំដរ';
              errorEl.style.display = 'block';
            }
          } else {
            inputEl.classList.remove('field-missing');
            if (errorEl) {
              errorEl.textContent = '';
              errorEl.style.display = 'none';
            }
          }
          return;
        }

        if (typeof window.validateCambodianPhone === 'function') {
          const res = window.validateCambodianPhone(val, getLang());
          if (!res.valid) {
            inputEl.classList.add('field-missing');
            if (errorEl) {
              errorEl.textContent = res.error;
              errorEl.style.display = 'block';
            }
          } else {
            inputEl.classList.remove('field-missing');
            if (errorEl) {
              errorEl.textContent = '';
              errorEl.style.display = 'none';
            }
            inputEl.value = res.normalized;
          }
        }
      });
    }

    const patientInput = document.getElementById('web_phone');
    const patientErr = document.getElementById('web_phone_error');
    const caregiverInput = document.getElementById('web_caregiver_phone');
    const caregiverErr = document.getElementById('web_caregiver_phone_error');

    bindPhoneInput(patientInput, patientErr, false);
    bindPhoneInput(caregiverInput, caregiverErr, true);

    window.addEventListener('borkeo-language-change', () => {
      const getLang = () => document.documentElement.lang === 'en' ? 'en' : 'km';
      if (patientErr && patientErr.style.display !== 'none' && patientInput?.value?.trim()) {
        const res = window.validateCambodianPhone?.(patientInput.value.trim(), getLang());
        if (res && !res.valid) patientErr.textContent = res.error;
      }
      if (caregiverErr && caregiverErr.style.display !== 'none' && caregiverInput?.value?.trim()) {
        const res = window.validateCambodianPhone?.(caregiverInput.value.trim(), getLang());
        if (res && !res.valid) caregiverErr.textContent = res.error;
      }
    });
  }

  window.getEmrCarePath = getEmrCarePath;
  window.configureTreatmentWorkflow = configureTreatmentWorkflow;
  window.savePatientRecord = savePatientRecord;
  window.ensureFirstExamDateTime = ensureFirstExamDateTime;
  window.printSignedOpdPrescription = () => printSignedOpd(() => window.printPrescription('opd'));
  window.printSignedOpdForm = () => printSignedOpd(() => window.printTriageOpdRx());
  window.loadPatientRecord = loadPatientRecord;
  window.switchNursingSection = switchNursingSection;
  window.getLoadedEmrPatient = () => loadedPatient;
  window.openPatientHistory = openPatientHistory;
  window.getAdmissionDoctorSignature = getAdmissionDoctorSignature;
  window.renderRecordSignatures = renderRecordSignatures;
  window.currentDoctorIdentitySignature = currentDoctorIdentitySignature;
  window.signatureHtml = signatureHtml;

  function syncOccupationField(sel) {
    const otherInp = sel.parentElement?.querySelector('#reg_occupation_other, #reg_occupation_other_legacy');
    if (!otherInp) return;
    if (sel.value === 'ផ្សេងៗ') {
      otherInp.style.display = 'block';
      otherInp.focus();
    } else {
      otherInp.style.display = 'none';
      otherInp.value = '';
    }
    if (typeof window.syncFields === 'function') window.syncFields();
  }
  function onOccupationOtherInput(inp) {
    if (typeof window.syncFields === 'function') window.syncFields();
  }
  window.syncOccupationField = syncOccupationField;
  window.onOccupationOtherInput = onOccupationOtherInput;

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initialize);
  } else {
    initialize();
  }
})();


  // Reset triage edit mode on patient load
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded', function resetTriageEditStateOnLoad() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('triage_save_btn');
    if (btn) delete btn.dataset.userEditing;
    if (typeof updateTriageSaveButtonState === 'function') updateTriageSaveButtonState();
  });
===== END FILE: public/emr-patient-record.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: public/emr-required-fields.js =====
'use strict';

(function requiredFieldsForEveryWorkspace() {
  const installed = new WeakSet();
  const controls = 'input:not([type="button"]):not([type="submit"]):not([type="reset"]),select,textarea,[contenteditable="true"]';
  const star = /[*＊✱∗]/u;
  const message = 'សូមបំពេញព័ត៌មានដែលមានសញ្ញា * ឱ្យបានគ្រប់ មុនបន្ត។';
  const marked = new WeakMap();

  function visible(element) {
    if (!element || element.closest('[hidden],[inert],dialog:not([open])')) return false;
    const frame = element.ownerDocument.defaultView.frameElement;
    if (frame && !visible(frame)) return false;
    const style = element.ownerDocument.defaultView.getComputedStyle(element);
    return style.display !== 'none' && style.visibility !== 'hidden' && style.visibility !== 'collapse' && element.getClientRects().length > 0;
  }

  function visualControl(field) {
    const selector = field.getAttribute('data-required-proxy');
    if (selector) { try { return field.ownerDocument.querySelector(selector); } catch { return null; } }
    if (field.tagName === 'SELECT' && field.closest('.emr-nat-wrapper')) {
      return field.closest('.emr-nat-wrapper').querySelector('.emr-nat-btn');
    }
    return field.type === 'hidden' ? null : field;
  }

  function applicable(field) {
    return !field.matches(':disabled') && !field.closest('[inert]') && visible(visualControl(field));
  }

  function fieldList(root) {
    if (root.tagName === 'FORM') return Array.from(root.elements).filter(field => field.matches(controls));
    return [ ...(root.matches?.(controls) ? [root] : []), ...root.querySelectorAll(controls) ].filter(field => {
      const island = field.closest('[data-required-island]');
      return !island || island === root || island.contains(root);
    });
  }

  // Resolve visual asterisks at action time, so dynamic and conditional forms
  // need no document-wide mutation observer or persistent required rewrites.
  function fieldsForLabel(label) {
    const ids = label.getAttribute('data-required-for');
    if (ids) return ids.split(/\s+/).map(id => label.ownerDocument.getElementById(id)).filter(Boolean);
    if (label.control) return [label.control];
    const nested = fieldList(label);
    if (nested.length) return nested;
    const group = label.closest('.input-group,.form-group,.form-field,.field,td');
    if (group) return fieldList(group);
    // A sibling container may hold a radio group or a custom select. Stop at
    // the next label instead of marking unrelated fields in the same row.
    let sibling = label.nextElementSibling;
    while (sibling && !sibling.matches('label,legend,.field-label,.required-star')) {
      const fields = fieldList(sibling);
      if (fields.length) return fields;
      sibling = sibling.nextElementSibling;
    }
    return [];
  }

  function requiredGroups(scope) {
    const groups = [];
    const scopedFields = fieldList(scope);
    const within = new Set(scopedFields);
    for (const field of scopedFields.filter(field => field.matches('[required],[aria-required="true"],[data-emr-required]'))) {
      if (field.matches(controls) && applicable(field)) groups.push([field]);
    }
    const labels = new Set([...scope.querySelectorAll('label,legend,.field-label,.required-star,[data-required-for],span,sup'),
      ...scopedFields.flatMap(field => Array.from(field.labels || []))]);
    for (const label of labels) {
      if (!visible(label) || !star.test(label.textContent)) continue;
      const caption = label.cloneNode(true);
      caption.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea,button,[contenteditable="true"],.emr-nat-popover').forEach(value => value.remove());
      if (!star.test(caption.textContent)) continue;
      // Plain spans qualify only as a standalone star. This excludes clinical
      // footnotes, risk legends and headings that happen to contain an asterisk.
      if (!label.matches('label,legend,.field-label,.required-star,[data-required-for]') && !/^[*＊✱∗]$/u.test(label.textContent.trim())) continue;
      if (label.closest('label,.field-label,[data-required-for]') !== label && label.closest('label,.field-label,[data-required-for]')) continue;
      const fields = fieldsForLabel(label).filter(field => within.has(field) && applicable(field));
      if (fields.length) groups.push(fields);
    }
    return groups;
  }

  function filled(field) {
    if (field.type === 'checkbox' || field.type === 'radio') return field.checked;
    if (field.type === 'file') return Boolean(field.files?.length);
    if (field.tagName === 'SELECT' && field.multiple) return Array.from(field.selectedOptions).some(option => option.value.trim());
    const value = field.isContentEditable ? field.textContent : field.value;
    return String(value ?? '').replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, '').trim().length > 0;
  }

  function clearField(field) {
    const visual = visualControl(field);
    if (!visual || !marked.has(visual)) return;
    const previous = marked.get(visual);
    if (previous === null) visual.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
    else visual.setAttribute('aria-invalid', previous);
    visual.classList.remove('emr-required-missing');
    marked.delete(visual);
  }

  function validate(scope) {
    if (!scope) return true;
    fieldList(scope).forEach(clearField);
    const missing = new Set();
    for (const fields of requiredGroups(scope)) {
      const choices = fields.every(field => field.type === 'radio' || field.type === 'checkbox');
      if (choices && fields.length > 1) {
        if (!fields.some(filled)) missing.add(fields[0]);
        continue;
      }
      for (const field of fields) {
        if (field.type === 'radio' && field.name) {
          const peers = fieldList(scope).filter(peer => peer.type === 'radio' && peer.name === field.name && peer.form === field.form && applicable(peer));
          if (!peers.some(filled)) missing.add(field);
        } else if (!filled(field)) missing.add(field);
      }
    }
    const existing = scope.querySelector(':scope > [data-emr-required-message]');
    if (!missing.size) {
      existing?.remove();
      for (const frame of scope.querySelectorAll('iframe')) {
        if (!visible(frame)) continue;
        let frameDoc;
        try { frameDoc = frame.contentDocument; } catch { continue; }
        if (frameDoc?.body && !validate(frameDoc.body)) return false;
      }
      return true;
    }
    for (const field of missing) {
      const visual = visualControl(field);
      if (!marked.has(visual)) marked.set(visual, visual.getAttribute('aria-invalid'));
      visual.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
      visual.classList.add('emr-required-missing');
    }
    const banner = existing || scope.ownerDocument.createElement('p');
    banner.setAttribute('data-emr-required-message', '');
    banner.setAttribute('data-emr-transient', '');
    banner.setAttribute('data-emr-no-auto-index', '');
    banner.setAttribute('role', 'alert');
    banner.textContent = message;
    if (!existing) scope.prepend(banner);
    const firstField = Array.from(missing).sort((a, b) => a === b ? 0 : (a.compareDocumentPosition(b) & 4 ? -1 : 1))[0];
    const first = visualControl(firstField);
    first.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'center', behavior: 'auto' });
    first.focus?.({ preventScroll: true });
    return false;
  }

  function scopeFor(action) {
    const doc = action.ownerDocument;
    const explicit = action.getAttribute('data-required-scope');
    if (explicit) { try { const scope = doc.querySelector(explicit); if (scope) return scope; } catch {} }
    return action.form || action.closest('[data-required-island]') || action.closest('form,dialog,[role="dialog"],.modal-content') ||
      action.closest('.sub-tab,.page') || action.closest('section,.panel,.card,main') || doc.body;
  }

  function bypass(action) {
    if (!action) return false;
    const label = (action.innerText || action.value || '').trim();
    return action.matches('[formnovalidate],[data-required-bypass],[type="reset"],[data-close],[data-cancel],.dialog-close,.modal-close,.btn-close') || /^[×✕✖]$/u.test(label) ||
      /^(?:បោះបង់|បិទ|ត្រឡប់|ថយក្រោយ|កែប្រែ|កែសម្រួល|បោះពុម្ព|មើល|ស្វែងរក|ផ្ទុកឡើងវិញ|cancel\b|close\b|back\b|discard\b|edit\b|print\b|preview\b|search\b|refresh\b|open\b)/iu.test(label);
  }

  function commits(action) {
    if (bypass(action)) return false;
    if (action.matches('[data-required-action],[data-save],[data-release],[data-submit],[data-send],[data-approve],[data-transfer],[data-confirm],.emr-save-button')) return true;
    if (action.form && (action.type === 'submit' || (action.tagName === 'BUTTON' && !action.hasAttribute('type')))) return true;
    const signal = [action.getAttribute('onclick'), action.id, action.getAttribute('data-action'), action.innerText, action.value].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    return /(?:រក្សាទុក|បញ្ជូន|ផ្ញើ|អនុម័ត|យល់ព្រម|បញ្ជាក់|ស្នើសុំ|\b(?:save|submit|send|approve|transfer|confirm)\b)/iu.test(signal) ||
      /\b(?:save|submit|send|approve|transfer)[A-Z_]/u.test(signal);
  }

  function block(event) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation(); }

  function install(doc) {
    if (!doc?.defaultView || installed.has(doc)) return;
    installed.add(doc);
    const win = doc.defaultView;
    if (win.BorkeoRequiredFields?.version === '37.7') return;
    win.BorkeoRequiredFields = { validate, scopeFor, version: '37.7' };
    if (doc !== document && doc.head) {
      const style = doc.createElement('link');
      style.rel = 'stylesheet'; style.href = '/public/emr-required-fields.css?v=3770';
      doc.head.append(style);
    }
    // Window capture runs before inline handlers and document-delegated saves.
    win.addEventListener('click', event => {
      const action = event.target.closest?.('button,input[type="submit"],input[type="button"],[role="button"],a[data-required-action],a[onclick]');
      if (action && bypass(action) && action.form && action.type === 'submit' && !action.formNoValidate) {
        action.formNoValidate = true;
        win.setTimeout(() => { action.formNoValidate = false; }, 0);
      }
      if (action && commits(action) && !validate(scopeFor(action))) block(event);
    }, true);
    win.addEventListener('submit', event => {
      if (!bypass(event.submitter) && !validate(event.target)) block(event);
    }, true);
    doc.addEventListener('invalid', event => {
      if (applicable(event.target) && (event.target.validity.valueMissing || !filled(event.target))) {
        event.preventDefault(); validate(scopeFor(event.target));
      }
    }, true);
    doc.addEventListener('input', event => clearField(event.target), true);
    doc.addEventListener('change', event => clearField(event.target), true);
    const proto = win.HTMLFormElement.prototype;
    const nativeSubmit = proto.submit;
    proto.submit = function guardedSubmit() {
      if (validate(this)) return nativeSubmit.call(this);
    };
    function frameLoaded(frame) { try { install(frame.contentDocument); } catch {} }
    doc.addEventListener('load', event => { if (event.target?.tagName === 'IFRAME') frameLoaded(event.target); }, true);
    const scanFrames = () => doc.querySelectorAll('iframe').forEach(frameLoaded);
    if (doc.readyState === 'loading') doc.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', scanFrames, { once: true });
    else scanFrames();
  }

  install(document);
})();
===== END FILE: public/emr-required-fields.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: public/emr-shared-encounter.js =====
'use strict';
(function sharedEncounter() {
  const pairs = { opd_weight: 'p2_weight', opd_height: 'p2_height', opd_temp: 'p2_temp', opd_bp_sys: 'p2_bp_sys', opd_bp_dia: 'p2_bp_dia', opd_pulse: 'p2_pulse', opd_resp: 'p2_resp', opd_spo2: 'p2_spo2' };
  const dirty = new Map();
  let home = null, sharedHost = null, ready = false;
  const isOpd = () => ['opd', 'opd-paraclinic'].includes(document.body.dataset.encounterView);
  const doctor = () => ['DOCTOR', 'CHIEF_WARD', 'WARD_HEAD', 'ADMIN', 'DIRECTOR', 'VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole);
  const writable = () => ready && document.body.dataset.encounterView === 'opd' && doctor() && window.EmrEncounterEditor?.canWrite() === true &&
    (['ADMIN', 'DIRECTOR', 'VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole) || ['DOCTOR_READY', 'IN_CONSULTATION'].includes(window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.()?.workflowStage));
  function apply() {
    if (!window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.()) return;
    const widget = document.getElementById('chief_complaint_tag_box');
    const dropdown = document.getElementById('chief_complaint_dropdown');
    if (widget && dropdown) {
      if (!home) { home = document.createComment('shared chief complaint home'); widget.before(home); }
      if (isOpd()) {
        const subjective = document.querySelector('#sub_opd [data-soap-record-box="s"]');
        if (subjective && !sharedHost) {
          sharedHost = document.createElement('div'); sharedHost.className = 'opd-shared-complaint';
          const label = document.createElement('label'); label.textContent = 'សញ្ញាតម្អូញចម្បង'; label.htmlFor = widget.id;
          const printed = document.createElement('span'); printed.className = 'opd-shared-complaint-print';
          sharedHost.append(label, printed); subjective.querySelector('.emr-soap-header')?.after(sharedHost);
        }
        if (sharedHost && widget.parentElement !== sharedHost) sharedHost.append(widget, dropdown);
      } else if (home.parentElement && widget.parentElement === sharedHost) home.after(widget, dropdown);
    }
    const resp = document.getElementById('opd_resp');
    if (resp && !document.getElementById('opd_spo2')) {
      const label = document.createElement('label'); label.textContent = ' SpO2 ';
      const input = document.createElement('input'); input.id = 'opd_spo2'; input.type = 'text'; input.className = 'fill-blank';
      input.setAttribute('data-emr-transient', ''); input.setAttribute('data-emr-no-auto-index', ''); input.style.width = '60px';
      label.append(input); resp.parentElement.append(label);
    }
    for (const [targetId, sourceId] of Object.entries(pairs)) {
      const target = document.getElementById(targetId), source = document.getElementById(sourceId);
      if (!target || !source) continue;
      target.dataset.sharedTriageId = sourceId;
      target.readOnly = !writable();
      if (!dirty.has(sourceId)) target.value = source.value;
      if (!target.dataset.sharedBound) {
        target.dataset.sharedBound = '1';
        target.addEventListener('input', () => {
          if (!writable()) return;
          source.value = target.value; dirty.set(sourceId, target.value);
          source.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
        });
      }
    }
    window.BorkeoChiefComplaint?.syncFromInput();
    syncPrint();
  }
  function syncPrint() {
    const printed = sharedHost?.querySelector('.opd-shared-complaint-print');
    if (printed) printed.textContent = document.getElementById('pmrs_chief_complaint')?.value || '';
  }
  document.addEventListener('input', event => {
    if (writable() && ['pmrs_chief_complaint', 'pmrs_chief_complaint_items'].includes(event.target.id)) dirty.set(event.target.id, event.target.value);
    if (event.target.id === 'pmrs_chief_complaint') syncPrint();
  });
  window.EmrSharedEncounter = {
    chiefEditable: () => isOpd() ? writable() : undefined,
    changes: () => {
      if (!writable() || !dirty.size) return undefined;
      const patch = Object.fromEntries(dirty);
      if (dirty.has('pmrs_chief_complaint')) patch.pmrs_chief_complaint_items = document.getElementById('pmrs_chief_complaint_items')?.value || '[]';
      return patch;
    },
    apply,
  };
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loading', () => { ready = false; dirty.clear(); apply(); });
  for (const event of ['emr:patient-loaded', 'emr:patient-saved']) document.addEventListener(event, () => { ready = true; dirty.clear(); setTimeout(apply, 0); });
  window.addEventListener('emr-session-ready', () => setTimeout(apply, 0));
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    new MutationObserver(changes => { if (changes.some(change => change.oldValue !== document.body.dataset.encounterView)) setTimeout(apply, 0); })
      .observe(document.body, { attributes: true, attributeOldValue: true, attributeFilter: ['data-encounter-view'] });
  });
})();
===== END FILE: public/emr-shared-encounter.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: public/maternal-emr.js =====
// v42.6 - Maternal services on the medical record page (EMR.html):
// - the old ANC form (#sub_anc) is read-only (inert); the official ANC record
//   (/anc-record) replaces it;
// - the midwife's medicine search shows only the medicines of the maternal
//   services (public/medicine-autocomplete.js BorkeoMedicineAllowedNames);
// - cervical screening: the midwife sends the result to the Gynecology ward
//   doctors' inbox (POST /api/maternal/cervix-result); the doctor's
//   acknowledgement completes the message.
// The send button carries data-required-bypass: its label contains ផ្ញើ, so
// emr-required-fields.js would otherwise read it as a record save and validate
// every required field of the whole OPD tab before the click. This panel saves
// nothing into the record (its fields are data-emr-transient); it checks its own
// result field in sendCervix, and the server checks the role.
'use strict';
(function () {
  const esc = (value) => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
  const english = () => window.getBorkeoLanguage?.() === 'en';
  const t = (key, en) => (english() ? en : (window.newText ? window.newText(key, en) : en));
  const role = () => String(window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole || '').toUpperCase();
  const patient = () => window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.() || null;
  let options = null;

  async function loadOptions() {
    if (options) return options;
    const response = await fetch('/api/maternal/config', { credentials: 'same-origin', cache: 'no-store' });
    options = response.ok ? (await response.json()).options : {};
    return options;
  }

  // The old ANC form opens read-only (saved values stay visible).
  function oldAncReadOnly() {
    const panel = document.getElementById('sub_anc');
    if (!panel || panel.dataset.readOnly === '1') return;
    panel.dataset.readOnly = '1';
    // inert: nothing can be typed or ticked, and the saved values are still read
    // and saved unchanged with the record (disabled controls could be skipped).
    panel.inert = true;
    panel.setAttribute('data-anc-old-readonly', '');
    const notice = document.createElement('p');
    notice.className = 'anc-old-notice no-print';
    const key = patient()?.patientKey || new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('patient') || '';
    notice.innerHTML = `${esc(t('anc.oldForm', 'Old ANC form (read only). Use the official ANC record.'))} ${key ? `<a href="/anc-record?patient=${encodeURIComponent(key)}">${esc(t('anc.open', 'Open the ANC record'))}</a>` : ''}`;
    panel.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', notice);
  }

  async function midwifeMedicines() {
    const current = patient();
    const service = String(current?.departmentCode || '').toLowerCase();
    if (role() !== 'MIDWIFE' || current?.serviceMode !== 'opd') { delete window.BorkeoMedicineAllowedNames; return; }
    const settings = await loadOptions();
    if (![...(settings.paidServiceCodes || []), ...(settings.freeServiceCodes || [])].includes(service)) return;
    const names = (settings.midwifeMedicines || []).map((name) => name.toLowerCase());
    window.BorkeoMedicineAllowedNames = () => names;
  }

  // Cervical screening result panel for the midwife.
  function cervixPanel() {
    const current = patient();
    if (role() !== 'MIDWIFE' || String(current?.departmentCode || '').toLowerCase() !== 'cervix-screening') return;
    const host = document.getElementById('opd-specialty-panels') || document.getElementById('sub_opd');
    if (!host || document.getElementById('cervix-result-panel')) return;
    const panel = document.createElement('section');
    panel.id = 'cervix-result-panel';
    panel.className = 'cervix-result-panel no-print';
    const results = [['negative', t('cervix.negative', 'VIA negative')], ['positive', t('cervix.positive', 'VIA positive')], ['suspect', t('cervix.suspect', 'Suspected cancer')]];
    panel.innerHTML = `<h3>${esc(t('cervix.title', 'Screening result for the Gynecology doctor'))}</h3>
      <label>${esc(t('cervix.result', 'Result'))} <select id="cervix-result" data-emr-transient><option value=""></option>${results.map(([value, label]) => `<option value="${value}">${esc(label)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
      <label>${esc(t('cervix.note', 'Note'))} <input id="cervix-note" type="text" maxlength="500" data-emr-transient></label>
      <button type="button" id="cervix-send" data-required-bypass>${esc(t('cervix.send', 'Send to the Gynecology doctors'))}</button>
      <span id="cervix-status" role="status"></span>`;
    host.prepend(panel);
    panel.querySelector('#cervix-send').addEventListener('click', sendCervix);
  }

  async function sendCervix() {
    const select = document.getElementById('cervix-result');
    const status = document.getElementById('cervix-status');
    if (!select?.value) { status.textContent = t('cervix.choose', 'Choose the result.'); return; }
    try {
      const me = await fetch('/api/auth/me', { credentials: 'same-origin', cache: 'no-store' }).then((r) => r.json());
      const response = await fetch('/api/maternal/cervix-result', { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': me.csrfToken },
        body: JSON.stringify({ patientKey: patient()?.patientKey, result: select.selectedOptions[0].textContent, note: document.getElementById('cervix-note').value }) });
      const body = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(body.error || String(response.status));
      status.textContent = t('cervix.sent', 'Sent to the Gynecology doctors.');
      select.dataset.sent = select.value;
    } catch (error) { status.textContent = error.message; }
  }

  // Saving the record also sends a chosen result that was not sent yet.
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-saved', () => {
    const select = document.getElementById('cervix-result');
    if (select?.value && select.dataset.sent !== select.value) sendCervix();
  });

  function apply() {
    oldAncReadOnly();
    midwifeMedicines().catch(() => {});
    cervixPanel();
  }
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded', () => window.setTimeout(apply, 0));
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', oldAncReadOnly, { once: true });
  else oldAncReadOnly();
  window.EmrMaternal = { apply, oldAncReadOnly, sendCervix };
  document.addEventListener('change', event => {
    if (event.target.id !== 'opd-module-select' || event.target.value !== 'anc') return;
    const key = patient()?.patientKey;
    if (key) { event.stopImmediatePropagation(); window.location.assign(`/anc-record?patient=${encodeURIComponent(key)}`); }
  }, true);
})();
===== END FILE: public/maternal-emr.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: public/new-text-data.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: public/responsive-layout.css =====
/* Borkeo EMR v14.3 - responsive layout layer
   Screen-only rules that let the application adapt from small laptops/tablets
   to large monitors without changing print layouts or clinical workflow. */

@media screen {
  *, *::before, *::after { box-sizing: border-box; }
  html, body {
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
    overflow-x: hidden;
  }
  body { min-height: 100vh; }
  img, svg, video, canvas, iframe { max-width: 100%; }
  button, input, select, textarea { max-width: 100%; box-sizing: border-box; }
  main, header, section, article, aside, form, fieldset, .card, .panel,
  [class$="-shell"], [class$="-content"], .container, .layout-container {
    min-width: 0;
    max-width: 100%;
  }
  .table-wrap, .table-scroll, .table-container, .queue-table-wrap,
  .opd-table-wrap, .responsive-table-scroll, .nursing-form-scroll,
  .report-table-wrap, .table-area {
    max-width: 100%;
    overflow-x: auto;
    overscroll-behavior-x: contain;
  }

  /* Keep the primary application shells within the viewport. */
  .home-shell, .patient-shell, .triage-shell, .opd-shell, .expense-shell,
  .finance-shell, .lab-shell, .imaging-shell, .billing-shell,
  .admin-shell, .department-shell, .pharmacy-shell, .ward-shell,
  .context-shell, .shell, .page-shell {
    width: min(100%, calc(100% - 24px));
    margin-inline: auto;
  }

  /* Prevent flex children from forcing the page wider than the viewport. */
  .home-hero, .patient-page-banner, .triage-banner, .opd-heading,
  .expense-banner, .finance-banner, .lab-banner, .imaging-banner,
  .billing-banner, .admin-banner, .department-page-banner, .clinical-appbar,
  .queue-head, .panel-heading, .section-heading, .toolbar, .opd-toolbar,
  .list-toolbar, .inpatient-view-toolbar, .nursing-form-toolbar {
    min-width: 0;
    max-width: 100%;
  }

  .home-hero > *, .patient-page-banner > *, .triage-banner > *,
  .opd-heading > *, .expense-banner > *, .finance-banner > *,
  .clinical-appbar > *, .queue-head > *, .panel-heading > * {
    min-width: 0;
    max-width: 100%;
  }

  /* Common data-entry grids collapse progressively. */
  .modern-grid, .modern-grid-2, .grid-2-col, .grid-3, .grid-4,
  .patient-registration-grid, .monitoring-vitals-grid, .vitals-grid,
  .ipd-body-grid, .discharge-form-grid, .admin-stats, .admin-approval-grid,
  .report-columns, .report-summary-grid, .monthly-report-grid,
  .monthly-service-summary, .total-grid, .day-columns, .expense-grid,
  .content-grid, .patient-summary-demographics, .patient-summary-fields,
  .followup-date-policy-panel {
    min-width: 0;
  }

  /* Long form controls must shrink instead of creating horizontal scrolling. */
  .form-group, .input-group, .filter-grid label, .discharge-form-grid label,
  .patient-registration-grid > *, .modern-grid > *, .modern-grid-2 > *,
  .grid-2-col > *, .grid-3 > *, .grid-4 > *, .vitals-grid > *,
  .monitoring-vitals-grid > * {
    min-width: 0;
    max-width: 100%;
  }

  .page-a4 {
    max-width: 100% !important;
    overflow: visible;
  }
}

===== BEGIN FILE: scripts/code-backup.mjs =====
// Versioned code snapshots only. Restore always creates a separate new folder.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const project = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const run = (args, cwd) => execFileSync('git', args, { cwd, encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true }).trim();
const sha = file => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(fs.readFileSync(file)).digest('hex');
export function safeCodePath(value) {
  const file = value.replaceAll('\\', '/');
  return file && !file.startsWith('/') && !/^[a-z]:/i.test(file) && !file.split('/').includes('..') &&
    !/^\.git(?:\/|$)/i.test(file) && !/(?:^|\/)\.env(?:\.|$)/i.test(file.replace(/^\.env\.example$/, 'reference-example')) && !/password[^/]*\.csv$/i.test(file) &&
    (!file.startsWith('data/') || /^data\/(icd10|gazetteer|health-facility|demo-photos)\//.test(file) || file === 'data/' || file === 'data/.gitkeep');
}
export function createCodeBackup({ cwd = project, destination = path.join(os.homedir(), 'Borkeo_EMR_Code_Backups') } = {}) {
  if (run(['status', '--porcelain', '--untracked-files=no'], cwd)) throw new Error('Commit or preserve tracked changes before taking a version snapshot.');
  const commit = run(['rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd);
  const files = run(['ls-tree', '-r', '--name-only', commit], cwd).split('\n');
  if (!files.every(safeCodePath)) throw new Error('Snapshot refused: tracked patient data or secret path.');
  const version = JSON.parse(run(['show', `${commit}:package.json`], cwd)).version;
  const directory = path.join(destination, `v${version}-${commit.slice(0, 8)}-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`);
  fs.mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true });
  const archive = path.join(directory, 'code.zip');
  run(['archive', '--format=zip', `--output=${archive}`, commit], cwd);
  const manifest = { version, commit, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), sha256: sha(archive), files };
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(directory, 'backup.json'), JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2) + '\n');
  return { directory, ...manifest };
}
export function restoreCodeBackup(directory, { destination = os.tmpdir() } = {}) {
  const manifest = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(directory, 'backup.json'), 'utf8'));
  const archive = path.join(directory, 'code.zip');
  if (sha(archive) !== manifest.sha256 || !Array.isArray(manifest.files) || !manifest.files.every(safeCodePath)) throw new Error('Invalid or altered code backup.');
  const entries = execFileSync('tar', ['-tf', archive], { encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true }).trim().split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean);
  if (!entries.every(safeCodePath)) throw new Error('Unsafe backup archive path.');
  fs.mkdirSync(destination, { recursive: true });
  const restored = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(destination, 'borkeo-restored-code-'));
  execFileSync('tar', ['-xf', archive, '-C', restored], { windowsHide: true });
  return { directory: restored, version: manifest.version, commit: manifest.commit };
}
if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  const [action = 'create', directory] = process.argv.slice(2);
  if (!['create', 'restore'].includes(action) || action === 'restore' && !directory) throw new Error('Use create, or restore <backup-directory>.');
  const result = action === 'create' ? createCodeBackup() : restoreCodeBackup(directory);
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ directory: result.directory, version: result.version, commit: result.commit }, null, 2));
}
===== END FILE: scripts/code-backup.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: server/anc-legacy.mjs =====
import fs from 'node:fs';
export const legacyAncFields = Object.freeze(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL('./catalogues/anc-legacy-fields.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8')));
export const ANC_SUPPLEMENTAL_FIELDS = Object.freeze([32, 34, 43, 44, 45, 47, 49, 51, 52, 53, 54].map(String));
// Only the selected encounter; never migrate another pregnancy by Health ID.
export function readLegacyAnc(row) {
  let stored = {};
  try { stored = JSON.parse(row.record_json || '{}'); } catch { /* empty form */ }
  const entries = [];
  for (const field of legacyAncFields) {
    const value = Object.hasOwn(stored, field.key) ? stored[field.key] : stored[field.legacyKey];
    if (field.type === 'radio') {
      if (value === true || value === 'true') entries.push({ label: field.label, value: field.option });
    } else if (value !== undefined && value !== null && String(value).trim()) entries.push({ label: field.label, value: String(value) });
  }
  return { patientKey: row.patient_key, caseNumber: row.case_number, date: row.entry_date, entries };
}
export function cleanAncSupplemental(details) {
  if (!details || typeof details !== 'object' || Array.isArray(details)) throw new Error('Invalid ANC encounter details.');
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(details).map(([key, value]) => {
    if (!ANC_SUPPLEMENTAL_FIELDS.includes(key) || typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > 2000) throw new Error('Invalid ANC encounter detail.');
    return [key, value];
  }));
}

===== BEGIN FILE: server/maternal.mjs =====
// v42.6 - Maternal services seen by the midwife (Registration -> Triage ->
// Cashier -> Midwife; vaccination is free and goes straight to the midwife):
// ANC with the official Ministry of Health ANC record (one record per
// pregnancy, linked to the Health ID), PNC, family planning, cervical cancer
// screening and vaccination. Safe abortion goes to the Gynecology ward.
// Everything here is additive: new tables only; old ANC forms are not changed.
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import { ancStatus, DEFAULT_OPTIONS, gestationalAge, datingEdd } from '../public/anc-engine.mjs';
import { signatureSnapshot } from './esign.mjs';
import { createSystemMessage } from './messages.mjs';
import { listStock, syncMedicineStock } from './stock.mjs';
import { sameMedicineUnit } from './medicine-units.mjs';
import { medicineCatalogue } from './medicine-catalogue.mjs';
import { listOpdQueue, opdLocalDate } from './opd-queue.mjs';
import { cleanAncSupplemental } from './anc-legacy.mjs';

const readJson = (file) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL(file, import.meta.url), 'utf8'));
let cachedChart = null, cachedSchedule = null, cachedLabels = null;
export const ancColourChart = () => (cachedChart ||= readJson('./catalogues/anc-color-chart.json'));
export const ancVisitSchedule = () => (cachedSchedule ||= readJson('./catalogues/anc-visit-schedule.json'));
export const ancRecordLabels = () => (cachedLabels ||= readJson('./catalogues/anc-record-labels.json'));

// Owner defaults (server/clinical-options.json "maternal"), read at each use so a change applies after saving the file.
export function maternalOptions() {
  let options = {};
  try { options = readJsonFresh('./clinical-options.json').maternal || {}; } catch { options = {}; }
  return {
    ...DEFAULT_OPTIONS,
    paidServiceCodes: ['anc', 'pnc', 'family-planning', 'cervix-screening'],
    freeServiceCodes: ['vaccination'],
    gynecologyOutpatientCodes: ['safe-abortion'],
    midwifeLabTests: ['HBSAG'],
    midwifeImaging: ['ULTRASOUND'],
    midwifeMedicines: ['Ferrous salt', 'Ferrous salt + folic acid', 'Folic acid', 'Mebendazole', 'Calcium'],
    ironPrescriptionMedicine: 'Ferrous salt + folic acid',
    tetanusVaccineName: 'Tetanus toxoid vaccine',
    taskCloseReasons: ['មិនមានតេស្ត', 'អ្នកជំងឺបដិសេធ', 'ធ្វើរួចនៅកន្លែងផ្សេង', 'ផ្សេងៗ'],
    cervicalResultWard: 'gynecology',
    cervicalResultRole: 'DOCTOR',
    referralSignRoles: ['DOCTOR', 'CHIEF_WARD'],
    referralSignWards: ['obstetric', 'gynecology'],
    ...options,
  };
}

function readJsonFresh(file) {
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL(file, import.meta.url), 'utf8'));
}

const parse = (value) => { try { return JSON.parse(value || '{}'); } catch { return {}; } };
const role = (session) => String(session?.activeRole || session?.active_role || session?.role || '').toUpperCase();
const staffName = (session) => session?.display_name || session?.displayName || session?.username || '';
const code = (row) => String(row?.department_code || '').toLowerCase();

export function isMaternalService(row, options = maternalOptions()) {
  return String(row?.service_mode || '').toLowerCase() === 'opd'
    && [...options.paidServiceCodes, ...options.freeServiceCodes].includes(code(row));
}
export const isFreeMaternalService = (row, options = maternalOptions()) => String(row?.service_mode || '').toLowerCase() === 'opd' && options.freeServiceCodes.includes(code(row));
export const isGynecologyOutpatient = (row, options = maternalOptions()) => String(row?.service_mode || '').toLowerCase() === 'opd' && options.gynecologyOutpatientCodes.includes(code(row));
// Seen by the midwife or the Gynecology ward, not by the OPD doctor.
export const isNotOpdDoctorPatient = (row) => isMaternalService(row) || isGynecologyOutpatient(row);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- schema
export function ensureMaternalSchema(database) {
  database.sqlite.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS anc_pregnancies (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, patient_key TEXT NOT NULL, health_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('open','delivered','referred','closed')),
    record_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', colour TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    opened_at INTEGER NOT NULL, opened_by_id TEXT NOT NULL, opened_by_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    closed_at INTEGER, closed_by_name TEXT, close_reason TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
  ) STRICT;
  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS anc_pregnancies_health_idx ON anc_pregnancies(health_id, status);
  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS anc_pregnancies_patient_idx ON anc_pregnancies(patient_key, status);
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS anc_visits (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, pregnancy_id TEXT NOT NULL, patient_key TEXT NOT NULL, visit_date TEXT NOT NULL,
    ga_weeks REAL, schedule_visit INTEGER, data_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', colour TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    referred INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, referral_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
    created_by_id TEXT NOT NULL, created_by_name TEXT NOT NULL, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL, updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE(pregnancy_id, patient_key, visit_date)
  ) STRICT;
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS anc_colour_history (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, pregnancy_id TEXT NOT NULL, colour TEXT NOT NULL, reasons_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
    changed_at INTEGER NOT NULL, changed_by_name TEXT NOT NULL
  ) STRICT;
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vaccinations (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, patient_key TEXT NOT NULL, health_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', pregnancy_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    vaccine_name TEXT NOT NULL, vaccine_label TEXT NOT NULL, cms_code TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', dose_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
    given_date TEXT NOT NULL, batch_number TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', injection_site TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    next_appointment TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', reaction TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', quantity REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, unit TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    stock_deducted INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, given_by_id TEXT NOT NULL, given_by_name TEXT NOT NULL, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
  ) STRICT;
  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS vaccinations_health_idx ON vaccinations(health_id, given_date);
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cervical_screening_results (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, patient_key TEXT NOT NULL, result TEXT NOT NULL, note TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', message_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    recorded_by_id TEXT NOT NULL, recorded_by_name TEXT NOT NULL, recorded_at INTEGER NOT NULL
  ) STRICT;`);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- access
// The midwife works in the Obstetric ward; she sees the maternal OPD services.
// Gynecology doctors see the safe-abortion outpatients.
export function canUseMaternalPatient(session, row, sessionWard) {
  const r = role(session);
  if (['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR'].includes(r)) return true;
  if (r === 'MIDWIFE') return isMaternalService(row);
  if (['DOCTOR', 'CHIEF_WARD'].includes(r) && sessionWard === 'gynecology') return isGynecologyOutpatient(row);
  return false;
}

export function canSignReferral(session, sessionWard, options = maternalOptions()) {
  return options.referralSignRoles.includes(role(session)) && options.referralSignWards.includes(String(sessionWard || ''));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- queue
// Today's midwife (or Gynecology outpatient) queue: paid services after the
// Cashier, vaccination at once (free).
export function maternalQueue(database, { date = opdLocalDate(), kind = 'midwife', paymentConfirmed }) {
  const options = maternalOptions();
  return listOpdQueue(database, date).filter((item) => {
    if (!['WAITING', 'IN_CONSULTATION'].includes(item.status) || item.transferredToIpdAt) return false;
    const row = database.findPatient(item.patientKey);
    if (!row || row.discharge_date) return false;
    if (kind === 'gynecology') return isGynecologyOutpatient(row, options) && paymentConfirmed(row);
    if (!isMaternalService(row, options)) return false;
    return isFreeMaternalService(row, options) || paymentConfirmed(row);
  }).map((item) => ({ ...item, form: formFor(item.departmentCode, item.patientKey) }));
}

export function formFor(departmentCode, patientKey) {
  const key = encodeURIComponent(patientKey);
  if (departmentCode === 'anc') return `/anc-record?patient=${key}`;
  if (departmentCode === 'vaccination') return `/vaccination?patient=${key}`;
  return `/emr?patient=${key}&view=opd`;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- pregnancy record
function pregnancyFromRow(row) {
  if (!row) return null;
  return {
    id: row.id, patientKey: row.patient_key, healthId: row.health_id, status: row.status, record: parse(row.record_json),
    colour: row.colour, version: row.version, openedAt: row.opened_at, openedBy: row.opened_by_name,
    closedAt: row.closed_at, closedBy: row.closed_by_name || '', closeReason: row.close_reason,
  };
}
