// Patient keys of the same person (same Health ID): every visit of a pregnancy.
function samePersonKeys(database, row) {
  const keys = new Set([row.patient_key]);
  if (row.health_id) for (const other of database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT patient_key FROM patient_records WHERE health_id=?').all(row.health_id)) keys.add(other.patient_key);
  return [...keys];
}

export function findPregnancy(database, row, { openOnly = false } = {}) {
  if (!openOnly) {
    const linked = database.sqlite.prepare(`SELECT p.* FROM anc_pregnancies p
      WHERE p.patient_key=? OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM anc_visits v WHERE v.pregnancy_id=p.id AND v.patient_key=?)
      ORDER BY p.opened_at DESC LIMIT 1`).get(row.patient_key, row.patient_key);
    if (linked) return pregnancyFromRow(linked);
  }
  const keys = samePersonKeys(database, row);
  const marks = keys.map(() => '?').join(',');
  const statusSql = openOnly ? "AND status='open'" : '';
  const found = database.sqlite.prepare(`SELECT * FROM anc_pregnancies WHERE (patient_key IN (${marks}) ${row.health_id ? 'OR health_id=?' : ''}) ${statusSql}
    ORDER BY CASE status WHEN 'open' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, opened_at DESC LIMIT 1`).get(...keys, ...(row.health_id ? [row.health_id] : []));
  return pregnancyFromRow(found);
}

export function getPregnancy(database, id) {
  return pregnancyFromRow(database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM anc_pregnancies WHERE id=?').get(String(id || '')));
}

// Values imported from Registration and from the Triage of this visit.
export function importedValues(row) {
  const data = parse(row.record_json);
  const value = (key) => String(data[`scope:triage:id:${key}`] ?? data[`id:${key}`] ?? '').trim();
  const bpSys = value('p2_bp_sys'), bpDia = value('p2_bp_dia');
  return {
    name: row.full_name || '', dateOfBirth: row.date_of_birth || String(data['scope:registration:id:web_dob'] || ''), age: row.age || '',
    weightKg: value('p2_weight'), heightCm: value('p2_height'), bp: bpSys && bpDia ? `${bpSys}/${bpDia}` : '',
    pulse: value('p2_pulse'), temperature: value('p2_temp'), spo2: value('p2_spo2'),
  };
}

export function openPregnancy(database, row, session, today = opdLocalDate()) {
  const existing = findPregnancy(database, row, { openOnly: true });
  if (existing) return { pregnancy: existing, created: false };
  const imported = importedValues(row);
  const now = Date.now();
  const record = {
    identity: { name: imported.name, dateOfBirth: imported.dateOfBirth, age: imported.age, gravida: '', births: '', miscarriages: '', abortions: '' },
    heightCm: imported.heightCm, imported: { ...imported, at: today, patientKey: row.patient_key },
    previous: [], labs: [], ultrasounds: [], ticks: {}, tasks: {}, history: {},
  };
  const id = crypto.randomUUID();
  database.sqlite.prepare(`INSERT INTO anc_pregnancies (id,patient_key,health_id,status,record_json,colour,version,opened_at,opened_by_id,opened_by_name,updated_at)
    VALUES (?,?,?,'open',?,'',1,?,?,?,?)`).run(id, row.patient_key, row.health_id || '', JSON.stringify(record), now, session.user_id, staffName(session), now);
  return { pregnancy: getPregnancy(database, id), created: true };
}

export function listVisits(database, pregnancyId) {
  return database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM anc_visits WHERE pregnancy_id=? ORDER BY visit_date, created_at').all(pregnancyId).map((row) => ({
    id: row.id, patientKey: row.patient_key, date: row.visit_date, gaWeeks: row.ga_weeks, scheduleVisit: row.schedule_visit,
    ...parse(row.data_json), colour: row.colour, referred: Boolean(row.referred), referral: parse(row.referral_json),
    createdBy: row.created_by_name, createdAt: row.created_at, updatedAt: row.updated_at,
  }));
}

export function colourHistory(database, pregnancyId) {
  return database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM anc_colour_history WHERE pregnancy_id=? ORDER BY changed_at').all(pregnancyId).map((row) => ({
    colour: row.colour, reasons: parse(row.reasons_json), changedAt: row.changed_at, changedBy: row.changed_by_name,
  }));
}

// Tetanus vaccines of this pregnancy (visit table "Vaccination").
export function pregnancyVaccinations(database, pregnancy) {
  return database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM vaccinations WHERE pregnancy_id=? ORDER BY given_date').all(pregnancy.id).map(vaccinationFromRow);
}

// Released Laboratory results count like the typed ones (colour, tasks).
export function recordWithResults(database, pregnancy) {
  const row = database.findPatient(pregnancy.patientKey);
  const released = row ? releasedResults(database, row) : { labs: [], ultrasounds: [] };
  return { ...pregnancy.record, labs: [...released.labs, ...(pregnancy.record.labs || [])] };
}

export function statusFor(database, pregnancy, today = opdLocalDate()) {
  const visits = listVisits(database, pregnancy.id);
  return ancStatus({ chart: ancColourChart(), schedule: ancVisitSchedule(), record: recordWithResults(database, pregnancy), visits, today, options: maternalOptions() });
}

// Allowed medicines of the midwife (by list name), e.g. iron + folic acid.
export function midwifeMedicineAllowed(name, options = maternalOptions()) {
  const value = String(name || '').toLowerCase().replace(/^[a-z]{2}\d{4}(?:,\s*[a-z]{2}\d{4})*-/, '');
  return options.midwifeMedicines.some((allowed) => value === allowed.toLowerCase() || value.startsWith(`${allowed.toLowerCase()}-`));
}

// Recompute the colour; keep the history of every change (date, reasons, who).
function refreshColour(database, pregnancyId, session, today) {
  const pregnancy = getPregnancy(database, pregnancyId);
  const status = statusFor(database, pregnancy, today);
  if (status.colour !== pregnancy.colour) {
    database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE anc_pregnancies SET colour=? WHERE id=?').run(status.colour, pregnancyId);
    database.sqlite.prepare('INSERT INTO anc_colour_history (id,pregnancy_id,colour,reasons_json,changed_at,changed_by_name) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)')
      .run(crypto.randomUUID(), pregnancyId, status.colour, JSON.stringify(status.reasons.map((reason) => ({ key: reason.key, level: reason.level, kh: reason.kh }))), Date.now(), staffName(session));
  }
  return status;
}

const RECORD_KEYS = ['identity', 'lmp', 'ultrasoundEdd', 'ultrasoundDate', 'heightCm', 'prePregnancyWeightKg', 'prePregnancyWeightSource',
  'history', 'previous', 'labs', 'ultrasounds', 'ticks', 'imported'];

export function saveRecord(database, pregnancyId, session, body, today = opdLocalDate()) {
  const pregnancy = getPregnancy(database, pregnancyId);
  if (!pregnancy) return null;
  if (pregnancy.status !== 'open') throw Object.assign(new Error('This ANC record is closed.'), { status: 409 });
  if (body?.expectedVersion !== undefined && Number(body.expectedVersion) !== pregnancy.version) throw Object.assign(new Error('The ANC record was changed by someone else. Reload it.'), { status: 409 });
  const incoming = body?.record && typeof body.record === 'object' ? body.record : {};
  const record = { ...pregnancy.record };
  for (const key of RECORD_KEYS) if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(incoming, key)) record[key] = incoming[key];
  const text = JSON.stringify(record);
  if (text.length > 400_000) throw new Error('The ANC record is too large.');
  database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE anc_pregnancies SET record_json=?, version=version+1, updated_at=? WHERE id=?').run(text, Date.now(), pregnancyId);
  refreshColour(database, pregnancyId, session, today);
  return getPregnancy(database, pregnancyId);
}

const VISIT_KEYS = ['uh', 'fp', 'fhr', 'bp', 'pulse', 'temperature', 'spo2', 'weight', 'wp', 'proteinuria', 'glucosuria', 'varices', 'fer',
  'complaint', 'diagnosis', 'appointment', 'rapid', 'imported', 'notScheduled', 'legacyDetails'];

// One visit per patient record (this day's encounter); the midwife name comes from the login.
export function saveVisit(database, pregnancyId, row, session, body, today = opdLocalDate()) {
  const pregnancy = getPregnancy(database, pregnancyId);
  if (!pregnancy) return null;
  if (pregnancy.status !== 'open') throw Object.assign(new Error('This ANC record is closed.'), { status: 409 });
  const status = statusFor(database, pregnancy, today);
  const data = {};
  for (const key of VISIT_KEYS) if (body?.visit && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body.visit, key)) data[key] = body.visit[key];
  if (data.legacyDetails !== undefined) data.legacyDetails = cleanAncSupplemental(data.legacyDetails);
  data.staffName = staffName(session);
  const now = Date.now();
  const existing = database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT id FROM anc_visits WHERE pregnancy_id=? AND patient_key=? AND visit_date=?').get(pregnancyId, row.patient_key, today);
  if (existing) {
    database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE anc_visits SET data_json=?, ga_weeks=?, schedule_visit=?, updated_at=? WHERE id=?')
      .run(JSON.stringify(data), status.ga ? status.ga.weeks : null, status.visit?.visit ?? null, now, existing.id);
  } else {
    database.sqlite.prepare(`INSERT INTO anc_visits (id,pregnancy_id,patient_key,visit_date,ga_weeks,schedule_visit,data_json,colour,created_by_id,created_by_name,created_at,updated_at)
      VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`).run(crypto.randomUUID(), pregnancyId, row.patient_key, today, status.ga ? status.ga.weeks : null,
      status.visit?.visit ?? null, JSON.stringify(data), '', session.user_id, staffName(session), now, now);
  }
  const after = refreshColour(database, pregnancyId, session, today);
  database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE anc_visits SET colour=? WHERE pregnancy_id=? AND patient_key=? AND visit_date=?').run(after.colour, pregnancyId, row.patient_key, today);
  return after;
}

// Task of the schedule: done, or closed undone with a reason (other: text).
export function updateTask(database, pregnancyId, session, body, options = maternalOptions()) {
  const pregnancy = getPregnancy(database, pregnancyId);
  if (!pregnancy) return null;
  const key = String(body?.key || '');
  if (!/^\d+:(records|lab|exam|ultrasound|care):\d+$/.test(key)) throw new Error('Task is invalid.');
  const action = String(body?.action || '');
  const tasks = { ...(pregnancy.record.tasks || {}) };
  if (action === 'reopen') delete tasks[key];
  else if (action === 'done') tasks[key] = { status: 'done', at: Date.now(), by: staffName(session) };
  else if (action === 'close') {
    const reason = String(body?.reason || '').trim();
    const other = options.taskCloseReasons[options.taskCloseReasons.length - 1];
    const reasonText = String(body?.reasonText || '').trim().slice(0, 500);
    if (!options.taskCloseReasons.includes(reason)) throw Object.assign(new Error('Choose the reason the task is not done.'), { code: 'reason-required' });
    if (reason === other && !reasonText) throw Object.assign(new Error('Write the reason.'), { code: 'reason-required' });
    tasks[key] = { status: 'closed', reason, reasonText, at: Date.now(), by: staffName(session) };
  } else throw new Error('Task action is invalid.');
  const record = { ...pregnancy.record, tasks };
  database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE anc_pregnancies SET record_json=?, version=version+1, updated_at=? WHERE id=?').run(JSON.stringify(record), Date.now(), pregnancyId);
  return getPregnancy(database, pregnancyId);
}

// Red: the midwife asks for the referral to the provincial hospital (CPA3);
// a doctor of the Obstetric or Gynecology ward signs it.
export function requestReferral(database, pregnancyId, row, session, body, today = opdLocalDate()) {
  const pregnancy = getPregnancy(database, pregnancyId);
  if (!pregnancy) return null;
  const status = statusFor(database, pregnancy, today);
  if (status.colour !== 'red') throw Object.assign(new Error('Only a red pregnancy is referred to CPA3.'), { status: 409 });
  let visit = database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM anc_visits WHERE pregnancy_id=? ORDER BY visit_date DESC, created_at DESC LIMIT 1').get(pregnancyId);
  if (!visit) { saveVisit(database, pregnancyId, row, session, { visit: {} }, today); visit = database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM anc_visits WHERE pregnancy_id=? ORDER BY visit_date DESC LIMIT 1').get(pregnancyId); }
  const referral = {
    requestedAt: Date.now(), requestedBy: staffName(session), destination: 'CPA3',
    reasons: status.reasons.filter((reason) => reason.level === 'red').map((reason) => reason.kh),
    note: String(body?.note || '').slice(0, 2000), treatment: String(body?.treatment || '').slice(0, 2000), signed: null,
  };
  database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE anc_visits SET referral_json=?, updated_at=? WHERE id=?').run(JSON.stringify(referral), Date.now(), visit.id);
  const message = createSystemMessage(database, {
    senderUserId: session.user_id, senderRole: role(session), senderWardCode: 'obstetric',
    destinationType: 'WARD', destinationCode: 'obstetric', destinationRole: 'DOCTOR',
    subject: `ANC · CPA3 · ${row.full_name || row.patient_key}`,
    body: `${row.full_name || ''} · ${row.health_id || row.case_number || ''} · /anc-record?patient=${encodeURIComponent(row.patient_key)}`,
    patientKey: row.patient_key,
  });
  return { visitId: visit.id, referral, messageId: message?.id || '' };
}

export function signReferral(database, pregnancyId, session, now = Date.now()) {
  const visit = database.sqlite.prepare("SELECT * FROM anc_visits WHERE pregnancy_id=? AND referral_json LIKE '%requestedAt%' ORDER BY visit_date DESC, created_at DESC LIMIT 1").get(pregnancyId);
  if (!visit) throw Object.assign(new Error('No referral is waiting for a signature.'), { status: 404 });
  const referral = parse(visit.referral_json);
  referral.signed = { ...signatureSnapshot(database, session, now), signedAt: now, signerRole: role(session) };
  database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE anc_visits SET referral_json=?, referred=1, updated_at=? WHERE id=?').run(JSON.stringify(referral), now, visit.id);
  database.sqlite.prepare("UPDATE anc_pregnancies SET status='referred', closed_at=?, closed_by_name=?, close_reason='CPA3', updated_at=? WHERE id=?")
    .run(now, staffName(session), now, pregnancyId);
  return { visitId: visit.id, referral };
}

export function closePregnancy(database, pregnancyId, session, body) {
  const status = String(body?.status || '');
  if (!['delivered', 'closed'].includes(status)) throw new Error('Choose delivered or closed.');
  const reason = String(body?.reason || '').trim().slice(0, 500);
  if (status === 'closed' && !reason) throw new Error('Write the reason.');
  const result = database.sqlite.prepare("UPDATE anc_pregnancies SET status=?, closed_at=?, closed_by_name=?, close_reason=?, updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND status='open'")
    .run(status, Date.now(), staffName(session), reason, Date.now(), String(pregnancyId || ''));
  return result.changes === 1 ? getPregnancy(database, pregnancyId) : null;
}

// Released Laboratory results and Imaging reports of this person for the record tables.
export function releasedResults(database, row) {
  const keys = samePersonKeys(database, row);
  const marks = keys.map(() => '?').join(',');
  let orders = [];
  try {
    orders = database.sqlite.prepare(`SELECT * FROM paraclinic_orders WHERE patient_key IN (${marks}) AND released_at IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL ORDER BY released_at`).all(...keys);
  } catch { orders = []; }
  const labs = [], ultrasounds = [];
  const LAB_FIELDS = { HIV: 'hiv', HIV_RDT: 'hiv', SYPHILIS_RDT: 'syphilis', VDRL: 'syphilis', RPR: 'syphilis', TPHA: 'syphilis', HBSAG: 'hbsag', HBV_RDT: 'hbsag', HCV: 'hcv', HCV_RDT: 'hcv', BLOOD_GROUP: 'group', HGB: 'hb', HCT: 'hct', PLT: 'plt' };
  for (const order of orders) {
    const result = parse(order.result_json);
    const date = new Date(Number(order.released_at || order.updated_at || Date.now())).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    if (order.modality === 'LAB') {
      const column = { date, source: 'lab', orderId: order.id };
      for (const item of result.items || []) {
        const field = LAB_FIELDS[String(item.code || '').toUpperCase()];
        if (field) column[field] = String(item.value ?? item.result ?? '');
        if (String(item.code || '').toUpperCase() === 'CBC') {
          for (const sub of item.components || item.parameters || []) {
            const name = String(sub.code || sub.name || '').toUpperCase();
            if (/^(HB|HGB)/.test(name)) column.hb = String(sub.value ?? '');
            if (/^(HCT|HEMATOCRIT)/.test(name)) column.hct = String(sub.value ?? '');
            if (/^PLT/.test(name)) column.plt = String(sub.value ?? '');
          }
        }
      }
      if (Object.keys(column).length > 3) labs.push(column);
    } else if (order.modality === 'ULTRASOUND') {
      const report = result.report || result;
      ultrasounds.push({ date, source: 'imaging', orderId: order.id, ga: report.ga || '', presentation: report.presentation || '', bpd: report.bpd || '',
        placenta: report.placenta || '', afi: report.afi || '', weight: report.weight || report.efw || '', edd: report.edd || '',
        other: String(report.conclusion || report.impression || report.findings || '').slice(0, 300) });
    }
  }
  return { labs, ultrasounds };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- vaccination
function vaccinationFromRow(row) {
  return {
    id: row.id, patientKey: row.patient_key, healthId: row.health_id, pregnancyId: row.pregnancy_id, vaccineName: row.vaccine_name,
    vaccineLabel: row.vaccine_label, cmsCode: row.cms_code, doseNumber: row.dose_number, givenDate: row.given_date,
    batchNumber: row.batch_number, injectionSite: row.injection_site, nextAppointment: row.next_appointment, reaction: row.reaction,
    quantity: row.quantity, unit: row.unit, stockDeducted: Boolean(row.stock_deducted), givenBy: row.given_by_name, createdAt: row.created_at,
  };
}

export const vaccineLines = () => medicineCatalogue().items.filter((item) => item.vaccine);

export function vaccinationHistory(database, row) {
  const keys = samePersonKeys(database, row);
  const marks = keys.map(() => '?').join(',');
  return database.sqlite.prepare(`SELECT * FROM vaccinations WHERE patient_key IN (${marks}) ${row.health_id ? 'OR health_id=?' : ''} ORDER BY given_date, created_at`)
    .all(...keys, ...(row.health_id ? [row.health_id] : [])).map(vaccinationFromRow);
}

// Dose quantity from the strength ("0.5 mL in ..."): the amount given each time.
function defaultQuantity(item) {
  const match = /(\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)\s*(mL|IU)\b/i.exec(String(item?.strength || ''));
  return match ? Number(match[1].replace(',', '.')) : 1;
}

export function recordVaccination(database, row, session, body, options = maternalOptions()) {
  const item = vaccineLines().find((line) => String(line.id) === String(body?.vaccineId));
  if (!item) throw new Error('Choose a vaccine from the list.');
  const doseNumber = Number(body?.doseNumber);
  if (!Number.isInteger(doseNumber) || doseNumber < 1 || doseNumber > 10) throw new Error('Dose number is invalid.');
  const givenDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(body?.givenDate || '')) ? body.givenDate : opdLocalDate();
  const history = vaccinationHistory(database, row);
  if (history.some((entry) => entry.vaccineName === item.name && entry.doseNumber === doseNumber) && body?.confirmDuplicate !== true) {
    throw Object.assign(new Error('This vaccine and dose number were already given.'), { status: 409, duplicate: true });
  }
  const quantity = Number(body?.quantity) > 0 ? Number(body.quantity) : defaultQuantity(item);
  const unit = String(body?.unit || item.unit || '').slice(0, 20);
  const pregnancy = item.name === options.tetanusVaccineName ? findPregnancy(database, row, { openOnly: true }) : null;
  const id = crypto.randomUUID();
  const text = (value, max) => String(value || '').trim().slice(0, max);
  // Stock: the line of the medicine list (label) or the medicine name, same unit.
  const stockItem = listStock(database, 'medicine').find((entry) => [item.label, item.name].some((name) => entry.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase()) && sameMedicineUnit(entry.unit, unit));
  database.sqlite.prepare(`INSERT INTO vaccinations (id,patient_key,health_id,pregnancy_id,vaccine_name,vaccine_label,cms_code,dose_number,given_date,batch_number,injection_site,next_appointment,reaction,quantity,unit,stock_deducted,given_by_id,given_by_name,created_at)
    VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`).run(id, row.patient_key, row.health_id || '', pregnancy?.id || '', item.name, item.label, item.code || '',
    doseNumber, givenDate, text(body?.batchNumber, 80), text(body?.injectionSite, 80), /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(body?.nextAppointment || '')) ? body.nextAppointment : '',
    text(body?.reaction, 500), quantity, unit, stockItem ? 1 : 0, session.user_id, staffName(session), Date.now());
  if (stockItem) syncMedicineStock(database, `VACCINATION:${id}`, [{ name: stockItem.name, unit: stockItem.unit, total: quantity }], session.user_id);
  return { vaccination: vaccinationFromRow(database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM vaccinations WHERE id=?').get(id)), pregnancyId: pregnancy?.id || '' };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- cervical screening
// The midwife's result goes to the Gynecology doctors' inbox (existing staff
// messages); the doctor's acknowledgement completes it.
export function sendCervicalResult(database, row, session, body, options = maternalOptions()) {
  const result = String(body?.result || '').trim().slice(0, 200);
  if (!result) throw new Error('Enter the screening result.');
  const note = String(body?.note || '').trim().slice(0, 2000);
  const message = createSystemMessage(database, {
    senderUserId: session.user_id, senderRole: role(session), senderWardCode: 'obstetric',
    destinationType: 'WARD', destinationCode: options.cervicalResultWard, destinationRole: options.cervicalResultRole,
    subject: ['ពិនិត្យមហារីកមាត់ស្បូន', row.full_name || row.patient_key].join(' · '),
    body: `${row.full_name || ''} · ${row.health_id || row.case_number || ''}\n${result}${note ? `\n${note}` : ''}\n/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(row.patient_key)}`,
    patientKey: row.patient_key,
  });
  const id = crypto.randomUUID();
  database.sqlite.prepare('INSERT INTO cervical_screening_results (id,patient_key,result,note,message_id,recorded_by_id,recorded_by_name,recorded_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)')
    .run(id, row.patient_key, result, note, message?.id || '', session.user_id, staffName(session), Date.now());
  return { id, messageId: message?.id || '' };
}

export { gestationalAge, datingEdd };
===== END FILE: server/maternal.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: server/routes/maternal.mjs =====
// v42.6 - /api/maternal/* (midwife queue, settings, cervical result),
// /api/anc/* (official ANC record) and /api/vaccinations.
import {
  ancColourChart, ancRecordLabels, ancVisitSchedule, canSignReferral, canUseMaternalPatient, closePregnancy, colourHistory,
  findPregnancy, getPregnancy, importedValues, isFreeMaternalService, listVisits, maternalOptions, maternalQueue, midwifeMedicineAllowed, openPregnancy,
  pregnancyVaccinations, recordVaccination, releasedResults, requestReferral, saveRecord, saveVisit, sendCervicalResult,
  signReferral, statusFor, updateTask, vaccinationHistory, vaccineLines,
} from '../maternal.mjs';
import { medicineCatalogue } from '../medicine-catalogue.mjs';
import { createSystemMessage } from '../messages.mjs';
import { opdLocalDate, updateOpdQueueStatus } from '../opd-queue.mjs';
import { prepareOpdPrescription, submitOpdPrescription } from '../pharmacy-prescriptions.mjs';
import { signatureSnapshot } from '../esign.mjs';
import { syncMedicineStock } from '../stock.mjs';
import { legacyAncFields, readLegacyAnc } from '../anc-legacy.mjs';

const READ_ROLES = new Set(['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR', 'MIDWIFE', 'DOCTOR', 'CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE']);

export function mountMaternalRoutes(router) {
  const fail = (ctx, status, error, extra = {}) => ctx.sendJson(ctx.response, status, { error, ...extra }, { secure: ctx.config.cookieSecure });
  const ok = (ctx, status, body) => ctx.sendJson(ctx.response, status, body, { secure: ctx.config.cookieSecure });

  function actor(ctx) {
    const { session, requireContext } = ctx;
    if (!session) { fail(ctx, 401, 'Authentication required.'); return null; }
    const role = requireContext(session);
    return { role, ward: ctx.sessionWard(session), session: { ...session, activeRole: role, active_role: role } };
  }

  // The patient for a maternal page: the midwife (maternal services), the
  // Gynecology ward (safe abortion) or anyone who already sees the record
  // (an Obstetric doctor signing a referral, management).
  function patientFor(ctx, who, patientKey, { write = false } = {}) {
    const { database, clinicalPatientKey, canAccessPatient } = ctx;
    const row = database.findPatient(clinicalPatientKey(patientKey));
    if (!row) { fail(ctx, 404, 'Patient not found.'); return null; }
    const maternal = canUseMaternalPatient(who.session, row, who.ward);
    const ward = canSignReferral(who.session, who.ward) || canAccessPatient(database, who.session, row);
    // Writing: the midwife's own services; a doctor who signs referrals reaches the
    // record too (each action checks its own role).
    const signer = canSignReferral(who.session, who.ward);
    if (!(maternal || (write && signer) || (!write && ward && READ_ROLES.has(who.role)))) { fail(ctx, 403, 'This patient is outside your service.'); return null; }
    return row;
  }

  router.get('/api/maternal/config', async (ctx) => {
    const who = actor(ctx); if (!who) return true;
    const options = maternalOptions();
    const allowed = new Set(options.midwifeMedicines.map((name) => name.toLowerCase()));
    return ok(ctx, 200, {
      chart: ancColourChart(), schedule: ancVisitSchedule(), labels: ancRecordLabels(), options, legacyFields: legacyAncFields,
      vaccines: vaccineLines(), midwifeMedicines: medicineCatalogue().items.filter((item) => allowed.has(item.name.toLowerCase())),
      canSignReferral: canSignReferral(who.session, who.ward), role: who.role, today: opdLocalDate(),
    });
  });

  router.get('/api/maternal/queue', async (ctx) => {
    const who = actor(ctx); if (!who) return true;
    const gynecology = ['DOCTOR', 'CHIEF_WARD'].includes(who.role) && who.ward === 'gynecology';
    const management = ['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR'].includes(who.role);
    if (!(who.role === 'MIDWIFE' || gynecology || management)) return fail(ctx, 403, 'The maternal services queue is for the midwife.');
    const kind = gynecology || ctx.url.searchParams.get('kind') === 'gynecology' ? 'gynecology' : 'midwife';
    try {
      const date = ctx.url.searchParams.get('date') || opdLocalDate();
      const patients = maternalQueue(ctx.database, { date, kind, paymentConfirmed: (row) => ctx.opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed(ctx.database, row) });
      return ok(ctx, 200, { date, kind, patients });
    } catch (error) {
      return fail(ctx, 400, error.message);
    }
  });

  // ANC record of the pregnancy (open, or the latest closed one, read-only).
  router.get('/api/anc/record', async (ctx) => {
    const who = actor(ctx); if (!who) return true;
    const row = patientFor(ctx, who, ctx.url.searchParams.get('patientKey')); if (!row) return true;
    const pregnancy = findPregnancy(ctx.database, row);
    const today = opdLocalDate();
    const body = {
      patient: { patientKey: row.patient_key, fullName: row.full_name, healthId: row.health_id, caseNumber: row.case_number, sex: row.sex, age: row.age, departmentCode: row.department_code },
      imported: importedValues(row), released: releasedResults(ctx.database, row), legacyAnc: readLegacyAnc(row), pregnancy: null, visits: [], colourHistory: [], status: null, vaccinations: [],
      canEdit: canUseMaternalPatient(who.session, row, who.ward) && who.role === 'MIDWIFE' || ['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(who.role),
      canSignReferral: canSignReferral(who.session, who.ward), today,
    };
    if (pregnancy) {
      body.pregnancy = pregnancy;
      body.visits = listVisits(ctx.database, pregnancy.id);
      body.colourHistory = colourHistory(ctx.database, pregnancy.id);
      body.status = statusFor(ctx.database, pregnancy, today);
      body.vaccinations = pregnancyVaccinations(ctx.database, pregnancy);
    }
    return ok(ctx, 200, body);
  });

  // Colour badge for the patient banner on every page.
  router.get('/api/anc/badge', async (ctx) => {
    const who = actor(ctx); if (!who) return true;
    const { database, clinicalPatientKey, canAccessPatient } = ctx;
    const row = database.findPatient(clinicalPatientKey(ctx.url.searchParams.get('patientKey')));
    if (!row || !(canAccessPatient(database, who.session, row) || canUseMaternalPatient(who.session, row, who.ward))) return ok(ctx, 200, { colour: '' });
    const pregnancy = findPregnancy(database, row, { openOnly: true });
    return ok(ctx, 200, { colour: pregnancy?.colour || '', status: pregnancy?.status || '' });
  });

  async function pregnancyAction(ctx, handler) {
    const who = actor(ctx); if (!who) return true;
    const body = await ctx.readJson(ctx.request, 500_000);
    const row = patientFor(ctx, who, body?.patientKey, { write: true });
    if (!row) return true;
    try {
      return await handler({ who, row, body });
    } catch (error) {
      return fail(ctx, error.status || 400, error.message, error.duplicate ? { duplicate: true } : error.code ? { code: error.code } : {});
    }
  }

  const midwifeOnly = (ctx, who) => {
    if (who.role === 'MIDWIFE' || ['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(who.role)) return true;
    fail(ctx, 403, 'Only the midwife writes the ANC record.');
    return false;
  };

  router.post('/api/anc/record/open', async (ctx) => pregnancyAction(ctx, ({ who, row }) => {
    if (!midwifeOnly(ctx, who)) return true;
    const result = openPregnancy(ctx.database, row, who.session);
    if (result.created) ctx.audit(ctx.database, ctx.request, 'ANC_RECORD_OPENED', { userId: who.session.user_id, subjectUsername: who.session.username, details: { patientKey: row.patient_key, pregnancyId: result.pregnancy.id } });
    return ok(ctx, result.created ? 201 : 200, { pregnancy: result.pregnancy, status: statusFor(ctx.database, result.pregnancy, opdLocalDate()) });
  }));

  router.post(/^\/api\/anc\/record\/([0-9a-f-]{36})\/(save|save-all|visit|task|referral|sign|close|prescription)$/, async (ctx) => {
    const [, id, action] = ctx.routeParams;
    return pregnancyAction(ctx, ({ who, row, body }) => {
      const pregnancy = getPregnancy(ctx.database, id);
      if (!pregnancy) return fail(ctx, 404, 'ANC record not found.');
      if (pregnancy.patientKey !== row.patient_key && !(pregnancy.healthId && pregnancy.healthId === row.health_id)) return fail(ctx, 403, 'The pregnancy does not belong to this patient.');
      const today = opdLocalDate();
      const event = (name, details = {}) => ctx.audit(ctx.database, ctx.request, name, { userId: who.session.user_id, subjectUsername: who.session.username, details: { patientKey: row.patient_key, pregnancyId: id, ...details } });
      if (action === 'sign') {
        if (!canSignReferral(who.session, who.ward)) return fail(ctx, 403, 'A doctor of the Obstetric or Gynecology ward signs the referral.');
        const signed = signReferral(ctx.database, id, who.session);
        event('ANC_REFERRAL_SIGNED', { visitId: signed.visitId });
        return ok(ctx, 200, { ...signed, pregnancy: getPregnancy(ctx.database, id) });
      }
      if (!midwifeOnly(ctx, who)) return true;
      if (action === 'save-all') {
        if (Number(body.expectedVersion) !== pregnancy.version) return fail(ctx, 409, 'The ANC record changed. Reload it before saving.');
        ctx.database.sqlite.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
        try {
          saveRecord(ctx.database, id, who.session, body, today);
          saveVisit(ctx.database, id, row, who.session, body, today);
          ctx.database.sqlite.exec('COMMIT');
        } catch (error) { ctx.database.sqlite.exec('ROLLBACK'); throw error; }
        try { updateOpdQueueStatus(ctx.database, row.patient_key, 'IN_CONSULTATION'); } catch { /* no queue entry */ }
        event('ANC_VISIT_SAVED', { unified: true });
        return ok(ctx, 200, { pregnancy: getPregnancy(ctx.database, id), visits: listVisits(ctx.database, id), colourHistory: colourHistory(ctx.database, id) });
      }
      if (action === 'save') {
        const saved = saveRecord(ctx.database, id, who.session, body, today);
        event('ANC_RECORD_SAVED', { colour: saved.colour });
        return ok(ctx, 200, { pregnancy: saved, status: statusFor(ctx.database, saved, today), colourHistory: colourHistory(ctx.database, id) });
      }
      if (action === 'visit') {
        const status = saveVisit(ctx.database, id, row, who.session, body, today);
        try { updateOpdQueueStatus(ctx.database, row.patient_key, 'IN_CONSULTATION'); } catch { /* not in today's queue */ }
        event('ANC_VISIT_SAVED', { colour: status.colour });
        return ok(ctx, 200, { status, visits: listVisits(ctx.database, id), pregnancy: getPregnancy(ctx.database, id), colourHistory: colourHistory(ctx.database, id) });
      }
      if (action === 'task') {
        const updated = updateTask(ctx.database, id, who.session, body);
        event('ANC_TASK_UPDATED', { key: body?.key, action: body?.action, reason: body?.reason || '' });
        return ok(ctx, 200, { pregnancy: updated, status: statusFor(ctx.database, updated, today) });
      }
      if (action === 'prescription') return sendPrescription(ctx, who, row, body, event);
      if (action === 'referral') {
        const referral = requestReferral(ctx.database, id, row, who.session, body, today);
        event('ANC_REFERRAL_REQUESTED', { visitId: referral.visitId });
        return ok(ctx, 200, { ...referral, visits: listVisits(ctx.database, id) });
      }
      const closed = closePregnancy(ctx.database, id, who.session, body);
      if (!closed) return fail(ctx, 409, 'This ANC record is already closed.');
      event('ANC_RECORD_CLOSED', { status: closed.status });
      return ok(ctx, 200, { pregnancy: closed });
    });
  });

  // The midwife's prescription (iron + folic acid ...) goes to Pharmacy like an
  // OPD prescription; only the medicines of the maternal services are accepted.
  function sendPrescription(ctx, who, row, body, event) {
    const { database } = ctx;
    const fields = ['name', 'route', 'unit', 'doseAmount', 'scheduleType', 'm', 'a', 'e', 'n', 'd', 'total', 'note', 'cmsCode'];
    const medications = (Array.isArray(body?.medications) ? body.medications.slice(0, 20) : [])
      .map((med) => Object.fromEntries(fields.map((key) => [key, String(med?.[key] ?? '').trim().slice(0, key === 'name' ? 600 : 200)])))
      .filter((med) => med.name);
    if (!medications.length) return fail(ctx, 400, 'Enter the medicines.');
    const refused = medications.filter((med) => !midwifeMedicineAllowed(med.name));
    if (refused.length) return fail(ctx, 403, 'ឆ្មបអាចចេញវេជ្ជបញ្ជាតែឱសថសេវាមាតាប៉ុណ្ណោះ។', { refused: refused.map((med) => med.name) });
    const data = ctx.parseStoredJson(row.record_json);
    const signature = signatureSnapshot(database, who.session, Date.now());
    data['system:opdMedications'] = JSON.stringify(medications);
    data['system:signature:medical'] = JSON.stringify(signature);
    const summary = { serviceMode: row.service_mode, fullName: row.full_name, healthId: row.health_id, caseNumber: row.case_number,
      age: row.age, sex: row.sex, departmentNameKh: row.department_name_kh, entryDate: row.entry_date };
    const snapshot = prepareOpdPrescription(data, summary, signature);
    let submission;
    database.sqlite.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
    try {
      database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE patient_records SET record_json=?, updated_at=? WHERE patient_key=?').run(JSON.stringify(data), Date.now(), row.patient_key);
      submission = submitOpdPrescription(database, row, snapshot, { userId: who.session.user_id, role: who.role, wardCode: who.ward });
      if (!submission.duplicate) {
        updateOpdQueueStatus(database, row.patient_key, 'SENT_TO_PHARMACY');
        syncMedicineStock(database, `OPD_RX:${submission.id}`, medications, who.session.user_id);
      }
      database.sqlite.exec('COMMIT');
    } catch (error) { database.sqlite.exec('ROLLBACK'); throw error; }
    event('OPD_PRESCRIPTION_SUBMITTED', { prescriptionId: submission.id, duplicate: submission.duplicate, source: 'ANC' });
    return ok(ctx, 200, { prescriptionId: submission.id, duplicate: submission.duplicate });
  }

  router.get('/api/vaccinations', async (ctx) => {
    const who = actor(ctx); if (!who) return true;
    const row = patientFor(ctx, who, ctx.url.searchParams.get('patientKey')); if (!row) return true;
    return ok(ctx, 200, { patient: { patientKey: row.patient_key, fullName: row.full_name, healthId: row.health_id, sex: row.sex, age: row.age, dateOfBirth: row.date_of_birth || '' },
      vaccines: vaccineLines(), history: vaccinationHistory(ctx.database, row), free: isFreeMaternalService(row), today: opdLocalDate() });
  });

  router.post('/api/vaccinations', async (ctx) => pregnancyAction(ctx, ({ who, row, body }) => {
    if (who.role !== 'MIDWIFE') return fail(ctx, 403, 'Only the midwife records a vaccination.');
    const result = recordVaccination(ctx.database, row, who.session, body);
    ctx.audit(ctx.database, ctx.request, 'VACCINATION_RECORDED', { userId: who.session.user_id, subjectUsername: who.session.username,
      details: { patientKey: row.patient_key, vaccinationId: result.vaccination.id, vaccine: result.vaccination.vaccineName, dose: result.vaccination.doseNumber } });
    return ok(ctx, 201, { ...result, history: vaccinationHistory(ctx.database, row) });
  }));

  // A test of the schedule the midwife does not order (OGTT, CBC, blood group):
  // a message asks the Obstetric ward doctors to order it.
  router.post('/api/maternal/ask-doctor', async (ctx) => pregnancyAction(ctx, ({ who, row, body }) => {
    if (who.role !== 'MIDWIFE') return fail(ctx, 403, 'Only the midwife asks for a test.');
    const test = String(body?.test || '').replace(/[^A-Z0-9_]/gi, '').slice(0, 40);
    if (!test) return fail(ctx, 400, 'Choose the test.');
    const message = createSystemMessage(ctx.database, { senderUserId: who.session.user_id, senderRole: who.role, senderWardCode: who.ward,
      destinationType: 'WARD', destinationCode: 'obstetric', destinationRole: 'DOCTOR',
      subject: `ANC · ${test} · ${row.full_name || row.patient_key}`, body: `ANC ${test}: ${row.full_name || ''} · ${row.health_id || row.case_number || ''} · /anc-record?patient=${encodeURIComponent(row.patient_key)}`, patientKey: row.patient_key });
    return ok(ctx, 201, { messageId: message.id });
  }));

  router.post('/api/maternal/cervix-result', async (ctx) => pregnancyAction(ctx, ({ who, row, body }) => {
    if (who.role !== 'MIDWIFE') return fail(ctx, 403, 'Only the midwife sends the screening result.');
    const sent = sendCervicalResult(ctx.database, row, who.session, body);
    ctx.audit(ctx.database, ctx.request, 'CERVICAL_RESULT_SENT', { userId: who.session.user_id, subjectUsername: who.session.username, details: { patientKey: row.patient_key, messageId: sent.messageId } });
    return ok(ctx, 201, sent);
  }));
}
===== END FILE: server/routes/maternal.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: server/routes/patients.mjs =====
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { selectionFor, beneficiaryPayment, saveBeneficiaryLink } from '../npca/beneficiaries.mjs';
import { mapCode } from '../npca/codes.mjs';
import { roleCanPage, patientWorkWard, sessionRole } from '../access.mjs';
import { mergeSharedEncounterFields } from '../shared-encounter-fields.mjs';
import { listAppointments, createAppointment, updateAppointmentStatus } from '../appointments.mjs';
import { listOrders } from '../paraclinic.mjs';
import { listEditorHistory, saveEditorHistory, patientRecordChangeNotes } from '../editor-history.mjs';
import { validPatientPhoto } from '../patient-photo.mjs';
import { signatureSnapshot } from '../esign.mjs';
import { referToOpd, updateOpdQueueStatus, getOpdQueueEntry } from '../opd-queue.mjs';
import { prepareOpdPrescription, submitOpdPrescription } from '../pharmacy-prescriptions.mjs';
import { queueLateWardMedicineOrder } from '../ward-medicine-approvals.mjs';
import { ordersForDate } from '../ward-medicines.mjs';
import { syncMedicineStock } from '../stock.mjs';
import { completePatientMessages } from '../messages.mjs';
import { syncClinicalRequests } from '../requests.mjs';
import { ensureAdmissionMovement, recordBedAssignment, recordMovement } from '../daily-report.mjs';
import { patientBill } from '../finance.mjs';
import { queueCashierPayment } from '../invoice-edits.mjs';
import { encounterAccess, assertEncounterWrite, consumeCorrection, RECORD_MANAGEMENT } from '../encounter-access.mjs';
import { allergyAlert, patientRef } from '../clinical-summary.mjs';
import { logFromContext } from '../record-access.mjs';
import { isFreeMaternalService, isGynecologyOutpatient, isMaternalService, maternalOptions } from '../maternal.mjs';

const caseTypeFromData = dataValue => {
  const hasNew = dataValue?.['scope:registration:id:emr_pmrs_case_new'] === true || dataValue?.['scope:registration:id:emr_pmrs_case_new'] === 'true';
  const hasOld = dataValue?.['scope:registration:id:emr_pmrs_case_old'] === true || dataValue?.['scope:registration:id:emr_pmrs_case_old'] === 'true';
  if (hasNew === hasOld) return '';
  return hasNew ? 'new' : 'old';
};

export function mountPatientsRoutes(router) {
  // 1. GET /api/appointments
  router.get('/api/appointments', async (ctx) => {
    const { session, database, config, sendJson, url, requireContext } = ctx;
    if (!session) return sendJson(ctx.response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const role = requireContext(session);
    if (!['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR', 'REGISTRATION'].includes(role)) {
      return sendJson(ctx.response, 403, { error: 'Registration access is required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    return sendJson(ctx.response, 200, {
      appointments: listAppointments(
        database,
        url.searchParams.get('startDate') || url.searchParams.get('date') || '',
        url.searchParams.get('endDate') || '',
        url.searchParams.get('rolling') === '1'
      )
    }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 2. POST /api/appointments
  router.post('/api/appointments', async (ctx) => {
    const { request, response, session, database, config, sendJson, readJson, audit, requireContext } = ctx;
    if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const role = requireContext(session);
    if (!['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'REGISTRATION'].includes(role)) {
      return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Registration access is required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    const body = await readJson(request, config.maxBodyBytes);
    try {
      const appointment = createAppointment(database, body || {}, session.user_id);
      audit(database, request, 'APPOINTMENT_CREATED', {
        userId: session.user_id,
        subjectUsername: session.username,
        details: { appointmentId: appointment.id, date: appointment.appointmentDate }
      });
      return sendJson(response, 201, { appointment }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendJson(response, 400, { error: error.message }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
  });

  // 3. POST /api/appointments/:id/status
  router.post(/^\/api\/appointments\/([0-9a-f-]+)\/status$/, async (ctx) => {
    const { request, response, session, database, config, sendJson, readJson, audit, requireContext, routeParams } = ctx;
    if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const role = requireContext(session);
    if (!['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'REGISTRATION'].includes(role)) {
      return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Registration access is required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    const appointmentId = routeParams[1];
    const body = await readJson(request, 10_000);
    try {
      const appointment = updateAppointmentStatus(database, appointmentId, body?.status, session.user_id);
      if (!appointment) return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Appointment was not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      audit(database, request, 'APPOINTMENT_STATUS_UPDATED', {
        userId: session.user_id,
        subjectUsername: session.username,
        details: { appointmentId: appointment.id, status: appointment.status }
      });
      return sendJson(response, 200, { appointment }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    } catch (error) {
      return sendJson(response, 400, { error: error.message }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
  });

  // 4. GET /api/encounter-access
  router.get('/api/encounter-access', async (ctx) => {
    const { response, session, database, config, sendJson, url, clinicalPatientKey } = ctx;
    if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'សូមចូលគណនីជាមុន។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const row = database.findPatient(clinicalPatientKey(url.searchParams.get('patientKey')));
    if (!row) return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'រកមិនឃើញកំណត់ត្រាអ្នកជំងឺ។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    return sendJson(response, 200, { access: encounterAccess(database, session, row, url.searchParams.get('scope') || '') }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 5. GET /api/record-viewer
  router.get('/api/record-viewer', async (ctx) => {
    const { response, session, database, config, sendJson, url, requireContext, clinicalPatientKey, parseStoredJson, patientRecordResponse, buildIpdDischargeSummary } = ctx;
    if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'សូមចូលគណនីជាមុន។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const row = database.findPatient(clinicalPatientKey(url.searchParams.get('patientKey')));
    if (!row) return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'រកមិនឃើញកំណត់ត្រាអ្នកជំងឺ។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const role = requireContext(session);
    if (!RECORD_MANAGEMENT.includes(role)) return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'ទំព័រនេះសម្រាប់គណៈគ្រប់គ្រងប៉ុណ្ណោះ។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const rows = row.health_id ? database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM patient_records WHERE health_id=? COLLATE NOCASE ORDER BY entry_date DESC,created_at DESC').all(row.health_id) : [row];
    const encounters = rows.map(record => {
      const data = parseStoredJson(record.record_json);
      return {
        patient: patientRecordResponse(record, true),
        orders: listOrders(database, { patientKey: record.patient_key }),
        resume: record.service_mode === 'ipd' ? buildIpdDischargeSummary(database, record, data, data['system:discharge'] || {}) : null
      };
    });
    return sendJson(response, 200, { encounters }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 6. GET /api/patients
  router.get('/api/patients', async (ctx) => {
    const { response, session, database, config, sendJson, url, requireContext, parseStoredJson, patientRecordResponse, paymentStatusForPatient, patientClinicalClosed, canAccessPatient } = ctx;
    if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const includePayment = url.searchParams.get('includePayment') === '1';
    const dispensedKeys = new Set(database.sqlite.prepare(`SELECT DISTINCT patient_key FROM pharmacy_prescriptions WHERE dispensed_at IS NOT NULL`).all().map(r => r.patient_key));
    const role = requireContext(session);
    // v41.0: allergy summary for the ward map bed popup (one query for all patients).
    const allergiesByRef = new Map();
    for (const allergy of database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM patient_allergies WHERE removed_at IS NULL ORDER BY created_at').all()) {
      if (!allergiesByRef.has(allergy.patient_ref)) allergiesByRef.set(allergy.patient_ref, []);
      allergiesByRef.get(allergy.patient_ref).push({ allergyType: allergy.allergy_type, substance: allergy.substance, severity: allergy.severity });
    }
    return sendJson(response, 200, {
      patients: database.listPatientSummaries({ omitPhotos: url.searchParams.get('omitPhotos') === '1' }).map(row => {
        const data = row._parsedData ?? (row._parsedData = parseStoredJson(row.record_json));
        const patient = patientRecordResponse(row, false, data);
        patient.allergyAlert = allergyAlert(allergiesByRef.get(patientRef(row)) || [], data);
        patient.queueNumber = Number(row.queue_num_1 || row.queue_num_2 || 0) || null;
        patient.triageStatus = row.triage_status || '';
        patient.admissionStatus = row.admission_status || '';
        if (includePayment || ['DOCTOR', 'PHARMACY'].includes(role)) patient.paymentStatus = paymentStatusForPatient(database, row.patient_key);
        patient.clinicalClosed = patientClinicalClosed(database, row, dispensedKeys, data);
        patient.canOpenRecord = canAccessPatient(database, session, row) && roleCanPage(role, '/emr');
        patient.recordAccess = encounterAccess(database, session, row);
        patient.canEditClinical = patient.recordAccess.canEdit;
        return patient;
      }),
    }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 7. GET /api/patient-record
  router.get('/api/patient-record', async (ctx) => {
    const { response, session, database, config, sendJson, url, clinicalPatientKey, canAccessPatient, syncOpdParaclinicToRecord, patientRecordResponse, parseStoredJson, paymentStatusForPatient, patientClinicalClosed, patientDataVisibleForRole } = ctx;
    if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const patientKey = clinicalPatientKey(url.searchParams.get('patientKey'));
    let patient = database.findPatient(patientKey);
    if (patient && !canAccessPatient(database, session, patient)) {
      return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'This patient is outside your active ward.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    const role = sessionRole(session);
    if (patient?.service_mode === 'opd' && ['DOCTOR', 'CHIEF_WARD', 'ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR'].includes(role) && url.searchParams.get('readOnly') !== '1' && encounterAccess(database, session, patient).canEdit) {
      syncOpdParaclinicToRecord(database, patientKey);
      patient = database.findPatient(patientKey);
    }
    const limitedIdentityRole = ['LAB', 'IMAGING', 'PHARMACY'].includes(role);
    const patientView = patient ? patientRecordResponse(patient, !limitedIdentityRole) : null;
    if (patientView) {
      const data = parseStoredJson(patient.record_json);
      patientView.queueNumber = Number(data['system:registrationQueueNumber'] || data['scope:registration:id:registration_queue_number'] || 0) || null;
      patientView.paymentStatus = paymentStatusForPatient(database, patient.patient_key);
      patientView.clinicalClosed = patientClinicalClosed(database, patient);
      patientView.recordAccess = encounterAccess(database, session, patient);
    }
    if (patientView && role === 'TRIAGE' && patientView.data) patientView.data = patientDataVisibleForRole(patientView.data, role);
    // v41.7: record view log (repeated openings within two minutes count once).
    if (patient) logFromContext(ctx, patient, 'open', url.searchParams.get('readOnly') === '1' ? 'record (view)' : 'record');
    return sendJson(response, 200, {
      patient: patientView,
      opdQueue: patient?.service_mode === 'opd' ? getOpdQueueEntry(database, patientKey) : null
    }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 8. GET /api/patient-editor-history
  router.get('/api/patient-editor-history', async (ctx) => {
    const { response, session, database, config, sendJson, url, clinicalFormReadRoles, clinicalPatientKey, canAccessPatient } = ctx;
    if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    if (!clinicalFormReadRoles.has(sessionRole(session))) return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Clinical access is required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const patientKey = clinicalPatientKey(url.searchParams.get('patientKey'));
    const patient = database.findPatient(patientKey);
    if (!patient || !canAccessPatient(database, session, patient)) return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Patient not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    return sendJson(response, 200, {
      entries: listEditorHistory(database, patientKey, { includeFieldChanges: ['ADMIN', 'DIRECTOR', 'VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(sessionRole(session)) })
    }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 9. GET /api/patient-history
  router.get('/api/patient-history', async (ctx) => {
    const { response, session, database, config, sendJson, url, requireContext, clinicalPatientKey, canAccessPatient, patientRecordResponse, patientDataVisibleForRole } = ctx;
    if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const role = requireContext(session);
    if (!['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR', 'REGISTRATION', 'TRIAGE', 'DOCTOR', 'CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE'].includes(role)) {
      return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Clinical access is required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    const patientKey = clinicalPatientKey(url.searchParams.get('patientKey'));
    const current = database.findPatient(patientKey);
    if (!current) return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Patient not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    if (!['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role) && !canAccessPatient(database, session, current)) {
      return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'This patient is outside your active ward.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    logFromContext(ctx, current, 'open', 'patient history'); // v41.7 record view log
    let rows;
    if (current.health_id) {
      rows = database.sqlite.prepare(`SELECT * FROM patient_records WHERE health_id=? ORDER BY CASE WHEN entry_date='' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END, entry_date DESC, created_at DESC`).all(current.health_id);
    } else {
      rows = [current];
    }
    const history = rows.map(row => {
      const item = patientRecordResponse(row, true);
      if (role === 'TRIAGE' && item.data) item.data = patientDataVisibleForRole(item.data, role);
      return item;
    });
    return sendJson(response, 200, { history }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 10. DELETE /api/patient-record
  router.delete('/api/patient-record', async (ctx) => {
    const { response, config, sendJson } = ctx;
    return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'សូមផ្ញើសំណើលុបទៅគណៈគ្រប់គ្រង។ មិនអាចលុបដោយផ្ទាល់បានទេ។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 11. DELETE /api/patient-triage
  router.delete('/api/patient-triage', async (ctx) => {
    const { response, config, sendJson } = ctx;
    return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'ការលុបត្រូវការសំណើ និងការអនុម័តពីគណៈគ្រប់គ្រង។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 12. POST /api/patient-triage
  router.post('/api/patient-triage', async (ctx) => {
    const { response, config, sendJson } = ctx;
    return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'ការលុបត្រូវការសំណើ និងការអនុម័តពីគណៈគ្រប់គ្រង។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 13. POST /api/patient-photo
  router.post('/api/patient-photo', async (ctx) => {
    const { request, response, session, database, config, sendJson, readJson, audit, clinicalWriteRoles, clinicalPatientKey, canAccessPatient, parseStoredJson } = ctx;
    if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const role = sessionRole(session);
    if (!clinicalWriteRoles.has(role) && !['REGISTRATION', 'TRIAGE', 'ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE'].includes(role)) {
      return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Your role cannot update patient photos.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    const body = await readJson(request, config.maxPatientBodyBytes);
    const patientKey = clinicalPatientKey(body?.patientKey);
    const photo = String(body?.photo || '');
    if (!validPatientPhoto(photo)) return sendJson(response, 400, { error: 'Invalid patient photo data.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const existing = database.findPatient(patientKey);
    if (!existing) return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Patient not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    if (body.expectedVersion !== undefined && (!Number.isInteger(body.expectedVersion) || body.expectedVersion !== existing.version)) {
      return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'The patient record changed. Reload the patient before uploading the photo.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    if (!canAccessPatient(database, session, existing)) return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'This patient is outside your active ward.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const data = parseStoredJson(existing.record_json);
    data['scope:registration:id:web_patient_photo'] = photo;
    data['scope:registration:id:patient_photo'] = photo;
    data['system:patientPhoto'] = photo;
    const updatedAt = Date.now();
    const changedPhoto = database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE patient_records SET record_json=?, version=version+1, updated_at=? WHERE patient_key=? AND version=?').run(JSON.stringify(data), updatedAt, patientKey, existing.version);
    if (!changedPhoto.changes) return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'The patient record changed. Reload before uploading the photo.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    const updated = database.findPatient(patientKey);
    audit(database, request, 'PATIENT_PHOTO_UPDATED', { userId: session.user_id, subjectUsername: session.username, details: { patientKey, activeRole: role, changedFields: patientRecordChangeNotes(existing, updated) } });
    return sendJson(response, 200, { ok: true, patientKey, patientVersion: updated.version, updatedAt }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
  });

  // 14. PUT /api/patient-record
  router.put('/api/patient-record', async (ctx) => {
    const {
      request, response, session, database, config, sendJson, readJson, audit,
      clinicalWriteRoles, clinicalPatientKey, sanitizePatientSummary, recordData,
      validateDailyMonitoringDates, badRequest, canAccessPatient, parseStoredJson,
      opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed, mergePatientSummaryForRole, mergeRecordDataForRole,
      registrationQueueNumberFromData, nextRegistrationQueueNumber, ensureUniqueRegistrationQueueNumber,
      addCatalogCharge,
      messagingVisibility, sessionWard, automaticWardMessage, notifyTriageReferral,
      releasePatientFromHospitalMap, patientRecordResponse, patientDataVisibleForRole,
      wardMedicineOrderDates,
    } = ctx;

    if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    if (!clinicalWriteRoles.has(sessionRole(session)) && !['REGISTRATION', 'TRIAGE'].includes(sessionRole(session))) {
      return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Your role cannot update patient records.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    const body = await readJson(request, config.maxPatientBodyBytes);
    if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) {
      return sendJson(response, 400, { error: 'Patient record information is invalid.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    const patientKey = clinicalPatientKey(body.patientKey);
    const submittedSummary = sanitizePatientSummary(body.summary);
    const submittedData = recordData(body.data);
    validateDailyMonitoringDates(database, submittedData);
    let summary = submittedSummary;
    if (!summary.caseNumber && !summary.healthId) badRequest('Case number or Health ID is required.');
    if (!summary.fullName) badRequest('Patient name is required.');

    const existingPatient = database.findPatient(patientKey);
    const activeRoleBeforeSave = sessionRole(session) === 'CHIEF_WARD' ? 'DOCTOR' : sessionRole(session);
    const visitSourceKey = body.newVisitFrom ? clinicalPatientKey(body.newVisitFrom) : '';
    if (visitSourceKey) {
      if (!['REGISTRATION', 'ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR'].includes(activeRoleBeforeSave)) {
        return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Only Registration can start a new visit from an existing patient.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }
      if (!database.findPatient(visitSourceKey)) return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'The previous patient record was not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      if (existingPatient || patientKey === visitSourceKey) return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'Use a new case number for this visit. The previous record must remain unchanged.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    const registrationSave = activeRoleBeforeSave === 'REGISTRATION' || (RECORD_MANAGEMENT.includes(activeRoleBeforeSave) && body.editorScope === 'registration');
    if (body.registrationOnly === true && (!registrationSave || body.transferToTriage === true || body.triageSave === true || body.submitOpdPrescription === true)) {
      return sendJson(response, 400, { error: 'Invalid registration-only action.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    if (activeRoleBeforeSave === 'TRIAGE' && !existingPatient) {
      return sendJson(response, 400, { error: 'This patient must be registered first. Triage cannot create a new patient record.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    if (existingPatient && !canAccessPatient(database, session, existingPatient)) {
      return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'This patient is outside your active ward.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    if (existingPatient && Number.isInteger(body.expectedVersion) && body.expectedVersion !== existingPatient.version) {
      return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'This patient record changed. Reopen the latest record before saving so earlier clinical notes are preserved.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    if (body.submitOpdPrescription === true && existingPatient && body.expectedVersion !== existingPatient.version) {
      return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'This patient record changed. Reopen the latest record before sending the prescription.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    const existingData = existingPatient ? parseStoredJson(existingPatient.record_json) : Object.create(null);
    if ((body.triageSave === true || body.triageEdit === true) && !['TRIAGE', ...RECORD_MANAGEMENT].includes(sessionRole(session))) {
      return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'គណនីនេះមិនអាចរក្សាទុកទ្រីយ៉ាបានទេ។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    if (body.transferToTriage === true && !['REGISTRATION', ...RECORD_MANAGEMENT].includes(sessionRole(session))) {
      return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'គណនីនេះមិនអាចបញ្ជូនពីការចុះឈ្មោះបានទេ។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    let editAccess = null;
    if (existingPatient) {
      try {
        editAccess = assertEncounterWrite(database, session, existingPatient, body.editorScope || undefined);
      } catch (error) {
        return sendJson(response, error.status || 403, { error: error.message }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }
    }
    if (body.editOnly === true && existingPatient && body.editorScope === 'triage' &&
      (submittedSummary.departmentCode !== existingPatient.department_code || submittedSummary.serviceMode !== existingPatient.service_mode)) {
      return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'ការប្តូរផ្នែកត្រូវប្រើសកម្មភាពបញ្ជូន។ រក្សាទុកនេះកែតែព័ត៌មានទ្រីយ៉ា។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    if (existingPatient && activeRoleBeforeSave === 'DOCTOR' && existingPatient.service_mode === 'opd') {
      const workflowStage = String(existingData['system:workflowStage'] || '');
      const isOpdClinicalSave = body.editorScope === 'medical' || body.editorScope === 'opd' || body.submitOpdPrescription === true || body.markMedicalComplete === true || Object.keys(submittedData).some(key => key.startsWith('scope:medical:') || key.startsWith('system:opdMedications')) || submittedData['system:medicalRecordCompleted'] === true;
      if (isOpdClinicalSave && (!['DOCTOR_READY', 'IN_CONSULTATION'].includes(workflowStage) || !opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed(database, existingPatient))) {
        return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'The OPD consultation fee must be confirmed by Cashier before the patient can meet the doctor.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }
    }
    const isTriageSaveOrEdit = body.triageSave === true || body.triageEdit === true || body.editorScope === 'triage';
    if (activeRoleBeforeSave === 'TRIAGE' && existingPatient && existingData['system:triageStatus'] === 'COMPLETED' && !isTriageSaveOrEdit) {
      return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'Triage is already completed for this patient. Use the patient record to view the completed triage.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }

    if (registrationSave) {
      const source = visitSourceKey ? database.findPatient(visitSourceKey) : null;
      if (existingData['system:previousPatientKey']) {
        if (String(summary.healthId || '').trim() !== String(existingPatient.health_id || '').trim()) return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'A returning visit must keep the existing Patient ID.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        if (caseTypeFromData(submittedData) === 'new') return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'An existing patient must keep Old Case for this visit.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }
      if (source && String(summary.healthId || '').trim() !== String(source.health_id || '').trim()) return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'A returning visit must keep the existing Patient ID.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      if (source && caseTypeFromData(submittedData) !== 'old') return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'An existing patient must use Old Case for a new visit.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      const knownId = summary.healthId && database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT patient_key FROM patient_records WHERE health_id=? COLLATE NOCASE LIMIT 1').get(summary.healthId);
      if (!existingPatient && knownId && caseTypeFromData(submittedData) === 'new') return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'This Patient ID already exists. Add a new Old Case record from the Patient ID in the patient list.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      if (body.transferToTriage && existingData['system:triageStatus'] === 'COMPLETED') return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'Start a new visit from Patient ID; do not overwrite a completed visit.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      const selectedCaseType = caseTypeFromData(submittedData) || String(existingData['system:caseType'] || '').toLowerCase();
      if ((activeRoleBeforeSave === 'REGISTRATION' || body.editorScope === 'registration' || body.transferToTriage) && !['new', 'old'].includes(selectedCaseType)) return sendJson(response, 400, { error: 'Select New Case or Old Case during registration before continuing.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
    if (activeRoleBeforeSave === 'TRIAGE' && !caseTypeFromData(existingData) && !['new', 'old'].includes(String(existingData['system:caseType'] || '').toLowerCase())) {
      return sendJson(response, 400, { error: 'Registration is incomplete. Select New Case or Old Case in Registration first.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }

    const activeClinicalRole = sessionRole(session) === 'CHIEF_WARD' ? 'DOCTOR' : sessionRole(session);
    const editorScope = RECORD_MANAGEMENT.includes(activeClinicalRole) && ['registration', 'triage', 'medical', 'nursing'].includes(body.editorScope)
      ? body.editorScope : '';
    const triageSave = !(body.editOnly === true && existingData['system:triageStatus'] === 'COMPLETED') && (body.triageSave === true || activeClinicalRole === 'TRIAGE' || editorScope === 'triage');
    // v42.6: the midwife writes the OPD forms of the maternal services (PNC, family planning, cervical screening).
    const maternalMidwife = activeRoleBeforeSave === 'MIDWIFE' && Boolean(existingPatient) && isMaternalService(existingPatient);
    const effectiveRoleForMerge = registrationSave ? 'REGISTRATION' : (activeRoleBeforeSave === 'TRIAGE' || triageSave || editorScope === 'triage' ? 'TRIAGE' : RECORD_MANAGEMENT.includes(activeRoleBeforeSave) ? (body.editorScope === 'nursing' ? 'NURSE' : 'DOCTOR') : maternalMidwife ? 'DOCTOR' : activeRoleBeforeSave);
    summary = mergePatientSummaryForRole(existingPatient, submittedSummary, effectiveRoleForMerge);
    let data = mergeRecordDataForRole(existingData, submittedData, effectiveRoleForMerge, Boolean(existingPatient));
    if (body.registrationOnly === true) data = { ...existingData, ...Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(submittedData).filter(([key]) => key.startsWith('scope:registration:'))) };
    if (body.sharedTriage !== undefined) {
      if (!existingPatient || existingPatient.service_mode !== 'opd' || !['DOCTOR', ...RECORD_MANAGEMENT].includes(activeClinicalRole) || body.editorScope !== 'medical' || body.clinicalSubTab !== 'opd') {
        return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Shared clinical fields require OPD doctor edit access.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }
      if (body.expectedVersion !== existingPatient.version) return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'Reload the current encounter before editing shared fields.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      try { data = mergeSharedEncounterFields(data, body.sharedTriage); }
      catch (error) { return sendJson(response, 400, { error: error.message }, { secure: config.cookieSecure }); }
    }
    // NPCA identity is taken only from a recent server-side beneficiary search.
    for (const key of Object.keys(data)) if (key.startsWith('system:npca')) { delete data[key]; if (key in existingData) data[key]=existingData[key]; }
    const npcaSelectionId = registrationSave ? submittedData['scope:registration:id:npca_selection'] : '';
    let npcaPerson = null, npcaPayment = '';
    if (npcaSelectionId) {
      npcaPerson = selectionFor(database,npcaSelectionId,session.user_id);
      npcaPayment = beneficiaryPayment(npcaPerson);
      if (!npcaPayment) return sendJson(response,400,{error:'សូមបញ្ជាក់ប្រភេទបណ្ណ និងគម្រោងទូទាត់ជាមួយ NPCA ជាមុន។'},{secure:config.cookieSecure});
      const surname=String(summary.lastName||'').trim(), name=String(summary.firstName||'').trim();
      if (surname!==String(npcaPerson.surname||'').trim() || name!==String(npcaPerson.name||'').trim() || summary.dateOfBirth!==npcaPerson.birthdate || mapCode('sex',summary.sex)!==mapCode('sex',npcaPerson.sex)) return sendJson(response,400,{error:'អត្តសញ្ញាណអ្នកជំងឺមិនត្រូវគ្នានឹងសមាជិកដែលបានជ្រើសរើសទេ។'},{secure:config.cookieSecure});
      data['system:npcaSpid']=npcaPerson.spid;data['system:npcaPatientCode']=npcaPerson.patient_code||'';
      data['system:npcaCards']=JSON.stringify(npcaPerson.benefit_cards||[]);data['system:npcaPaymentType']=npcaPayment;
    }
    delete data['scope:registration:id:npca_selection'];

    if (registrationSave) {
      data['system:caseType'] = caseTypeFromData(data);
      const sameArrivalDay = existingPatient && existingPatient.entry_date === summary.entryDate;
      const existingQueueNumber = sameArrivalDay ? registrationQueueNumberFromData(existingData) : '';
      const queueNumber = existingQueueNumber || nextRegistrationQueueNumber(database, patientKey, summary.entryDate);
      ensureUniqueRegistrationQueueNumber(database, patientKey, summary.entryDate, queueNumber);
      data['system:registrationQueueNumber'] = queueNumber;
    }
    const savedAt = Date.now();
    if (activeRoleBeforeSave === 'DOCTOR' && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(submittedData, 'system:dailyMonitoringRecords')) {
      let doctorDaily = [];
      try {
        const rawDaily = submittedData['system:dailyMonitoringRecords'];
        const parsedDaily = typeof rawDaily === 'string' ? JSON.parse(rawDaily) : rawDaily;
        if (Array.isArray(parsedDaily)) doctorDaily = parsedDaily;
      } catch { /* Malformed follow-ups cannot replace the doctor's report source. */ }
      let previousDaily = [];
      try { previousDaily=typeof existingData['system:dailyMonitoringRecords']==='string'?JSON.parse(existingData['system:dailyMonitoringRecords']):existingData['system:dailyMonitoringRecords']; } catch {}
      const priorNotes = Array.isArray(previousDaily) ? previousDaily : [];
      const clinicalContent = note => { const { npcaSignature, ...content } = note || {}; return JSON.stringify(content); };
      const npcaSnapshot=signatureSnapshot(database,session,savedAt);
      const npcaAuthor={userId:npcaSnapshot.userId,signerName:npcaSnapshot.signerName,displayName:npcaSnapshot.displayName,role:npcaSnapshot.role,signedAt:npcaSnapshot.signedAt};
      doctorDaily = doctorDaily.map(note => {
        if(!note||typeof note!=='object'||Array.isArray(note))return note;
        const previous=priorNotes.find(old=>old&&String(old.id||old.dtVal)===String(note.id||note.dtVal));
        const { npcaSignature, ...content }=note;
        return {...content,...(previous&&clinicalContent(previous)===clinicalContent(note)
          ? previous.npcaSignature ? {npcaSignature:previous.npcaSignature}:{}
          : {npcaSignature:npcaAuthor})};
      });
      data['system:dailyMonitoringRecords'] = JSON.stringify(doctorDaily);
      data['system:doctorDailyMedicineOrders'] = JSON.stringify(doctorDaily.slice(0,500));
    }
    if (existingPatient?.service_mode === 'opd') {
      const latestRx = database.sqlite.prepare(`SELECT dispensed_at FROM pharmacy_prescriptions
        WHERE patient_key=? ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT 1`).get(patientKey);
      if (latestRx?.dispensed_at) {
        let before = existingData['system:opdMedications'] || '[]';
        let after = data['system:opdMedications'] || '[]';
        try { before = JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(before)); after = JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(after)); } catch {}
        if (body.submitOpdPrescription === true || before !== after) {
          return sendJson(response, 409, { error: 'ឱសថស្ថានបានបញ្ជាក់ថាអ្នកជំងឺបានទទួលឱសថរួចហើយ។ មិនអាចកែវេជ្ជបញ្ជានេះបានទេ។' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
      }
    }
    const admissionKeys = [
      'system:admissionStatus', 'system:careType',
      'system:currentDepartmentCode', 'system:currentDepartmentNameKh',
      'system:currentDepartmentNameEn', 'system:currentWardCode', 'system:currentRoomId',
      'system:currentRoomNameKh', 'system:currentRoomNameEn',
      'system:currentBedId', 'system:currentBedLabel', 'system:discharge', 'system:opdDischarge',
    ];
    admissionKeys.forEach(key => {
      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(existingData, key)) data[key] = existingData[key];
      else delete data[key];
    });
    const existingDischarge = existingData['system:discharge'];
    const hasFormalDischarge = existingData['system:admissionStatus'] === 'discharged' &&
      existingDischarge && typeof existingDischarge === 'object';
    const hasOpdDischarge = existingPatient?.service_mode === 'opd' && Boolean(existingData['system:opdDischarge']?.dispensedAt);
    summary.dischargeDate = hasFormalDischarge || hasOpdDischarge ? existingPatient.discharge_date : '';

    if (registrationSave) {
      if (visitSourceKey) data['system:previousPatientKey'] = visitSourceKey;
      if (!existingPatient) {
        summary.departmentCode = '';
        summary.departmentNameKh = '';
        summary.departmentNameEn = '';
        summary.serviceMode = '';
      }
      if (!existingData['system:triageStatus'] || existingData['system:triageStatus'] === 'REGISTERED' && body.transferToTriage === true) {
        data['system:triageStatus'] = body.registrationOnly === true ? 'REGISTERED' : 'WAITING';
        data['system:workflowStage'] = body.registrationOnly === true ? 'REGISTRATION_SAVED' : 'TRIAGE_WAITING';
        data['system:registeredAt'] = existingData['system:registeredAt'] || savedAt;
      }
    } else if (triageSave) {
      if (!summary.departmentCode || !summary.serviceMode) badRequest('Select a receiving ward/service before completing triage.');
      // v42.6: the official ANC record imports every Triage value; none may be blank.
      if (String(summary.departmentCode).toLowerCase() === 'anc') {
        const missing = ['p2_weight', 'p2_height', 'p2_bp_sys', 'p2_bp_dia', 'p2_pulse', 'p2_temp', 'p2_spo2']
          .filter(key => !String(data[`scope:triage:id:${key}`] ?? '').trim());
        if (missing.length) return sendJson(response, 400, { error: 'សូមបំពេញសញ្ញាជីវិតទាំងអស់ មុនបញ្ជូនទៅពិនិត្យផ្ទៃពោះ។', missing }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }
      data['system:signature:triage'] = JSON.stringify(signatureSnapshot(database, session, savedAt));
      data['system:triageStatus'] = 'COMPLETED';
      const paraclinicRequested = submittedData['scope:triage:id:triage_paraclinic'] === true || submittedData['scope:triage:id:triage_paraclinic'] === 'true';
      data['system:triageParaclinic'] = paraclinicRequested;
      const wasTriageTransferred = Boolean(
        existingPatient && (
          existingData['system:triageStatus'] === 'COMPLETED' ||
          existingData['system:triageTransferred'] ||
          existingData['system:triageCompletedAt'] ||
          existingData['system:triageSaved']
        )
      );
      if (!wasTriageTransferred) {
        data['system:workflowStage'] = summary.serviceMode === 'ipd' ? 'WARD_WAITING'
          : paraclinicRequested ? 'OPD_PARACLINIC_ORDERING' : 'CASHIER_PENDING_OPD';
        data['system:triageCompletedAt'] = savedAt;
        data['system:triageReferredWardCode'] = summary.departmentCode;
        if (summary.serviceMode === 'opd') {
          data['system:opdEncounterAt'] = savedAt;
          if (paraclinicRequested) data['system:opdPreConsultParaclinicAt'] = savedAt;
          else delete data['system:opdPreConsultParaclinicAt'];
          delete data['system:opdConsultedAt'];
          delete data['system:opdConsultationPaidAt'];
          delete data['system:opdConsultationInvoiceId'];
        }
      } else {
        if (existingData['system:workflowStage']) data['system:workflowStage'] = existingData['system:workflowStage'];
        if (existingData['system:triageCompletedAt']) data['system:triageCompletedAt'] = existingData['system:triageCompletedAt'];
        data['system:triageUpdatedAt'] = savedAt;
      }
    } else if (activeClinicalRole === 'DOCTOR' || editorScope === 'medical' || maternalMidwife) {
      const doctorSig = JSON.stringify(signatureSnapshot(database, session, savedAt));
      data['system:signature:medical'] = doctorSig;
      const subTab = String(body.clinicalSubTab || '').toLowerCase();
      const svcMode = String(summary.serviceMode || existingPatient?.service_mode || '').toLowerCase();
      if (subTab === 'opd' || svcMode === 'opd') {
        data['system:signature:opdDoctor'] = doctorSig;
      } else if (subTab === 'ipd' || svcMode === 'ipd') {
        data['system:signature:ipdDoctor'] = doctorSig;
      } else {
        if (svcMode === 'opd') data['system:signature:opdDoctor'] = doctorSig;
        else data['system:signature:ipdDoctor'] = doctorSig;
      }
      if (activeClinicalRole === 'DOCTOR' && svcMode === 'opd') {
        data['system:opdConsultedAt'] = data['system:opdConsultedAt'] || savedAt;
      }
